1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field

    14→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan import stale_policy as stale_policy_mod
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import promoted_insertion_index

    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (

    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel

    24→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    25→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    26→
    27→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    28→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    29→    "triage::observe",
    30→    "triage::reflect",
    31→    "triage::organize",
    32→    "triage::enrich",
    33→    "triage::sense-check",
    34→    "triage::commit",
    35→)
    36→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    37→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    38→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    39→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    40→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"

    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→# Result dataclasses

    48→
    49→@dataclass
    50→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    51→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    52→
    53→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    54→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→
    56→    @property
    57→    def changes(self) -> int:
    58→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)

    63→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    64→
    65→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    66→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    67→
    68→    @property
    69→    def changes(self) -> int:
    70→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)

    72→
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→# ID helpers
    75→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    76→
    77→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    78→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    79→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    80→        state,
    81→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    82→    )
    83→
    84→
    85→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    86→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    87→
    88→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    89→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    90→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    91→    """
    92→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    93→        state,
    94→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    95→    )
    96→
    97→
    98→def current_under_target_ids(
    99→    state: StateModel,
   100→    *,
   101→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   102→) -> set[str]:
   103→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   104→
   105→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   106→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   107→    """
   108→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   109→        state,
   110→        target_strict=target_strict,
   111→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   112→    )
   113→
   114→
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→# Helpers for sync_stale_dimensions / sync_unscored_dimensions
   117→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118→
   119→def _has_objective_backlog(
   120→    state: StateModel,
   121→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   122→) -> bool:
   123→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   124→    if policy is not None:
   125→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   126→    return any(
   127→        f.get("status") == "open"
   128→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   129→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   130→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   131→    )
   132→
   133→
   134→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   135→    order: list[str],
   136→    *,
   137→    keep_ids: set[str],
   138→    pruned: list[str],
   139→) -> None:
   140→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   141→    to_remove = [
   142→        fid for fid in order
   143→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   144→        and fid not in keep_ids
   145→    ]
   146→    for fid in to_remove:
   147→        order.remove(fid)
   148→        pruned.append(fid)
   149→
   150→
   151→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   152→    order: list[str],
   153→    plan: PlanModel,
   154→    *,
   155→    inject_ids: set[str],
   156→    to_front: bool,
   157→    injected: list[str],
   158→) -> None:
   159→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   160→
   161→    When *to_front* is True, inserts after promoted items (front of queue).
   162→    Otherwise appends to the back.
   163→    """
   164→    existing = set(order)
   165→    if to_front:
   166→        insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   167→        for sid in reversed(sorted(inject_ids)):
   168→            if sid not in existing:
   169→                order.insert(insert_at, sid)
   170→                injected.append(sid)
   171→    else:
   172→        for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   173→            if sid not in existing:
   174→                order.append(sid)
   175→                injected.append(sid)
   176→
   177→
   178→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   179→# Helpers for sync_triage_needed
   180→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   181→
   182→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
   183→    state: StateModel,
   184→    meta: dict,
   185→) -> set[str]:
   186→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
   187→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   188→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids
   189→
   190→
   191→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   192→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   193→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   194→        while sid in order:
   195→            order.remove(sid)
   196→
   197→
   198→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   199→    order: list[str],
   200→    plan: PlanModel,
   201→    confirmed: set[str],
   202→) -> bool:
   203→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   204→
   205→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   206→    """
   207→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   208→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
   209→    existing = set(order)
   210→    injected_count = 0
   211→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   212→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   213→            order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   214→            injected_count += 1
   215→    return injected_count > 0
   216→
   217→
   218→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   219→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   220→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   221→
   222→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   223→    plan: PlanModel,
   224→    state: StateModel,
   225→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   226→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   227→
   228→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   229→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   230→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   231→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   232→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   233→    """
   234→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   235→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   236→
   237→    # Mid-cycle: don't inject unscored dimensions — they'll surface at cycle end.
   238→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   239→        return result
   240→
   241→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   242→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   243→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   244→
   245→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   246→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   247→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   248→
   249→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   250→    _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=unscored_ids, to_front=True, injected=result.injected)
   251→
   252→    return result
   253→
   254→
   255→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   256→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   257→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   258→
   259→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   260→    plan: PlanModel,
   261→    state: StateModel,
   262→    *,
   263→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   264→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   265→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   266→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   267→
   268→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   269→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   270→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   271→    2. Inject stale and under-target dimension IDs when either:
   272→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle: append to back), OR
   273→       b. A cycle just completed (post-cycle: insert at front so subjective
   274→          review takes priority over new objective issues).
   275→    """
   276→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   277→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   278→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   279→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   280→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   281→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   282→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   283→
   284→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   285→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   286→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   287→
   288→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   289→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   290→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   291→
   292→    if not should_inject:
   293→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   294→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   295→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   296→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   297→        to_evict = [
   298→            fid for fid in order
   299→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   300→            and fid in injectable_ids
   301→        ]
   302→        for fid in to_evict:
   303→            order.remove(fid)
   304→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   305→
   306→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   307→        to_front = cycle_just_completed and has_real_items
   308→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=injectable_ids, to_front=to_front, injected=result.injected)
   309→
   310→    return result
   311→
   312→
   313→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   314→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   315→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   316→
   317→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   318→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   319→
   320→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   321→    """
   322→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   323→
   324→
   325→@dataclass
   326→class TriageSyncResult:
   327→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   328→
   329→    injected: bool = False
   330→    pruned: bool = False
   331→
   332→    @property
   333→    def changes(self) -> int:
   334→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   335→
   336→
   337→def sync_triage_needed(
   338→    plan: PlanModel,
   339→    state: StateModel,
   340→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   341→    """Inject triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
   342→
   343→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   344→
   345→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   346→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   347→
   348→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   349→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   350→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   351→    """
   352→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   353→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   354→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   355→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   356→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   357→
   358→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   359→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   360→
   361→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   362→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   363→
   364→    if already_present:
   365→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   366→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   367→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   368→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   369→        # user-started triage session.
   370→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   371→            new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   372→
   373→            if not new_since_triage:
   374→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   375→                _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   376→                if current_hash:
   377→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   378→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   379→                result.pruned = True
   380→        return result
   381→
   382→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   383→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   384→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   385→        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   386→
   387→        if new_since_triage:
   388→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   389→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, plan, confirmed)
   390→        else:
   391→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   392→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   393→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   394→
   395→    return result
   396→
   397→
   398→@dataclass
   399→class ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   400→    """What changed during a score-checkpoint sync."""
   401→
   402→    injected: bool = False
   403→
   404→    @property
   405→    def changes(self) -> int:
   406→        return int(self.injected)
   407→
   408→
   409→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   410→    plan: PlanModel,
   411→    state: StateModel,
   412→    *,
   413→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   414→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   415→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   416→
   417→    Injects when:
   418→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   419→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   420→
   421→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan
   422→    so the user sees their updated strict score right after reviews finish.
   423→    """
   424→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   425→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   426→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   427→
   428→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   429→        return result
   430→
   431→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   432→    if policy is not None:
   433→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   434→            return result
   435→    else:
   436→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   437→        if unscored:
   438→            return result
   439→
   440→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   441→    insert_at = 0
   442→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   443→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   444→            insert_at = i + 1
   445→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   446→    result.injected = True
   447→    return result
   448→
   449→
   450→@dataclass
   451→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   452→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   453→
   454→    injected: bool = False
   455→
   456→    @property
   457→    def changes(self) -> int:
   458→        return int(self.injected)
   459→
   460→
   461→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   462→    plan: PlanModel,
   463→    state: StateModel,
   464→    *,
   465→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   466→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   467→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   468→
   469→    Only injects when:
   470→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   471→    - At least one objective issue exists
   472→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   473→    - No triage stages are pending
   474→    """
   475→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   476→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   477→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   478→
   479→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   480→        return result
   481→
   482→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   483→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   484→        return result
   485→
   486→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   487→    if policy is not None:
   488→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   489→            return result
   490→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   491→    else:
   492→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   493→        if unscored:
   494→            return result
   495→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   496→        has_objective = any(
   497→            f.get("status") == "open"
   498→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   499→            for f in issues.values()
   500→        )
   501→    if not has_objective:
   502→        return result
   503→
   504→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items, at the end of the
   505→    # synthetic block (so create-plan comes after score-checkpoint).
   506→    insert_at = 0
   507→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   508→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   509→            insert_at = i + 1
   510→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   511→    result.injected = True
   512→    return result
   513→
   514→
   515→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   516→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   517→
   518→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   519→    """
   520→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   521→
   522→
   523→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   524→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   525→
   526→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   527→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   528→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   529→    resolved since then.
   530→
   531→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   532→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   533→    through the plan.
   534→    """
   535→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   536→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state)
   537→
   538→
   539→@dataclass
   540→class ImportScoresSyncResult:
   541→    """What changed during an import-scores sync."""
   542→
   543→    injected: bool = False
   544→
   545→    @property
   546→    def changes(self) -> int:
   547→        return int(self.injected)
   548→
   549→
   550→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   551→    plan: PlanModel,
   552→    state: StateModel,
   553→    *,
   554→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   555→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   556→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   557→
   558→    Only injects when:
   559→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   560→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   561→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   562→
   563→    Positioned after score-checkpoint, before create-plan.
   564→    """
   565→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   566→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   567→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   568→
   569→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   570→        return result
   571→
   572→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   573→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   574→        return result
   575→
   576→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items
   577→    insert_at = 0
   578→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   579→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   580→            insert_at = i + 1
   581→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   582→    result.injected = True
   583→    return result
   584→
   585→
   586→@dataclass
   587→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   588→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   589→
   590→    injected: bool = False
   591→
   592→    @property
   593→    def changes(self) -> int:
   594→        return int(self.injected)
   595→
   596→
   597→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   598→    plan: PlanModel,
   599→    state: StateModel,
   600→    *,
   601→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   602→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   603→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   604→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   605→
   606→    Injects when either:
   607→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   608→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   609→
   610→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   611→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan.
   612→    """
   613→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   614→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   615→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   616→
   617→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   618→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   619→        return result
   620→
   621→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   622→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   623→
   624→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   625→    if not should_inject:
   626→        if policy is not None:
   627→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   628→        else:
   629→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   630→
   631→    if not should_inject:
   632→        return result
   633→
   634→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   635→    insert_at = 0
   636→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   637→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   638→            insert_at = i + 1
   639→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   640→    result.injected = True
   641→    return result
   642→
   643→
   644→__all__ = [
   645→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   646→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   647→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   648→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   649→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   650→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   651→    "WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID",
   652→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   653→    "WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID",
   654→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
   655→    "WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID",
   656→    "CommunicateScoreSyncResult",
   657→    "CreatePlanSyncResult",
   658→    "ImportScoresSyncResult",
   659→    "ScoreCheckpointSyncResult",
   660→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   661→    "TriageSyncResult",
   662→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   663→    "current_under_target_ids",
   664→    "current_unscored_ids",
   665→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   666→    "is_triage_stale",
   667→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",

   672→    "sync_stale_dimensions",

1→"""Pure policy helpers for stale/unscored subjective planning decisions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import all_subjective_entries
    11→
    12→_REVIEW_DETECTORS = ("review", "concerns")
    13→
    14→
    15→def open_review_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    16→    """Return IDs of open review/concerns issues from state."""
    17→    return {
    18→        fid
    19→        for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    20→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def current_stale_ids(
    25→    state: StateModel,
    26→    *,
    27→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    28→) -> set[str]:
    29→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    30→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    31→    if not dim_scores:
    32→        return set()
    33→
    34→    stale: set[str] = set()
    35→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    36→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    37→            continue
    38→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    39→        if dim_key:
    40→            stale.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    41→    return stale
    42→
    43→
    44→def current_unscored_ids(
    45→    state: StateModel,
    46→    *,
    47→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    48→) -> set[str]:
    49→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored."""
    50→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    51→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
    52→        unscored: set[str] = set()
    53→        for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    54→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    55→                continue
    56→            if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    57→                continue
    58→            if dim_key:
    59→                unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    60→        return unscored
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    unscored = set()
    67→    for data in dim_scores.values():
    68→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
    69→            continue
    70→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    71→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
    72→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
    73→            continue
    74→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
    75→            continue
    76→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
    77→        if dim_key:
    78→            unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    79→    return unscored
    80→
    81→
    82→def current_under_target_ids(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    86→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    87→) -> set[str]:
    88→    """Return under-target subjective IDs that are neither stale nor unscored."""
    89→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    90→    if not dim_scores:
    91→        return set()
    92→
    93→    stale_ids = current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    94→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    95→
    96→    under_target: set[str] = set()
    97→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    98→        if entry.get("placeholder") or entry.get("stale"):
    99→            continue
   100→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   101→        if strict_val >= target_strict:
   102→            continue
   103→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
   104→        if not dim_key:
   105→            continue
   106→        item_id = f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}"
   107→        if item_id not in stale_ids and item_id not in unscored_ids:
   108→            under_target.add(item_id)
   109→    return under_target
   110→
   111→
   112→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   113→    """Hash open review/concerns issue IDs to detect triage-relevant changes."""
   114→    review_ids = sorted(open_review_ids(state))
   115→    if not review_ids:
   116→        return ""
   117→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   118→
   119→
   120→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   121→    """Return open review/concerns IDs that appeared since the last triage."""
   122→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   123→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   124→    return open_review_ids(state) - triaged if triaged else set()
   125→
   126→
   127→def is_triage_stale(
   128→    plan: dict,
   129→    state: StateModel,
   130→) -> bool:
   131→    """Return True when genuinely new review issues appeared since last triage.
   132→
   133→    In-progress triage (confirmed stages + stage IDs in queue) is NOT
   134→    considered stale — the lifecycle filter in the work queue already
   135→    forces triage stages to the front.
   136→    """
   137→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   138→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   139→    return bool(open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids)
   140→
   141→
   142→__all__ = [
   143→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   144→    "current_stale_ids",
   145→    "current_under_target_ids",
   146→    "current_unscored_ids",
   147→    "is_triage_stale",
   148→    "open_review_ids",
   149→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   150→]
   151→
```

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle issue churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import get_issue_note
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_meta import append_log_entry
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_skip import resurface_stale_skips
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    SupersededEntry,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    19→    SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES,
    20→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    21→    sync_triage_needed,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    24→
    25→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass
    29→class ReconcileResult:
    30→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    31→
    32→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    34→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    35→    clusters_completed: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    36→    changes: int = 0
    37→
    38→
    39→def _find_candidates(
    40→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    41→) -> list[str]:
    42→    """Find open issues that could be remaps for a disappeared issue."""
    43→    candidates: list[str] = []
    44→    for fid, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items():
    45→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    46→            continue
    47→        if issue.get("detector") == detector and issue.get("file") == file:
    48→            candidates.append(fid)
    49→    return candidates
    50→
    51→
    52→def _is_issue_alive(state: StateModel, issue_id: str) -> bool:
    53→    """Return True if the issue exists and is open."""
    54→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    55→    if issue is None:
    56→        return False
    57→    return issue.get("status") == "open"
    58→
    59→
    60→def _supersede_id(
    61→    plan: PlanModel,
    62→    state: StateModel,
    63→    issue_id: str,
    64→    now: str,
    65→) -> bool:
    66→    """Move a disappeared issue to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    67→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    68→    detector = ""
    69→    file = ""
    70→    summary = ""
    71→    if issue:
    72→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    73→        file = issue.get("file", "")
    74→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    75→
    76→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    77→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    78→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != issue_id]
    79→
    80→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    81→        "original_id": issue_id,
    82→        "original_detector": detector,
    83→        "original_file": file,
    84→        "original_summary": summary,
    85→        "status": "superseded",
    86→        "superseded_at": now,
    87→        "remapped_to": None,
    88→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    89→    }
    90→
    91→    # Preserve any existing override note
    92→    override_note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    93→    if override_note:
    94→        entry["note"] = override_note
    95→
    96→    plan["superseded"][issue_id] = entry
    97→
    98→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster issue_ids
    99→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   100→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   101→    if issue_id in order:
   102→        order.remove(issue_id)
   103→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   104→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, {issue_id})
   105→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   106→        ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   107→        if issue_id in ids:
   108→            ids.remove(issue_id)
   109→
   110→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
   111→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(issue_id)
   112→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   113→        override["cluster"] = None
   114→        override["updated_at"] = now
   115→
   116→    return True
   117→
   118→
   119→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   120→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   121→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   122→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   123→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   124→
   125→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   126→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   127→        try:
   128→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   129→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   130→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   131→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   132→                to_prune.append(fid)
   133→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   134→            to_prune.append(fid)
   135→
   136→    for fid in to_prune:
   137→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   138→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   139→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   140→
   141→    return to_prune
   142→
   143→
   144→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   145→    plan: PlanModel,
   146→    state: StateModel,
   147→) -> ReconcileResult:
   148→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   149→
   150→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   151→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   152→    """
   153→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   154→    result = ReconcileResult()
   155→    now = utc_now()
   156→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   157→
   158→    # Collect all issue IDs referenced by the plan
   159→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   160→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   161→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   162→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   163→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   164→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   165→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   166→
   167→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   168→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   169→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   170→    referenced_ids = {
   171→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   172→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   173→    }
   174→
   175→    # Snapshot non-epic cluster sizes before superseding so we can detect
   176→    # clusters that become empty (all issues resolved by scan).
   177→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   178→    pre_sizes: dict[str, int] = {
   179→        name: len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   180→        for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   181→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   182→    }
   183→
   184→    # Check each referenced ID
   185→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   186→        if not _is_issue_alive(state, fid):
   187→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   188→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   189→                result.changes += 1
   190→
   191→    # Detect manual clusters that just became empty (all issues auto-resolved).
   192→    # Log cluster_done so the plan tracks completion the same way as user resolves.
   193→    for name, prev_size in pre_sizes.items():
   194→        if prev_size == 0:
   195→            continue  # was already empty
   196→        cluster = clusters.get(name)
   197→        if cluster is None:
   198→            continue
   199→        if len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])) == 0:
   200→            result.clusters_completed.append(name)
   201→            append_log_entry(
   202→                plan,
   203→                "cluster_done",
   204→                issue_ids=[],
   205→                cluster_name=name,
   206→                actor="system",
   207→                detail={"reason": "all issues resolved by scan"},
   208→            )
   209→            result.changes += 1
   210→
   211→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead issues, delete empty epics
   212→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   213→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   214→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   215→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   216→            continue
   217→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   218→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if _is_issue_alive(state, fid)]
   219→        if alive_ids != issue_ids:
   220→            cluster["issue_ids"] = alive_ids
   221→            result.changes += 1
   222→        if not alive_ids:
   223→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   224→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   225→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   226→        result.changes += 1
   227→
   228→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   229→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   230→
   231→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   232→    if resurfaced:
   233→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   234→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   235→
   236→    # Prune old superseded entries
   237→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   238→    result.pruned = pruned
   239→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   240→
   241→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   242→    if result.changes > 0:
   243→        append_log_entry(
   244→            plan,
   245→            "reconcile",
   246→            issue_ids=result.superseded,
   247→            actor="system",
   248→            detail={
   249→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   250→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   251→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   252→                "clusters_completed_count": len(result.clusters_completed),
   253→            },
   254→        )
   255→
   256→    return result
   257→
   258→
   259→@dataclass
   260→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
   261→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
   262→
   263→    new_ids: set[str]
   264→    added_to_queue: list[str]
   265→    triage_injected: bool
   266→
   267→
   268→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   269→    plan: PlanModel,
   270→    state: StateModel,
   271→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   272→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   273→
   274→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order and injects triage stages
   275→    if needed. Returns None when there are no new issues to sync.
   276→    """
   277→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   278→    new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   279→    if not new_ids:
   280→        return None
   281→
   282→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   283→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   284→    existing = set(order)
   285→    added: list[str] = []
   286→    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
   287→        if fid not in existing:
   288→            order.append(fid)
   289→            added.append(fid)
   290→
   291→    # Inject triage stages if needed
   292→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   293→    triage_injected = bool(
   294→        triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "injected", False)
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   298→        new_ids=new_ids,
   299→        added_to_queue=added,
   300→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   301→    )
   302→
   303→
   304→__all__ = [
   305→    "ReconcileResult",
   306→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   307→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   308→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   309→]
   310→

1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions`` and ``auto_cluster``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import issue_in_scan_scope
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    16→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    17→
    18→# Detectors whose issues are NOT objective mechanical work.
    19→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    20→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    21→})
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    25→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    26→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    27→
    28→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective issues?
    29→    objective_count: int  # how many
    30→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    31→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    32→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    33→
    34→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    35→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    36→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    37→            return True  # unconditional
    38→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    39→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    40→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    41→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    42→        return False
    43→
    44→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    45→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    46→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    47→            return False  # never evict unscored
    48→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    49→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    50→        return False
    51→
    52→    @property
    53→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    54→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    55→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    56→
    57→
    58→def _is_evidence_only(issue: dict) -> bool:
    59→    """Return True if the issue is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    60→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    61→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    62→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    63→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    64→        issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    65→        if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
    66→            return True
    67→    return False
    68→
    69→
    70→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = object()
    71→
    72→
    73→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    74→    state: StateModel,
    75→    *,
    76→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    77→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    78→    plan: dict | None = None,
    79→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    80→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    81→
    82→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    83→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    84→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, issues whose IDs
    85→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    86→
    87→    Imports policy helpers from ``stale_policy`` so this module remains
    88→    side-effect free and cycle-safe.
    89→    """
    90→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_policy import (
    91→        current_stale_ids,
    92→        current_under_target_ids,
    93→        current_unscored_ids,
    94→    )
    95→
    96→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
    97→        state.get("scan_path")
    98→        if scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY
    99→        else scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   103→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   104→
   105→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective issues.
   106→    # Evidence-only issues (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   107→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   108→    # Issues outside scan_path and plan-skipped issues are also excluded
   109→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   110→    objective_count = sum(
   111→        1
   112→        for fid, f in issues.items()
   113→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   114→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   115→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   116→        and not _is_evidence_only(f)
   117→        and issue_in_scan_scope(str(f.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   118→        and fid not in skipped_ids
   119→    )
   120→
   121→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   122→    stale = current_stale_ids(state)
   123→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   124→
   125→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   126→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   127→        objective_count=objective_count,
   128→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   129→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   130→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   131→    )
   132→
   133→
   134→__all__ = [
   135→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   136→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   137→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   138→]
   139→
```

1→"""Subjective-dimension scoring helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.subjective_dimensions import (
     6→    DISPLAY_NAMES,
     7→    default_dimension_keys,
     8→)
     9→from desloppify.base.text_utils import is_numeric
    10→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS
    11→
    12→def _display_fallback(dim_name: str) -> str:
    13→    words = dim_name.replace("_", " ")
    14→    return words[0].upper() + words[1:] if words else words
    15→
    16→
    17→def _normalize_dimension_key(dim_name: object) -> str:
    18→    if not isinstance(dim_name, str):
    19→        return ""
    20→    return "_".join(dim_name.strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    21→
    22→
    23→def _primary_lang_from_issues(issues: dict) -> str | None:
    24→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    25→    for issue in issues.values():
    26→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
    27→            continue
    28→        raw_lang = issue.get("lang")
    29→        if not isinstance(raw_lang, str) or not raw_lang.strip():
    30→            continue
    31→        key = raw_lang.strip().lower()
    32→        counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    33→    if not counts:
    34→        return None
    35→    return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    36→
    37→
    38→def _dimension_display_name(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> str:
    39→    try:
    40→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    41→            dimension_display_name,  # cycle-break: subjective/core.py ↔ metadata.py
    42→        )
    43→
    44→        return str(dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    45→    except (AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    46→        return DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim_name, _display_fallback(dim_name))
    47→
    48→
    49→def _dimension_weight(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> float:
    50→    try:
    51→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    52→            dimension_weight,  # cycle-break: subjective/core.py ↔ metadata.py
    53→        )
    54→
    55→        return float(dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    56→    except (AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    57→        return 1.0
    58→
    59→
    60→def _compute_dimension_score(
    61→    assessment: dict | None, has_assessment: bool,
    62→) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    63→    """Compute (score, pass_rate, assessment_score) for a dimension."""
    64→    assessment_score = (
    65→        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(assessment.get("score", 0))))
    66→        if isinstance(assessment, dict)
    67→        else 0.0
    68→    )
    69→    integrity_penalty = (
    70→        assessment.get("integrity_penalty")
    71→        if isinstance(assessment, dict)
    72→        else None
    73→    )
    74→    reset_pending = bool(
    75→        isinstance(assessment, dict)
    76→        and (
    77→            assessment.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
    78→            or assessment.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
    79→            or assessment.get("placeholder") is True
    80→        )
    81→    )
    82→    if reset_pending:
    83→        score = 0.0
    84→        pass_rate = 0.0
    85→    elif integrity_penalty == "target_match_reset":
    86→        score = 0.0
    87→        pass_rate = 0.0
    88→    elif has_assessment:
    89→        score = assessment_score
    90→        pass_rate = score / 100.0
    91→    else:
    92→        score = 0.0
    93→        pass_rate = 0.0
    94→    return score, pass_rate, assessment_score
    95→
    96→
    97→def _extract_components(assessment: dict) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, float]]:
    98→    """Extract component names and scores from an assessment dict."""
    99→    components: list[str] = []
   100→    component_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   101→    raw_components = assessment.get("components")
   102→    if isinstance(raw_components, list):
   103→        components = [
   104→            str(item).strip()
   105→            for item in raw_components
   106→            if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
   107→        ]
   108→    raw_component_scores = assessment.get("component_scores")
   109→    if isinstance(raw_component_scores, dict):
   110→        for key, value in raw_component_scores.items():
   111→            if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
   112→                continue
   113→            if not is_numeric(value):
   114→                continue
   115→            component_scores[key.strip()] = round(
   116→                max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))),
   117→                1,
   118→            )
   119→    return components, component_scores
   120→
   121→
   122→def append_subjective_dimensions(
   123→    results: dict,
   124→    issues: dict,
   125→    assessments: dict | None,
   126→    failure_set: frozenset[str],
   127→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   128→) -> None:
   129→    """Append subjective review dimensions to results dict (mutates results).
   130→
   131→    Subjective scoring is evidence-first: open review issues for a dimension
   132→    determine pass-rate, while imported assessment scores are retained as
   133→    metadata for transparency.
   134→    """
   135→    raw_defaults = default_dimension_keys()
   136→    allowed = (
   137→        {_normalize_dimension_key(name) for name in allowed_dimensions}
   138→        if allowed_dimensions is not None
   139→        else None
   140→    )
   141→
   142→    default_dimensions = []
   143→    for raw_dim in raw_defaults:
   144→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
   145→        if not dim:
   146→            continue
   147→        if allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   148→            continue
   149→        default_dimensions.append(dim)
   150→
   151→    assessed: dict[str, dict] = {}
   152→    for raw_dim, payload in (assessments or {}).items():
   153→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
   154→        if not dim:
   155→            continue
   156→        # Real assessments (from review imports) always count regardless of
   157→        # allowed_dimensions — they were deliberately imported and should not
   158→        # be silently discarded.  However, system-generated placeholders
   159→        # (scan_reset_subjective) are not real assessments and should respect
   160→        # the allowed_dimensions scope like defaults.
   161→        is_placeholder = isinstance(payload, dict) and (
   162→            payload.get("placeholder") is True
   163→            or payload.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   164→            or payload.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   165→        )
   166→        if is_placeholder and allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   167→            continue
   168→        assessed[dim] = payload
   169→    existing_lower = {k.lower() for k in results}
   170→    lang_name = _primary_lang_from_issues(issues)
   171→
   172→    all_dims = list(default_dimensions)
   173→    for dim_name in assessed:
   174→        if dim_name not in default_dimensions:
   175→            all_dims.append(dim_name)
   176→
   177→    for dim_name in all_dims:
   178→        is_default = dim_name in default_dimensions
   179→        assessment = assessed.get(dim_name)
   180→        has_assessment = isinstance(assessment, dict)
   181→        if not is_default and not assessment:
   182→            continue
   183→
   184→        display = _dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   185→        if display.lower() in existing_lower:
   186→            display = f"{display} (subjective)"
   187→
   188→        # Count open review/concern issues for display (work queue), but
   189→        # these do NOT drive the dimension score — only assessment scores do.
   190→        issue_count = sum(
   191→            1
   192→            for issue in issues.values()
   193→            if issue.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   194→            and issue.get("status") in failure_set
   195→            and _normalize_dimension_key(
   196→                issue.get("detail", {}).get("dimension")
   197→            )
   198→            == dim_name
   199→        )
   200→
   201→        score, pass_rate, assessment_score = _compute_dimension_score(
   202→            assessment, has_assessment,
   203→        )
   204→        reset_pending = bool(
   205→            isinstance(assessment, dict)
   206→            and (
   207→                assessment.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   208→                or assessment.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   209→                or assessment.get("placeholder") is True
   210→            )
   211→        )
   212→        components: list[str] = []
   213→        component_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   214→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
   215→            components, component_scores = _extract_components(assessment)
   216→
   217→        results[display] = {
   218→            "score": round(float(score), 1),
   219→            "tier": 4,
   220→            "checks": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   221→            "failing": issue_count,
   222→            "detectors": {
   223→                "subjective_assessment": {
   224→                    "potential": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   225→                    "pass_rate": round(pass_rate, 4),
   226→                    "failing": issue_count,
   227→                    "weighted_failures": round(SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS * (1 - pass_rate), 4),
   228→                    "assessment_score": round(assessment_score, 1),
   229→                    "placeholder": reset_pending or not has_assessment,
   230→                    "dimension_key": dim_name,
   231→                    "configured_weight": round(
   232→                        _dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name), 6
   233→                    ),
   234→                    "components": components,
   235→                    **(
   236→                        {"component_scores": component_scores}
   237→                        if component_scores
   238→                        else {}
   239→                    ),
   240→                }
   241→            },
   242→        }
   243→
   244→
   245→__all__ = [
   246→    "DISPLAY_NAMES",
   247→    "append_subjective_dimensions",
   248→]
   249→

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES
     8→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     9→    scorecard_dimension_rows,

    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def all_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return ALL subjective dimension entries, bypassing the display pipeline.
    76→
    77→    Unlike ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` this reads ``dimension_scores``
    78→    directly — no elegance collapsing, no row-count limit, no placeholder
    79→    hiding.  Use this for *enumeration* (work-queue construction, stale/
    80→    under-target detection) where every individual dimension must be visible.
    81→    """
    82→    if dim_scores is None:
    83→        dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    84→    if not isinstance(dim_scores, dict) or not dim_scores:
    85→        return []
    86→
    87→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    88→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    89→
    90→    entries: list[dict] = []
    91→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():
    92→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
    93→            continue
    94→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    95→        is_subjective = (
    96→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    97→            or str(name).strip().lower() in subjective_display_names
    98→        )
    99→        if not is_subjective:
   100→            continue
   101→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   102→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   103→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
   104→        placeholder = bool(
   105→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
   106→            or (
   107→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
   108→                and data.get("failing", 0) == 0
   109→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   110→            )
   111→        )
   112→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   113→        stale = bool(data.get("stale")) or bool(
   114→            dim_key
   115→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   116→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   117→        )
   118→        entries.append(
   119→            {
   120→                "name": name,
   121→                "score": score,
   122→                "strict": strict,
   123→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   124→                "failing": int(data.get("failing", 0) or 0),
   125→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   126→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   127→                "stale": stale,
   128→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   129→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   130→            }

   134→
   135→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
   136→    state: dict,
   137→    *,

   141→
   142→    WARNING: This function routes through the scorecard *display* pipeline
   143→    (elegance collapsing, 20-row cap, placeholder hiding).  For enumeration
   144→    of all individual dimensions (work-queue, stale/under-target detection),
   145→    use ``all_subjective_entries`` instead.
   146→    """
   147→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   148→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
   149→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
   150→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
