1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — sync plan queue metadata after a scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
     7→
     8→if TYPE_CHECKING:
     9→    from desloppify.app.commands.scan.workflow import ScanRuntime
    10→
    11→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    14→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    17→    append_log_entry,
    18→    auto_cluster_issues,
    19→    load_plan,
    20→    reconcile_plan_after_scan,
    21→    save_plan,
    22→    sync_communicate_score_needed,
    23→    sync_create_plan_needed,
    24→    sync_stale_dimensions,
    25→    sync_triage_needed,
    26→    sync_unscored_dimensions,
    27→)
    28→
    29→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    30→
    31→
    32→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    33→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    34→    return bool(
    35→        plan.get("queue_order")
    36→        or plan.get("overrides")
    37→        or plan.get("clusters")
    38→        or plan.get("skipped")
    39→    )
    40→
    41→
    42→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    43→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    44→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    45→        return False
    46→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    47→    if recon.resurfaced:
    48→        print(
    49→            colorize(
    50→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    51→                "cyan",
    52→            )
    53→        )
    54→    return bool(recon.changes)
    55→
    56→
    57→def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    58→    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""
    59→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
    60→    if sync.injected:
    61→        print(
    62→            colorize(
    63→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
    64→                "cyan",
    65→            )
    66→        )
    67→    return bool(sync.changes)
    68→
    69→
    70→def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    71→    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
    72→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
    73→    if sync.pruned:
    74→        print(
    75→            colorize(
    76→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
    77→                "cyan",
    78→            )
    79→        )
    80→    if sync.injected:
    81→        print(
    82→            colorize(
    83→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
    84→                "cyan",
    85→            )
    86→        )
    87→    return bool(sync.changes)
    88→
    89→
    90→def _sync_auto_clusters(
    91→    plan: dict[str, object],
    92→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
    93→    *,
    94→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    95→    policy=None,
    96→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
    97→) -> bool:
    98→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
    99→    return bool(auto_cluster_issues(
   100→        plan, state,
   101→        target_strict=target_strict,
   102→        policy=policy,
   103→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   104→    ))
   105→
   106→
   107→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   108→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   109→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   110→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   111→        return False
   112→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   113→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   114→        return False
   115→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   116→    if scores.strict is None:
   117→        return False
   118→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   119→        "strict": scores.strict,
   120→        "overall": scores.overall,
   121→        "objective": scores.objective,
   122→        "verified": scores.verified,
   123→    }
   124→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   125→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   126→    return True
   127→
   128→
   129→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   130→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   131→) -> bool:
   132→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   133→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   134→        return False
   135→
   136→    try:
   137→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   138→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   139→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   140→            score_display_mode,
   141→        )
   142→
   143→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   144→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   145→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   146→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   147→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   148→        return False
   149→    if not queue_empty:
   150→        return False
   151→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   152→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   153→    return True
   154→
   155→
   156→def _subjective_policy_context(
   157→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   158→    plan: dict[str, object],
   159→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   160→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   161→    from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility
   162→
   163→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   164→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   165→        runtime.state,
   166→        target_strict=target_strict,
   167→        plan=plan,
   168→    )
   169→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   170→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed
   171→
   172→
   173→def _sync_unscored_and_log(
   174→    plan: dict[str, object],
   175→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   176→) -> bool:
   177→    changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   178→    if changed:
   179→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   180→    return changed
   181→
   182→
   183→def _sync_stale_and_log(
   184→    plan: dict[str, object],
   185→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   186→    *,
   187→    policy,
   188→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   189→) -> bool:
   190→    changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
   191→        plan,
   192→        state,
   193→        lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
   194→            p,
   195→            s,
   196→            policy=policy,
   197→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   198→        ),
   199→    )
   200→    if changed:
   201→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   202→    return changed
   203→
   204→
   205→def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   206→    plan: dict[str, object],
   207→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   208→    *,
   209→    target_strict: float,
   210→    policy,
   211→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   212→) -> bool:
   213→    changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   214→        plan,
   215→        state,
   216→        target_strict=target_strict,
   217→        policy=policy,
   218→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   219→    )
   220→    if changed:
   221→        append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   222→    return changed
   223→
   224→
   225→def _sync_triage_and_log(
   226→    plan: dict[str, object],
   227→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   228→) -> bool:
   229→    triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   230→    if not triage_sync.changes:
   231→        return False
   232→    if triage_sync.injected:
   233→        print(
   234→            colorize(
   235→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review issues changed since last triage.",
   236→                "cyan",
   237→            )
   238→        )
   239→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   240→    return True
   241→
   242→
   243→def _sync_communicate_score_and_log(
   244→    plan: dict[str, object],
   245→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   246→    *,
   247→    policy,
   248→) -> bool:
   249→    communicate_sync = sync_communicate_score_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   250→    if not communicate_sync.changes:
   251→        return False
   252→    append_log_entry(
   253→        plan,
   254→        "sync_communicate_score",
   255→        actor="system",
   256→        detail={"injected": True},
   257→    )
   258→    return True
   259→
   260→
   261→def _sync_create_plan_and_log(
   262→    plan: dict[str, object],
   263→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   264→    *,
   265→    policy,
   266→) -> bool:
   267→    create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   268→    if not create_plan_sync.changes:
   269→        return False
   270→    if create_plan_sync.injected:
   271→        print(
   272→            colorize(
   273→                "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   274→                "cyan",
   275→            )
   276→        )
   277→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   278→    return True
   279→
   280→
   281→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   282→    plan: dict[str, object],
   283→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   284→) -> bool:
   285→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   286→    if seeded:
   287→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   288→        return True
   289→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   290→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   291→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   292→    if cleared:
   293→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   294→    return cleared
   295→
   296→
   297→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   298→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   299→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   300→    try:
   301→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   302→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   303→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   304→        return
   305→    dirty = False
   306→
   307→    if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   308→        dirty = True
   309→
   310→    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   311→        dirty = True
   312→
   313→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   314→        runtime,
   315→        plan,
   316→    )
   317→    if _sync_stale_and_log(
   318→        plan,
   319→        runtime.state,
   320→        policy=policy,
   321→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   322→    ):
   323→        dirty = True
   324→
   325→    if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   326→        plan,
   327→        runtime.state,
   328→        target_strict=target_strict,
   329→        policy=policy,
   330→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   331→    ):
   332→        dirty = True
   333→
   334→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   335→        dirty = True
   336→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   337→        dirty = True
   338→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   339→        dirty = True
   340→    if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   341→        dirty = True
   342→
   343→    if dirty:
   344→        try:
   345→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   346→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   347→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   348→

    37→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.

    40→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    41→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    42→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    43→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),

    45→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    46→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    47→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    48→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    49→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    50→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    51→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    52→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    53→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    54→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    55→}
    56→
    57→
    58→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    59→    state: StateModel,
    60→    *,
    61→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    62→    now: str,
    63→) -> None:
    64→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical issues change.
    65→
    66→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    67→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    68→    """
    69→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    70→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    71→        return
    72→
    73→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    74→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    75→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    76→        if meta is None or not meta.marks_dims_stale:
    77→            continue
    78→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector)
    79→        if dims:
    80→            affected_dims.update(dims)
    81→            continue
    82→        # Safety fallback for newly added "marks_dims_stale" detectors that
    83→        # have not declared fine-grained dimension mappings yet.
    84→        affected_dims.update(
    85→            dim
    86→            for dim in assessments
    87→            if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
    88→        )
    89→
    90→    if not affected_dims:
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    94→        if dimension not in assessments:
    95→            continue
    96→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    97→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    98→            continue
    99→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
   100→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
   101→            continue
   102→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
   103→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_issues_changed"
   104→        payload["stale_since"] = now
   105→
   106→

     2→

    13→from desloppify.base.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,

    23→from desloppify.engine._state import _recompute_stats
    24→
    25→
    26→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    27→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    28→
    29→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    30→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    31→    """
    32→    if is_numeric(value):
    33→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    34→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    35→        raw = value.get("score")
    36→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    37→            return None
    38→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    39→    return None
    40→
    41→
    42→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    43→    state: StateModel,
    44→    *,
    45→    status: str,
    46→    resolved_issues: list[dict],
    47→    now: str,
    48→) -> None:
    49→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review issues are resolved.
    50→
    51→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    52→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    53→    for a re-review.
    54→    """
    55→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    56→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    57→        return
    58→
    59→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    60→    for issue in resolved_issues:
    61→        if issue.get("detector") != "review":
    62→            continue
    63→        dimension = str(issue.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    64→        if dimension:
    65→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    66→
    67→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    68→        if dimension not in assessments:
    69→            continue
    70→
    71→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    72→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    73→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    74→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_issue_{status}"
    75→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    76→        else:
    77→            assessments[dimension] = {
    78→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    79→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    80→                "refresh_reason": f"review_issue_{status}",
    81→                "stale_since": now,
    82→            }
    83→
    84→
    85→def match_issues(
    86→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    87→) -> list[dict]:
    88→    """Return issues matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    89→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    90→    return [
    91→        issue
    92→        for issue_id, issue in state["issues"].items()
    93→        if not issue.get("suppressed")
    94→        if (status_filter == "all" or issue["status"] == status_filter)
    95→        and _matches_pattern(issue_id, issue, pattern)
    96→    ]
    97→
    98→
    99→def resolve_issues(
   100→    state: StateModel,
   101→    pattern: str,
   102→    status: str,
   103→    note: str | None = None,
   104→    attestation: str | None = None,
   105→) -> list[str]:
   106→    """Set issue status for matches and return affected issue IDs."""
   107→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   108→    now = utc_now()
   109→    resolved: list[str] = []
   110→    resolved_issues: list[dict] = []
   111→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   112→    for issue in match_issues(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   113→        previous_status = str(issue.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   114→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   115→            continue
   116→
   117→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   118→        if status == "wontfix":
   119→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   120→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   121→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   122→                "captured_at": now,
   123→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   124→                "tier": issue.get("tier"),
   125→                "confidence": issue.get("confidence"),
   126→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(issue.get("detail", {})),
   127→            }
   128→        if status == "open":
   129→            issue["reopen_count"] = int(issue.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   130→            issue.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   131→            issue.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   132→            previous_note = issue.get("note")
   133→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   135→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   136→            reopen_attestation = {
   137→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   138→                "text": attestation or note,
   139→                "attested_at": now,
   140→                "scan_verified": False,
   141→            }
   142→            reopen_attestation["previous_status"] = previous_status
   143→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = reopen_attestation
   144→
   145→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   146→            "status": status,
   147→            "note": note,
   148→            "resolved_at": now,
   149→            "suppressed": False,
   150→            "suppressed_at": None,
```

1→"""Helper utilities for plan triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_policy import open_review_ids
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    11→    TRIAGE_IDS,
    12→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    13→    purge_ids,
    14→    review_issue_snapshot_hash,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import utc_now
    17→
    18→from .services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    19→
    20→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"]
    21→
    22→
    23→def has_triage_in_queue(plan: dict) -> bool:
    24→    """Check if any triage stage ID is in the queue."""
    25→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    26→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    27→
    28→def inject_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> None:
    29→    """Inject all triage stage IDs into the queue (fresh start)."""
    30→    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
    31→    remaining = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in TRIAGE_IDS]
    32→    order[:] = [*TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, *remaining]
    33→
    34→def purge_triage_stage(plan: dict, stage_name: str) -> None:
    35→    """Purge a single triage stage ID from the queue."""
    36→    sid = f"triage::{stage_name}"
    37→    purge_ids(plan, [sid])
    38→
    39→def cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    40→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    41→    try:
    42→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    43→    except ValueError:
    44→        return []
    45→    cleared: list[str] = []
    46→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    47→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    48→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    49→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    50→            cleared.append(later)
    51→    return cleared
    52→
    53→def print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared: list[str], stages: dict) -> None:
    54→    """Print yellow cascade-clear message with next-step guidance."""
    55→    if not cleared:
    56→        return
    57→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    58→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    59→        (s for s in _STAGE_ORDER if s in stages and not stages[s].get("confirmed_at")),
    60→        None,
    61→    )
    62→    if next_unconfirmed:
    63→        print(colorize(
    64→            f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
    65→            "dim",
    66→        ))
    67→
    68→def observe_dimension_breakdown(si) -> tuple[dict[str, int], list[str]]:
    69→    """Count issues per dimension from a TriageInput. Returns (by_dim, sorted_dim_names)."""
    70→    by_dim: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    71→    for _fid, f in si.open_issues.items():
    72→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    73→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
    74→        by_dim[dim] += 1
    75→    dim_names = sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d))
    76→    return dict(by_dim), dim_names
    77→
    78→def group_issues_into_observe_batches(
    79→    si,
    80→    max_batches: int = 5,
    81→) -> list[tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict]]]:
    82→    """Group issues by dimension into batches for parallel observe.
    83→
    84→    Returns list of (dimension_names, issues_subset) tuples.
    85→    Single batch if only one dimension exists.
    86→    """
    87→    by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(si)
    88→
    89→    if len(dim_names) <= 1:
    90→        return [(dim_names, dict(si.open_issues))]
    91→
    92→    # Distribute dimensions into balanced batches by issue count
    93→    num_batches = min(max_batches, len(dim_names))
    94→    batch_dims: list[list[str]] = [[] for _ in range(num_batches)]
    95→    batch_counts: list[int] = [0] * num_batches
    96→
    97→    # Greedy: assign each dimension (largest first) to the lightest batch
    98→    for dim in dim_names:
    99→        lightest = min(range(num_batches), key=lambda i: batch_counts[i])
   100→        batch_dims[lightest].append(dim)

   166→    budget_warning: str | None
   167→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]
   168→    hidden_total: int
   169→
   170→
   171→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   172→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   173→    config: dict[str, object],
   174→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   175→    lang: LangRun | None,
   176→) -> LangRun | None:
   177→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   178→    if not lang:
   179→        return None
   180→
   181→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   182→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   183→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   184→        lang,
   185→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   186→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   187→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   188→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,

   203→def _apply_assessment_reset(payload: dict, *, source: str, now: str) -> None:
   204→    """Apply a standard reset mutation to a single subjective assessment payload.
   205→
   206→    Sets score to 0.0, stamps assessed_at/reset_by/placeholder, and strips
   207→    any cached scoring artifacts (integrity_penalty, components,
   208→    component_scores) plus provisional-override markers.
   209→    """
   210→    payload["score"] = 0.0
   211→    payload["source"] = source
   212→    payload["assessed_at"] = now
   213→    payload["reset_by"] = source
   214→    payload["placeholder"] = True
   215→    payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   216→    payload.pop("components", None)
   217→    payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   218→    payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   219→    payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)

   238→    source = "scan_reset_subjective"
   239→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):

   241→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   242→            _apply_assessment_reset(payload, source=source, now=now)

   245→            "score": 0.0,
   246→            "source": source,
   247→            "assessed_at": now,
   248→            "reset_by": source,
   249→            "placeholder": True,

   273→def _is_mid_cycle(args: argparse.Namespace) -> bool:

   285→def prepare_scan_runtime(args: argparse.Namespace) -> ScanRuntime:
   286→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   287→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   288→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   289→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   290→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   291→    path = Path(args.path)
   292→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   293→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   294→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   295→    expired_manual_override_count = 0
   296→    if not _is_mid_cycle(args):
   297→        expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   298→            state
   299→        )
   300→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   301→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   302→            state,
   303→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   304→        )
   305→
   306→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   307→    profile = resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   308→    include_slow_effective = effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   309→
   310→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   311→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   312→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   313→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   314→
   315→    return ScanRuntime(
   316→        args=args,
   317→        state_path=state_file,
   318→        state=state,
   319→        path=path,
   320→        config=config,
   321→        lang=lang,
   322→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   323→        profile=profile,

   325→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   326→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   327→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   328→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,

   332→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(
   333→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   334→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   335→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   336→    """Append stale exclude issues when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   337→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   338→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   339→        return issues
   340→
   341→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   342→    stale = audit_excluded_dirs(
   343→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, get_project_root()
   344→    )
   345→    if not stale:
   346→        return issues
   347→
   348→    augmented = list(issues)
   349→    augmented.extend(stale)

    30→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    31→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    32→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    33→    if isinstance(history, list):
    34→        for entry in reversed(history):

    36→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    37→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    38→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    39→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    40→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    41→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    42→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    43→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    44→    return None

    47→def _count_issues(issues: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    48→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    49→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    50→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    51→
    52→    for issue in issues.values():
    53→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
    54→            continue
    55→        status = issue["status"]
    56→        tier = issue.get("tier", 3)
    57→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    58→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    59→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    60→
    61→    return counters, tier_stats

   256→            subjective_assessments,
   257→            target=integrity_target,
   258→        )
   259→    state["subjective_integrity"] = integrity_meta
   260→
   261→    has_active_checks = any((count or 0) > 0 for count in merged.values())
   262→    if not has_active_checks and not subjective_assessments:
   263→        _set_perfect_scores(state)
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    allowed_subjective = _resolve_allowed_subjective_dimensions(state)
   267→
   268→    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
   269→        issues,
   270→        merged,
   271→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   272→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
   273→    )
   274→    _materialize_dimension_scores(state, bundle)
   275→
   276→
   277→def recompute_stats(
   278→    state: StateModel,
   279→    scan_path: str | None = None,
   280→    *,
   281→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   282→) -> None:
   283→    """Recompute stats and canonical health scores from issues."""
   284→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   285→    issues = path_scoped_issues(state["issues"], scan_path)
   286→    counters, tier_stats = _count_issues(issues)
   287→    state["stats"] = {
   288→        "total": sum(counters.values()),
   289→        **counters,
   290→        "by_tier": {
   291→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   292→        },
   293→    }
   294→    _update_objective_health(
   295→        state,
   296→        issues,
   297→        subjective_integrity_target=subjective_integrity_target,
   298→    )
   299→
   300→
   301→__all__ = [
   302→    "_count_issues",
   303→    "_update_objective_health",
   304→    "recompute_stats",
   305→]
   306→
```

    48→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    49→        totals = {

    51→                "checks": 0,
    52→                "failing": 0,
    53→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    54→                "detectors": {},
    55→            }

    58→

    62→                continue
    63→

    65→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    66→                pass_rate, failing, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    67→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    68→                totals[mode]["failing"] += failing
    69→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    70→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {

    72→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    73→                    "failing": failing,
    74→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me create a final summary based on all the exploration I've done.

## Summary: How Rescanning Changes Task Prioritization

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's the mechanism by which a rescan causes priority reordering:

### **The Core Issue: Subjective Assessment Staleness**

When a rescan occurs, the system marks subjective assessments as **stale** (needing refresh), which directly affects task prioritization in the `next` command. This happens through several interconnected mechanisms:

#### **1. Mechanical Changes Trigger Staleness (`merge.py` lines 58-105)**

When issues from certain "mechanical" detectors change during a scan merge, their related subjective dimensions are marked stale:

```python
_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    # ... 10+ more detector->dimension mappings
}
```

The merge operation calls `_mark_stale_on_mechanical_change()` which sets:
```python
payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_issues_changed"
payload["stale_since"] = now
```

#### **2. Review Resolution Also Triggers Staleness (`resolution.py` lines 42-83)**

When you resolve review issues, the related subjective assessments are marked stale:
```python
payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_issue_{status}"
payload["stale_since"] = now
```

#### **3. Stale Assessments Flow to Plan Queue (`stale_policy.py` and `stale_dimensions.py`)**

The `needs_review_refresh` flag on subjective assessments determines which dimensions get queued:

- **`current_stale_ids()`** in `stale_policy.py` (lines 24-41): Returns dimensions marked stale
- **`sync_stale_dimensions()`** in `stale_dimensions.py` (lines 259-310): Injects stale dimension IDs into the plan's `queue_order` when:
  - No objective backlog exists (mid-cycle), OR
  - A cycle just completed (post-cycle: front-of-queue priority)

#### **4. Queue Ordering Reflects the Plan (`work_queue/core.py`)**

The work queue builder:
1. Reads `plan["queue_order"]` to determine position/priority
2. Stamps sort keys based on plan position (lines in `plan_order.py`)
3. Subjective dimensions that get injected at the front due to staleness become higher-priority items in `next`

### **How "Expired 20 Subjective Assessments" Happens**

The message appears when provisional manual override assessments expire at scan time (`workflow.py` lines 254-270):

```python
def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(state):
    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
    for payload in assessments.values():
        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
            continue
        _apply_assessment_reset(payload, source="manual_override_expired", now=now)
        expired += 1  # Count these
```

This only happens when **not mid-cycle** (line 296):
```python
if not _is_mid_cycle(args):
    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(state)
```

### **The Stale Flag In Dimension Scores**

The `stale` field in dimension entries (visible via `scorecard_projection.py` lines 113-117) is computed as:

```python
stale = bool(data.get("stale")) or bool(
    dim_key
    and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
    and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
)
```

This flag determines whether a dimension shows "needs refresh" signals in the UI and affects which dimensions get re-queued.

### **Post-Scan Reconciliation Pipeline**

After a scan, `plan_reconcile.py` runs this sequence:
1. **reconcile_plan_after_scan()** — supersedes disappeared issues
2. **sync_unscored_dimensions()** — inject placeholder dimensions
3. **sync_stale_dimensions()** — inject stale dimensions (THIS IS WHERE PRIORITY CHANGES)
4. **sync_auto_clusters()** — recompute clusters
5. **sync_triage_needed()** — inject triage if review issues changed

Each sync function can modify `plan["queue_order"]`, which directly reorders what `next` shows.

### **Recent Branch Changes (wip/triage-runner)**

The current branch simplifies the `is_triage_stale()` logic:
- **Old**: Checked both genuinely-new review issues AND whether triage stages were in the queue
- **New**: Only checks for genuinely-new review issues, trusting the lifecycle filter to manage triage stage visibility

This reduces false-positive staleness detection when triage is already in progress.

   120→def _has_objective_backlog(
   121→    state: StateModel,
   122→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   123→) -> bool:
   124→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   125→    if policy is not None:
   126→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   127→    return any(
   128→        f.get("status") == "open"
   129→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   130→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   131→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   132→    )

   134→
   135→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   136→    order: list[str],
   137→    *,
   138→    keep_ids: set[str],
   139→    pruned: list[str],
   140→) -> None:
   141→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   142→    to_remove = [
   143→        fid for fid in order
   144→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   145→        and fid not in keep_ids
   146→    ]
   147→    for fid in to_remove:
   148→        order.remove(fid)
   149→        pruned.append(fid)
   150→
   151→
   152→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   153→    order: list[str],
   154→    *,
   155→    inject_ids: set[str],
   156→    injected: list[str],
   157→) -> None:
   158→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   159→
   160→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   161→    """
   162→    existing = set(order)
   163→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   164→        if sid not in existing:
   165→            order.append(sid)
   166→            injected.append(sid)
   167→

   170→# Helpers for sync_triage_needed
   171→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   172→
   173→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
   174→    state: StateModel,
   175→    meta: dict,
   176→) -> set[str]:
   177→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
   178→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   179→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids
   180→
   181→
   182→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   183→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   184→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   185→        while sid in order:
   186→            order.remove(sid)
   187→
   188→
   189→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   190→    order: list[str],
   191→    confirmed: set[str],
   192→) -> bool:
   193→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   194→
   195→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   196→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   197→    """
   198→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
   199→    existing = set(order)
   200→    injected_count = 0
   201→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   202→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   203→            order.append(sid)
   204→            injected_count += 1
   205→    return injected_count > 0

   208→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   209→# Unscored dimension sync (back of queue, unconditional)
   210→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   211→
   212→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   213→    plan: PlanModel,
   214→    state: StateModel,
   215→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   216→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   217→
   218→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   219→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   220→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   221→    2. **Inject** — append currently-unscored IDs to the *back* of
   222→       ``queue_order``.  Never reorders existing items.
   223→    """
   224→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   225→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   226→
   227→    # Mid-cycle: don't inject unscored dimensions — they'll surface at cycle end.
   228→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   229→        return result
   230→
   231→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   232→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   233→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   234→
   235→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   236→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   237→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   238→
   239→    # --- Inject: append unscored IDs to back of queue ---------------------
   240→    _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=unscored_ids, injected=result.injected)
   241→
   242→    return result
   243→
   244→
   245→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   246→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   247→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   248→
   249→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   250→    plan: PlanModel,
   251→    state: StateModel,
   252→    *,
   253→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   254→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   255→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   256→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   257→
   258→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   259→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   260→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   261→    2. Append stale and under-target dimension IDs to the *back* when either:
   262→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle), OR
   263→       b. A cycle just completed.
   264→       Never reorders existing items.
   265→    """
   266→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   267→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   268→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   269→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   270→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   271→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   272→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   273→
   274→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   275→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   276→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   277→
   278→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   279→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   280→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   281→
   282→    if not should_inject:
   283→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   284→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   285→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   286→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   287→        to_evict = [
   288→            fid for fid in order
   289→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   290→            and fid in injectable_ids
   291→        ]
   292→        for fid in to_evict:
   293→            order.remove(fid)
   294→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   295→
   296→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   297→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   298→
   299→    return result
   300→
   301→

   303→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   304→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   305→
   306→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   307→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   308→
   309→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   310→    """
   311→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   312→
   313→
   314→@dataclass
   315→class TriageSyncResult:
   316→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   317→
   318→    injected: bool = False
   319→    pruned: bool = False
   320→
   321→    @property
   322→    def changes(self) -> int:
   323→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   324→
   325→
   326→def sync_triage_needed(
   327→    plan: PlanModel,
   328→    state: StateModel,
   329→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   330→    """Append triage stage IDs to back of queue when review issues change.
   331→
   332→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   333→
   334→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   335→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   336→
   337→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   338→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   339→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   340→    """
   341→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   342→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   343→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   344→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   345→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   346→
   347→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   348→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   349→
   350→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   351→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   352→
   353→    if already_present:
   354→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   355→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   356→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   357→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   358→        # user-started triage session.
   359→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   360→            new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   361→
   362→            if not new_since_triage:
   363→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   364→                _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   365→                if current_hash:
   366→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   367→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   368→                result.pruned = True
   369→        return result
   370→
   371→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   372→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   373→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   374→        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   375→
   376→        if new_since_triage:
   377→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   378→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, confirmed)
   379→        else:
   380→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   381→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   382→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   383→
   384→    return result
   385→

   444→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   445→    plan: PlanModel,
   446→    state: StateModel,
   447→    *,
   448→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   449→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   450→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   451→
   452→    Only injects when:
   453→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   454→    - At least one objective issue exists
   455→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   456→    - No triage stages are pending
   457→
   458→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   459→    """
   460→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   461→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   462→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   463→
   464→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   465→        return result
   466→
   467→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   468→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   469→        return result
   470→
   471→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   472→    if policy is not None:
   473→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   474→            return result
   475→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   476→    else:
   477→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   478→        if unscored:
   479→            return result
   480→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   481→        has_objective = any(
   482→            f.get("status") == "open"
   483→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   484→            for f in issues.values()
   485→        )
   486→    if not has_objective:
   487→        return result
   488→
   489→    order.append(WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   490→    result.injected = True
   491→    return result

   511→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   512→    through the plan.

   515→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state)

   524→    @property
   525→    def changes(self) -> int:
   526→        return int(self.injected)
   527→
   528→
   529→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   530→    plan: PlanModel,
   531→    state: StateModel,
   532→    *,
   533→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   534→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   535→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   536→
   537→    Only injects when:
   538→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   539→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   540→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   541→
   542→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   543→    """
   544→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   545→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   546→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   547→
   548→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   549→        return result
   550→
   551→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   552→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   553→        return result
   554→
   555→    order.append(WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   556→    result.injected = True
   557→    return result
   558→
   559→
   560→@dataclass
   561→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   562→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   563→
   564→    injected: bool = False
   565→
   566→    @property
   567→    def changes(self) -> int:
   568→        return int(self.injected)
   569→
   570→
   571→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   572→    plan: PlanModel,
   573→    state: StateModel,
   574→    *,
   575→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   576→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   577→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   578→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   579→
   580→    Injects when either:
   581→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   582→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   583→
   584→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   585→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   586→    """
   587→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   588→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   589→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   590→
   591→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   592→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   593→        return result
   594→
   595→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   596→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   597→
   598→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   599→    if not should_inject:
   600→        if policy is not None:
   601→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   602→        else:
   603→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   604→
   605→    if not should_inject:
   606→        return result
   607→
   608→    order.append(WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   609→    result.injected = True
   610→    return result
   611→
   612→
   613→__all__ = [
   614→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   615→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   616→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   617→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   618→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   619→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",

   173→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
   174→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
   175→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   176→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   177→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   178→    if not has_triage:
   179→        return ""
   180→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   181→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   182→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check") if s in stages]
   183→    if completed:
   184→        return (
   185→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/5 stages complete) — "
   186→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   187→        )
   188→    return (
   189→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   190→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   191→    )
   192→

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle issue churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import get_issue_note
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_meta import append_log_entry
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_skip import resurface_stale_skips
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    SupersededEntry,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    19→    SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES,
    20→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    21→    sync_triage_needed,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    24→
    25→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass
    29→class ReconcileResult:
    30→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    31→
    32→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    34→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    35→    clusters_completed: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    36→    changes: int = 0
    37→
    38→
    39→def _find_candidates(
    40→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    41→) -> list[str]:
    42→    """Find open issues that could be remaps for a disappeared issue."""
    43→    candidates: list[str] = []
    44→    for fid, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items():
    45→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    46→            continue
    47→        if issue.get("detector") == detector and issue.get("file") == file:
    48→            candidates.append(fid)
    49→    return candidates
    50→
    51→
    52→def _is_issue_alive(state: StateModel, issue_id: str) -> bool:
    53→    """Return True if the issue exists and is open."""
    54→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    55→    if issue is None:
    56→        return False
    57→    return issue.get("status") == "open"
    58→
    59→
    60→def _supersede_id(
    61→    plan: PlanModel,
    62→    state: StateModel,
    63→    issue_id: str,
    64→    now: str,
    65→) -> bool:
    66→    """Move a disappeared issue to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    67→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    68→    detector = ""
    69→    file = ""
    70→    summary = ""
    71→    if issue:
    72→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    73→        file = issue.get("file", "")
    74→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    75→
    76→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    77→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    78→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != issue_id]
    79→
    80→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    81→        "original_id": issue_id,
    82→        "original_detector": detector,
    83→        "original_file": file,
    84→        "original_summary": summary,
    85→        "status": "superseded",
    86→        "superseded_at": now,
    87→        "remapped_to": None,
    88→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    89→    }
    90→
    91→    # Preserve any existing override note
    92→    override_note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    93→    if override_note:
    94→        entry["note"] = override_note
    95→
    96→    plan["superseded"][issue_id] = entry
    97→
    98→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster issue_ids
    99→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   100→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   101→    if issue_id in order:
   102→        order.remove(issue_id)
   103→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   104→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, {issue_id})
   105→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   106→        ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   107→        if issue_id in ids:
   108→            ids.remove(issue_id)
   109→
   110→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
   111→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(issue_id)
   112→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   113→        override["cluster"] = None
   114→        override["updated_at"] = now
   115→
   116→    return True
   117→
   118→
   119→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   120→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   121→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   122→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   123→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   124→
   125→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   126→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   127→        try:
   128→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   129→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   130→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   131→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   132→                to_prune.append(fid)
   133→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   134→            to_prune.append(fid)
   135→
   136→    for fid in to_prune:
   137→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   138→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   139→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   140→
   141→    return to_prune
   142→
   143→
   144→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   145→    plan: PlanModel,
   146→    state: StateModel,
   147→) -> ReconcileResult:
   148→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   149→
   150→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   151→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   152→    """
   153→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   154→    result = ReconcileResult()
   155→    now = utc_now()
   156→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   157→
   158→    # Collect all issue IDs referenced by the plan
   159→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   160→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   161→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   162→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   163→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   164→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   165→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   166→
   167→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   168→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   169→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   170→    referenced_ids = {
   171→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   172→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   173→    }
   174→
   175→    # Snapshot non-epic cluster sizes before superseding so we can detect
   176→    # clusters that become empty (all issues resolved by scan).
   177→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   178→    pre_sizes: dict[str, int] = {
   179→        name: len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   180→        for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   181→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   182→    }
   183→
   184→    # Check each referenced ID
   185→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   186→        if not _is_issue_alive(state, fid):
   187→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   188→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   189→                result.changes += 1
   190→
   191→    # Detect manual clusters that just became empty (all issues auto-resolved).
   192→    # Log cluster_done so the plan tracks completion the same way as user resolves.
   193→    for name, prev_size in pre_sizes.items():
   194→        if prev_size == 0:
   195→            continue  # was already empty
   196→        cluster = clusters.get(name)
   197→        if cluster is None:
   198→            continue
   199→        if len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])) == 0:
   200→            result.clusters_completed.append(name)
   201→            append_log_entry(
   202→                plan,
   203→                "cluster_done",
   204→                issue_ids=[],
   205→                cluster_name=name,
   206→                actor="system",
   207→                detail={"reason": "all issues resolved by scan"},
   208→            )
   209→            result.changes += 1
   210→
   211→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead issues, delete empty epics
   212→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   213→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   214→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   215→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   216→            continue
   217→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   218→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if _is_issue_alive(state, fid)]
   219→        if alive_ids != issue_ids:
   220→            cluster["issue_ids"] = alive_ids
   221→            result.changes += 1
   222→        if not alive_ids:
   223→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   224→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   225→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   226→        result.changes += 1
   227→
   228→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   229→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   230→
   231→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   232→    if resurfaced:
   233→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   234→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   235→
   236→    # Prune old superseded entries
   237→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   238→    result.pruned = pruned
   239→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   240→
   241→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   242→    if result.changes > 0:
   243→        append_log_entry(
   244→            plan,
   245→            "reconcile",
   246→            issue_ids=result.superseded,
   247→            actor="system",
   248→            detail={
   249→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   250→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   251→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   252→                "clusters_completed_count": len(result.clusters_completed),
   253→            },
   254→        )
   255→
   256→    return result
   257→
   258→
   259→@dataclass
   260→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
   261→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
   262→
   263→    new_ids: set[str]
   264→    added_to_queue: list[str]
   265→    triage_injected: bool
   266→
   267→
   268→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   269→    plan: PlanModel,
   270→    state: StateModel,
   271→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   272→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   273→
   274→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order and injects triage stages
   275→    if needed. Returns None when there are no new issues to sync.
   276→    """
   277→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   278→    new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   279→    if not new_ids:
   280→        return None
   281→
   282→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   283→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   284→    existing = set(order)
   285→    added: list[str] = []
   286→    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
   287→        if fid not in existing:
   288→            order.append(fid)
   289→            added.append(fid)
   290→
   291→    # Inject triage stages if needed
   292→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   293→    triage_injected = bool(
   294→        triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "injected", False)
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   298→        new_ids=new_ids,
   299→        added_to_queue=added,
   300→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   301→    )
   302→
   303→
   304→__all__ = [
   305→    "ReconcileResult",
   306→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   307→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   308→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   309→]
   310→

    35→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:

    94→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    95→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
    96→
    97→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
    98→    """
    99→    from desloppify.engine._plan.triage_playbook import (
   100→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   101→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
   102→    )
   103→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   104→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   105→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   109→    order_set = set(order)
   110→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   111→    if not present:
   112→        return []
   113→
   114→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   115→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   116→
   117→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   118→    open_review_count = sum(
   119→        1 for f in issues.values()
   120→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   121→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   122→    )
   123→
   124→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   125→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
   126→
   127→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   128→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   129→        if sid not in present:
   130→            continue
   131→        if name in confirmed:
   132→            continue
   133→
   134→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   135→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   136→        blocked_by = sorted(
   137→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   138→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   142→        if name == "commit":
   143→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   144→
   145→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   146→            "id": sid,
   147→            "tier": 1,
   148→            "confidence": "high",
   149→            "detector": "triage",
   150→            "file": ".",
   151→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   152→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   153→            "detail": {
   154→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   155→                "stage": name,
   156→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   157→            },
   158→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   159→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   160→        }
   161→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   162→        items.append(item)
   163→    return items
   164→

     2→

    25→
    26→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    27→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    28→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    29→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    30→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    31→_UNDER_TARGET_KEY = "subjective::under-target"
    32→_UNDER_TARGET_NAME = "auto/under-target-review"
    33→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
