```
    35→
    36→def _manual_member_ids(clusters: dict) -> set[str]:
    37→    """Collect all issue IDs belonging to manual (non-auto) clusters."""
    38→    ids: set[str] = set()
    39→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    40→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    41→            ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    42→    return ids
    43→
    44→
    45→def _group_clusterable_issues(
    46→    issues: dict,
    47→    *,
    48→    manual_member_ids: set[str],
    49→) -> tuple[dict[str, list[str]], dict[str, dict]]:
    50→    """Group open, non-suppressed, non-manual issues by detector/subtype key.
    51→
    52→    Returns (groups_by_key, issue_data) where groups_by_key maps grouping keys
    53→    to lists of issue IDs, filtered to clusters >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE.
    54→    """
    55→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    56→    issue_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
    57→    for fid, issue in issues.items():
    58→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    59→            continue
    60→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
    61→            continue
    62→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
    63→            continue
    64→
    65→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    66→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    67→        key = _grouping_key(issue, meta)
    68→        if key is None:
    69→            continue
    70→
    71→        groups[key].append(fid)
    72→        issue_data[fid] = issue
    73→
    74→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
    75→    return groups, issue_data
    76→
    77→
    78→def _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
    79→    plan: dict,
    80→    *,
    81→    clusters: dict,
    82→    existing_name: str,
    83→    member_ids: list[str],
    84→    now: str,
    85→) -> int:
    86→    """Sync member IDs for a user-modified cluster, returns count of changes."""
    87→    cluster = clusters[existing_name]
    88→    changes = 0
    89→    existing_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    90→    new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
    91→    if new_ids:
    92→        cluster["issue_ids"].extend(new_ids)
    93→        cluster["updated_at"] = now
    94→        changes = 1
    95→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    96→    for fid in member_ids:
    97→        if fid not in overrides:
    98→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
    99→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = existing_name
   100→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   101→    return changes
   102→
   103→
   104→def _subjective_state_sets(
   105→    state: StateModel,
   106→    *,
   107→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   108→    target_strict: float,
   109→) -> tuple[set, set, set]:
   110→    """Return (stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids) for subjective cluster logic."""
   111→    if policy is not None:
   112→        unscored_ids = policy.unscored_ids
   113→        stale_ids = policy.stale_ids
   114→        under_target_ids = policy.under_target_ids
   115→    else:
   116→        unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   117→        stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   118→        under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   119→    return stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids
   120→
   121→
   122→def _has_objective_backlog(
   123→    issues: dict,
   124→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   125→) -> bool:
   126→    """Check if objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   127→    if policy is not None:
   128→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   129→    return any(
   130→        f.get("status") == "open"
   131→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   132→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   133→        for f in issues.values()
   134→    )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _sync_auto_cluster(
   138→    plan: dict,
   139→    clusters: dict,
   140→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   141→    *,
   142→    cluster_key: str,
   143→    cluster_name: str,
   144→    member_ids: list[str],
   145→    description: str,
   146→    action: str,
   147→    now: str,
   148→    optional: bool = False,
   149→) -> int:
   150→    """Create or update an auto-cluster and sync its override entries."""
   151→    changes = 0
   152→    existing_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key)
   153→    if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   154→        cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   155→        old_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   156→        new_ids_set = set(member_ids)
   157→        if (
   158→            old_ids != new_ids_set
   159→            or cluster.get("description") != description
   160→            or cluster.get("action") != action
   161→        ):
   162→            cluster["issue_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   163→            cluster["description"] = description
   164→            cluster["action"] = action
   165→            cluster["updated_at"] = now
   166→            changes = 1
   167→    else:
   168→        new_cluster = {
   169→            "name": cluster_name,
   170→            "description": description,
   171→            "issue_ids": list(member_ids),
   172→            "created_at": now,
   173→            "updated_at": now,
   174→            "auto": True,
   175→            "cluster_key": cluster_key,
   176→            "action": action,
   177→            "user_modified": False,
   178→        }
   179→        if optional:
   180→            new_cluster["optional"] = True
   181→        clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   182→        existing_by_key[cluster_key] = cluster_name
   183→        changes = 1
   184→
   185→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   186→    current_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key, cluster_name)
   187→    for fid in member_ids:
   188→        if fid not in overrides:
   189→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   190→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   191→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   192→
   193→    return changes
   194→
   195→
   196→def sync_issue_clusters(
   197→    plan: dict,
   198→    issues: dict,
   199→    clusters: dict,
   200→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   201→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   202→    now: str,
   203→) -> int:
   204→    """Group open issues by detector/subtype and sync auto-clusters."""
   205→    changes = 0
   206→
   207→    groups, issue_data = _group_clusterable_issues(
   208→        issues, manual_member_ids=_manual_member_ids(clusters)
   209→    )
   210→
   211→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   212→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   213→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   214→
   215→        rep = issue_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   216→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   217→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   218→        members = [issue_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in issue_data]
   219→
   220→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   221→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   222→
   223→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   224→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   225→
   226→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   227→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   228→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   229→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   230→                changes += _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
   231→                    plan,
   232→                    clusters=clusters,
   233→                    existing_name=existing_name,
   234→                    member_ids=member_ids,
   235→                    now=now,
   236→                )
   237→                continue
   238→
   239→        if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   240→            cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   241→
   242→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   243→            plan,
   244→            clusters,
   245→            existing_by_key,
   246→            cluster_key=key,
   247→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   248→            member_ids=member_ids,
   249→            description=description,
   250→            action=action,
   251→            now=now,
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    return changes
   255→
   256→
   257→def sync_subjective_clusters(
   258→    plan: dict,
   259→    state: StateModel,
   260→    issues: dict,
   261→    clusters: dict,
   262→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   263→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   264→    now: str,
   265→    *,
   266→    target_strict: float,
   267→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   268→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   269→) -> int:
   270→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   271→    changes = 0
   272→
   273→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   274→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   275→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    stale_state_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_state_ids = _subjective_state_sets(
   279→        state, policy=policy, target_strict=target_strict
   280→    )
   281→
   282→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   283→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   284→    )
   285→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   286→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids
   287→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   288→    )
   289→
   290→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   291→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   292→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   293→        description = (
   294→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   295→        )
   296→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   297→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   298→            plan,
   299→            clusters,
   300→            existing_by_key,
   301→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   302→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   303→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   304→            description=description,
   305→            action=action,
   306→            now=now,
   307→        )
   308→
   309→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   310→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   311→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   312→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   313→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   314→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   315→            plan,
   316→            clusters,
   317→            existing_by_key,
   318→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   319→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   320→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   321→            description=description,
   322→            action=action,
   323→            now=now,
   324→        )
   325→
   326→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(under_target_ids)
   327→
   328→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   329→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   330→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   331→    ut_prune = [
   332→        fid for fid in prev_ut_ids
   333→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   334→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   335→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   336→        and fid in order
   337→    ]
   338→    for fid in ut_prune:
   339→        order.remove(fid)
   340→        changes += 1
   341→
   342→    has_objective_items = _has_objective_backlog(issues, policy)
   343→
   344→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   345→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   346→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   347→        description = (
   348→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   349→            f"dimensions under target score"
   350→        )
   351→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   352→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   353→            plan,
   354→            clusters,
   355→            existing_by_key,
   356→            [REDACTED],
   357→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   358→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   359→            description=description,
   360→            action=action,
   361→            now=now,
   362→            optional=True,
   363→        )
   364→
   365→        existing_order = set(order)
   366→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   367→            if fid not in existing_order:
   368→                order.append(fid)
   369→
   370→    if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:
   371→        objective_evict = [
   372→            fid for fid in order
   373→            if fid in under_target_ids
   374→        ]
   375→        for fid in objective_evict:
   376→            order.remove(fid)
   377→            changes += 1
   378→
   379→    return changes
   380→
   381→
   382→def prune_stale_clusters(
   383→    plan: dict,
   384→    issues: dict,
   385→    clusters: dict,
   386→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   387→    now: str,
   388→) -> int:
   389→    """Delete auto-clusters that no longer have matching groups."""
   390→    changes = 0
   391→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   392→        cluster = clusters[name]
   393→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   394→            continue
   395→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   396→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   397→            continue
   398→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   399→            alive = [
   400→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   401→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   402→            ]
   403→            if alive:
   404→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])):
   405→                    cluster["issue_ids"] = alive
   406→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   407→                    changes += 1
   408→                continue
   409→        del clusters[name]
   410→        for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   411→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   412→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   413→                override["cluster"] = None
   414→                override["updated_at"] = now
   415→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   416→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   417→        changes += 1
   418→    return changes
   419→
   420→
   421→__all__ = ["prune_stale_clusters", "sync_issue_clusters", "sync_subjective_clusters"]
   422→
```

I need to understand the eviction logic in `sync_stale_dimensions` and whether it's still needed after the "always append to back" refactor.

In `desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`, `sync_stale_dimensions` has an eviction block (around lines 282-294) that removes stale/under-target subjective items from the queue mid-cycle when an objective backlog exists. When the backlog clears, those items get re-appended at the back — which changes their position.

Questions to answer:
1. What is the lifecycle of a subjective item in the queue? Trace: unscored → scored → stale → refreshed. How does each transition affect queue presence?
2. Is the eviction actually needed? What would happen if we just left stale items in the queue during mid-cycle? Would the work queue builder skip them anyway? Check `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/` for any filtering that already handles this.
3. Look at `desloppify/engine/_plan/subjective_policy.py` — does `SubjectiveVisibility` already gate subjective items from appearing in `next` output?
4. Check `desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py` and any queue display/next logic to see if subjective items are filtered at display time rather than needing queue-level eviction.
5. Look at `plan_reconcile.py` — does the eviction happen every scan, meaning items bounce in and out repeatedly?

The goal: determine if eviction can be removed entirely (relying on display-time filtering instead), which would make the queue truly position-stable.

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — append never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *back* of the queue unconditionally.
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→
    10→Invariant: new items are always appended — sync never reorders existing queue.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan import stale_policy as stale_policy_mod
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    20→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    21→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    24→
    25→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    26→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    27→
    28→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    29→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    30→    "triage::observe",
    31→    "triage::reflect",
    32→    "triage::organize",
    33→    "triage::enrich",
    34→    "triage::sense-check",
    35→    "triage::commit",
    36→)
    37→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    38→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    39→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    40→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    41→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    42→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    43→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    44→
    45→
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# Result dataclasses
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→@dataclass
    51→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    52→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    53→
    54→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    56→
    57→    @property
    58→    def changes(self) -> int:
    59→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    60→
    61→
    62→@dataclass
    63→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    64→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    65→
    66→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    67→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    68→
    69→    @property
    70→    def changes(self) -> int:
    71→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    72→
    73→
    74→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    75→# ID helpers
    76→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    77→
    78→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    79→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    80→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    81→        state,
    82→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    83→    )
    84→
    85→
    86→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    87→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    88→
    89→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    90→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    91→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    92→    """
    93→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    94→        state,
    95→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    96→    )
    97→
    98→
    99→def current_under_target_ids(
   100→    state: StateModel,
   101→    *,
   102→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   103→) -> set[str]:
   104→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   105→
   106→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   107→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   108→    """
   109→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   110→        state,
   111→        target_strict=target_strict,
   112→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   113→    )
   114→
   115→
   116→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→# Helpers for sync_stale_dimensions / sync_unscored_dimensions
   118→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→
   120→def _has_objective_backlog(
   121→    state: StateModel,
   122→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   123→) -> bool:
   124→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   125→    if policy is not None:
   126→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   127→    return any(
   128→        f.get("status") == "open"
   129→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   130→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   131→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   132→    )
   133→
   134→
   135→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   136→    order: list[str],
   137→    *,
   138→    keep_ids: set[str],
   139→    pruned: list[str],
   140→) -> None:
   141→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   142→    to_remove = [
   143→        fid for fid in order
   144→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   145→        and fid not in keep_ids
   146→    ]
   147→    for fid in to_remove:
   148→        order.remove(fid)
   149→        pruned.append(fid)
   150→
   151→
   152→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   153→    order: list[str],
   154→    *,
   155→    inject_ids: set[str],
   156→    injected: list[str],
   157→) -> None:
   158→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   159→
   160→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   161→    """
   162→    existing = set(order)
   163→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   164→        if sid not in existing:
   165→            order.append(sid)
   166→            injected.append(sid)
   167→
   168→
   169→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   170→# Helpers for sync_triage_needed
   171→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   172→
   173→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
   174→    state: StateModel,
   175→    meta: dict,
   176→) -> set[str]:
   177→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
   178→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   179→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids
   180→
   181→
   182→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   183→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   184→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   185→        while sid in order:
   186→            order.remove(sid)
   187→
   188→
   189→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   190→    order: list[str],
   191→    confirmed: set[str],
   192→) -> bool:
   193→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   194→
   195→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   196→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   197→    """
   198→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
   199→    existing = set(order)
   200→    injected_count = 0
   201→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   202→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   203→            order.append(sid)
   204→            injected_count += 1
   205→    return injected_count > 0
   206→
   207→
   208→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   209→# Unscored dimension sync (back of queue, unconditional)
   210→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   211→
   212→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   213→    plan: PlanModel,
   214→    state: StateModel,
   215→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   216→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   217→
   218→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   219→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   220→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   221→    2. **Inject** — append currently-unscored IDs to the *back* of
   222→       ``queue_order``.  Never reorders existing items.
   223→    """
   224→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   225→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   226→
   227→    # Mid-cycle: don't inject unscored dimensions — they'll surface at cycle end.
   228→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   229→        return result
   230→
   231→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   232→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   233→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   234→
   235→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   236→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   237→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   238→
   239→    # --- Inject: append unscored IDs to back of queue ---------------------
   240→    _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=unscored_ids, injected=result.injected)
   241→
   242→    return result
   243→
   244→
   245→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   246→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   247→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   248→
   249→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   250→    plan: PlanModel,
   251→    state: StateModel,
   252→    *,
   253→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   254→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   255→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   256→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   257→
   258→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   259→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   260→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   261→    2. Append stale and under-target dimension IDs to the *back* when either:
   262→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle), OR
   263→       b. A cycle just completed.
   264→       Never reorders existing items.
   265→    """
   266→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   267→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   268→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   269→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   270→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   271→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   272→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   273→
   274→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   275→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   276→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   277→
   278→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   279→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   280→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   281→
   282→    if not should_inject:
   283→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   284→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   285→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   286→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   287→        to_evict = [
   288→            fid for fid in order
   289→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   290→            and fid in injectable_ids
   291→        ]
   292→        for fid in to_evict:
   293→            order.remove(fid)
   294→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   295→
   296→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   297→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   298→
   299→    return result
   300→
   301→
   302→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   303→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   304→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   305→
   306→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   307→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   308→
   309→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   310→    """
   311→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   312→
   313→
   314→@dataclass
   315→class TriageSyncResult:
   316→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   317→
   318→    injected: bool = False
   319→    pruned: bool = False
   320→
   321→    @property
   322→    def changes(self) -> int:
   323→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   324→
   325→
   326→def sync_triage_needed(
   327→    plan: PlanModel,
   328→    state: StateModel,
   329→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   330→    """Append triage stage IDs to back of queue when review issues change.
   331→
   332→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   333→
   334→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   335→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   336→
   337→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   338→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   339→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   340→    """
   341→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   342→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   343→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   344→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   345→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   346→
   347→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   348→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   349→
   350→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   351→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   352→
   353→    if already_present:
   354→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   355→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   356→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   357→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   358→        # user-started triage session.
   359→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   360→            new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   361→
   362→            if not new_since_triage:
   363→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   364→                _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   365→                if current_hash:
   366→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   367→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   368→                result.pruned = True
   369→        return result
   370→
   371→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   372→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   373→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   374→        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   375→
   376→        if new_since_triage:
   377→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   378→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, confirmed)
   379→        else:
   380→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   381→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   382→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   383→
   384→    return result
   385→
   386→
   387→@dataclass
   388→class ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   389→    """What changed during a score-checkpoint sync."""
   390→
   391→    injected: bool = False
   392→
   393→    @property
   394→    def changes(self) -> int:
   395→        return int(self.injected)
   396→
   397→
   398→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   399→    plan: PlanModel,
   400→    state: StateModel,
   401→    *,
   402→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   403→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   404→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   405→
   406→    Injects when:
   407→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   408→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   409→
   410→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   411→    """
   412→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   413→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   414→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   415→
   416→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   417→        return result
   418→
   419→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   420→    if policy is not None:
   421→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   422→            return result
   423→    else:
   424→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   425→        if unscored:
   426→            return result
   427→
   428→    order.append(WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   429→    result.injected = True
   430→    return result
   431→
   432→
   433→@dataclass
   434→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   435→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   436→
   437→    injected: bool = False
   438→
   439→    @property
   440→    def changes(self) -> int:
   441→        return int(self.injected)
   442→
   443→
   444→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   445→    plan: PlanModel,
   446→    state: StateModel,
   447→    *,
   448→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   449→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   450→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   451→
   452→    Only injects when:
   453→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   454→    - At least one objective issue exists
   455→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   456→    - No triage stages are pending
   457→
   458→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   459→    """
   460→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   461→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   462→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   463→
   464→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   465→        return result
   466→
   467→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   468→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   469→        return result
   470→
   471→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   472→    if policy is not None:
   473→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   474→            return result
   475→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   476→    else:
   477→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   478→        if unscored:
   479→            return result
   480→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   481→        has_objective = any(
   482→            f.get("status") == "open"
   483→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   484→            for f in issues.values()
   485→        )
   486→    if not has_objective:
   487→        return result
   488→
   489→    order.append(WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   490→    result.injected = True
   491→    return result
   492→
   493→
   494→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   495→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   496→
   497→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   498→    """
   499→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   500→
   501→
   502→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   503→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   504→
   505→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   506→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   507→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   508→    resolved since then.
   509→
   510→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   511→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   512→    through the plan.
   513→    """
   514→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   515→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state)
   516→
   517→
   518→@dataclass
   519→class ImportScoresSyncResult:
   520→    """What changed during an import-scores sync."""
   521→
   522→    injected: bool = False
   523→
   524→    @property
   525→    def changes(self) -> int:
   526→        return int(self.injected)
   527→
   528→
   529→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   530→    plan: PlanModel,
   531→    state: StateModel,
   532→    *,
   533→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   534→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   535→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   536→
   537→    Only injects when:
   538→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   539→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   540→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   541→
   542→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   543→    """
   544→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   545→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   546→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   547→
   548→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   549→        return result
   550→
   551→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   552→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   553→        return result
   554→
   555→    order.append(WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   556→    result.injected = True
   557→    return result
   558→
   559→
   560→@dataclass
   561→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   562→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   563→
   564→    injected: bool = False
   565→
   566→    @property
   567→    def changes(self) -> int:
   568→        return int(self.injected)
   569→
   570→
   571→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   572→    plan: PlanModel,
   573→    state: StateModel,
   574→    *,
   575→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   576→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   577→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   578→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   579→
   580→    Injects when either:
   581→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   582→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   583→
   584→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   585→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   586→    """
   587→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   588→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   589→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   590→
   591→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   592→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   593→        return result
   594→
   595→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   596→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   597→
   598→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   599→    if not should_inject:
   600→        if policy is not None:
   601→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   602→        else:
   603→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   604→
   605→    if not should_inject:
   606→        return result
   607→
   608→    order.append(WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   609→    result.injected = True
   610→    return result
   611→
   612→
   613→__all__ = [
   614→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   615→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   616→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   617→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   618→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   619→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   620→    "WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID",
   621→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   622→    "WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID",
   623→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
   624→    "WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID",
```

```
1→"""Pure policy helpers for stale/unscored subjective planning decisions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import all_subjective_entries
    11→
    12→_REVIEW_DETECTORS = ("review", "concerns")
    13→
    14→
    15→def open_review_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    16→    """Return IDs of open review/concerns issues from state."""
    17→    return {
    18→        fid
    19→        for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    20→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def current_stale_ids(
    25→    state: StateModel,
    26→    *,
    27→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    28→) -> set[str]:
    29→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    30→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    31→    if not dim_scores:
    32→        return set()
    33→
    34→    stale: set[str] = set()
    35→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    36→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    37→            continue
    38→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    39→        if dim_key:
    40→            stale.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    41→    return stale
    42→
    43→
    44→def current_unscored_ids(
    45→    state: StateModel,
    46→    *,
    47→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    48→) -> set[str]:
    49→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored."""
    50→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    51→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
    52→        unscored: set[str] = set()
    53→        for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    54→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    55→                continue
    56→            if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    57→                continue
    58→            if dim_key:
    59→                unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    60→        return unscored
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    unscored = set()
    67→    for data in dim_scores.values():
    68→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
    69→            continue
    70→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    71→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
    72→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
    73→            continue
    74→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
    75→            continue
    76→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
    77→        if dim_key:
    78→            unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    79→    return unscored
    80→
    81→
    82→def current_under_target_ids(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    86→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    87→) -> set[str]:
    88→    """Return under-target subjective IDs that are neither stale nor unscored."""
    89→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    90→    if not dim_scores:
    91→        return set()
    92→
    93→    stale_ids = current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    94→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    95→
    96→    under_target: set[str] = set()
    97→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    98→        if entry.get("placeholder") or entry.get("stale"):
    99→            continue
   100→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   101→        if strict_val >= target_strict:
   102→            continue
   103→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
   104→        if not dim_key:
   105→            continue
   106→        item_id = f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}"
   107→        if item_id not in stale_ids and item_id not in unscored_ids:
   108→            under_target.add(item_id)
   109→    return under_target
   110→
   111→
   112→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   113→    """Hash open review/concerns issue IDs to detect triage-relevant changes."""
   114→    review_ids = sorted(open_review_ids(state))
   115→    if not review_ids:
   116→        return ""
   117→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   118→
   119→
   120→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   121→    """Return open review/concerns IDs that appeared since the last triage."""
   122→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   123→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   124→    return open_review_ids(state) - triaged if triaged else set()
   125→
   126→
   127→def is_triage_stale(
   128→    plan: dict,
   129→    state: StateModel,
   130→) -> bool:
   131→    """Return True when genuinely new review issues appeared since last triage.
   132→
   133→    In-progress triage (confirmed stages + stage IDs in queue) is NOT
   134→    considered stale — the lifecycle filter in the work queue already
   135→    forces triage stages to the front.
   136→    """
   137→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   138→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   139→    return bool(open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids)
   140→
   141→
   142→__all__ = [
   143→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   144→    "current_stale_ids",
   145→    "current_under_target_ids",
   146→    "current_unscored_ids",
   147→    "is_triage_stale",
   148→    "open_review_ids",
   149→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   150→]
   151→
```

```
1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions`` and ``auto_cluster``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import issue_in_scan_scope
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    16→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    17→
    18→# Detectors whose issues are NOT objective mechanical work.
    19→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    20→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    21→})
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    25→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    26→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    27→
    28→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective issues?
    29→    objective_count: int  # how many
    30→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    31→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    32→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    33→
    34→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    35→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    36→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    37→            return True  # unconditional
    38→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    39→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    40→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    41→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    42→        return False
    43→
    44→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    45→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    46→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    47→            return False  # never evict unscored
    48→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    49→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    50→        return False
    51→
    52→    @property
    53→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    54→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    55→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    56→
    57→
    58→def _is_evidence_only(issue: dict) -> bool:
    59→    """Return True if the issue is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    60→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    61→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    62→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    63→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    64→        issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    65→        if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
    66→            return True
    67→    return False
    68→
    69→
    70→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = object()
    71→
    72→
    73→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    74→    state: StateModel,
    75→    *,
    76→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    77→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    78→    plan: dict | None = None,
    79→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    80→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    81→
    82→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    83→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    84→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, issues whose IDs
    85→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    86→
    87→    Imports policy helpers from ``stale_policy`` so this module remains
    88→    side-effect free and cycle-safe.
    89→    """
    90→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_policy import (
    91→        current_stale_ids,
    92→        current_under_target_ids,
    93→        current_unscored_ids,
    94→    )
    95→
    96→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
    97→        state.get("scan_path")
    98→        if scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY
    99→        else scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   103→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   104→
   105→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective issues.
   106→    # Evidence-only issues (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   107→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   108→    # Issues outside scan_path and plan-skipped issues are also excluded
   109→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   110→    objective_count = sum(
   111→        1
   112→        for fid, f in issues.items()
   113→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   114→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   115→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   116→        and not _is_evidence_only(f)
   117→        and issue_in_scan_scope(str(f.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   118→        and fid not in skipped_ids
   119→    )
   120→
   121→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   122→    stale = current_stale_ids(state)
   123→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   124→
   125→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   126→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   127→        objective_count=objective_count,
   128→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   129→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   130→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   131→    )
   132→
   133→
   134→__all__ = [
   135→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   136→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   137→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   138→]
   139→
```

```
   123→def migrate_v5_to_v6(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   124→    """Migrate v5 → v6: unified queue system."""
   125→    # cycle-break: schema_migrations.py ↔ schema.py (via stale_dimensions.py)
   126→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   127→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   128→        WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   129→    )
   130→
   131→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   132→
   133→    # Handle legacy synthesis::pending or triage::pending
   134→    for legacy_pending in ("synthesis::pending", "triage::pending"):
   135→        if legacy_pending in order:
   136→            idx = order.index(legacy_pending)
   137→            order.remove(legacy_pending)
   138→            meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {}))
   139→            confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})).keys())
   140→            stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   141→            to_inject = [
   142→                stage_id
   143→                for stage_id, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False)
   144→                if name not in confirmed and stage_id not in order
   145→            ]
   146→            for offset, stage_id in enumerate(to_inject):
   147→                order.insert(idx + offset, stage_id)
   148→            break
   149→
   150→    if plan.pop("pending_plan_gate", False):
   151→        if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in order:
   152→            insert_at = 0
   153→            for idx, issue_id in enumerate(order):
   154→                if issue_id.startswith("triage::") or issue_id.startswith("synthesis::"):
   155→                    insert_at = idx + 1
   156→            order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   157→    else:
   158→        plan.pop("pending_plan_gate", None)

   161→def migrate_synthesis_to_triage(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   162→    """Migrate synthesis::* → triage::* naming throughout the plan.
   163→
   164→    - Renames ``synthesis::*`` IDs to ``triage::*`` in ``queue_order`` and ``skipped``
   165→    - Renames ``epic_synthesis_meta`` key to ``epic_triage_meta``
   166→    - Renames ``synthesis_stages`` to ``triage_stages`` inside that meta dict
   167→    - Renames ``synthesized_ids`` to ``triaged_ids`` inside that meta dict
   168→    - Renames ``synthesis_version`` to ``triage_version`` in cluster dicts
   169→    """
   170→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   171→    for index, issue_id in enumerate(order):
   172→        if issue_id.startswith("synthesis::"):
   173→            order[index] = "triage::" + issue_id[len("synthesis::"):]
   174→
   175→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   176→    for old_key in [key for key in skipped if key.startswith("synthesis::")]:
   177→        new_key = "triage::" + old_key[len("synthesis::"):]
   178→        entry = skipped.pop(old_key)
   179→        if isinstance(entry, dict):
   180→            entry["issue_id"] = new_key
   181→        skipped[new_key] = entry
   182→
   183→    meta = plan.pop("epic_synthesis_meta", None)
   184→    if meta is not None:
   185→        if isinstance(meta, dict):
   186→            _rename_key(meta, "synthesis_stages", "triage_stages")
   187→            _rename_key(meta, "synthesized_ids", "triaged_ids")
   188→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   189→
   190→    for entry in skipped.values():
   191→        if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("kind") == "synthesized_out":
   192→            entry["kind"] = "triaged_out"
   193→
   194→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   195→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   196→            _rename_key(cluster, "synthesis_version", "triage_version")
   197→
```

```
   107→    issue_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]
   108→    if not issue_ids:
   109→        return 0
   110→
   111→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   112→
   113→    # Remove from skipped if present
   114→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   115→    for fid in issue_ids:
   116→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   117→
   118→    # Remove from current position in order
   119→    for fid in issue_ids:
   120→        if fid in order:
   121→            order.remove(fid)
   122→
   123→    # Resolve insertion point
   124→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, issue_ids)
   125→
   126→    # Insert in original order
   127→    for i, fid in enumerate(issue_ids):
   128→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   129→
   130→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   131→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   132→    if position == "top":
   133→        add_promoted_ids(plan, issue_ids)
   134→
   135→    return len(issue_ids)
   136→
   137→
   138→__all__ = ["move_items"]
   139→
```

```
    43→# Sentinel: "read scan_path from state" (the safe default).
    44→# Callers that want to override can pass an explicit str or None.
    45→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE = object()
    46→
    47→
    48→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    49→class QueueBuildOptions:
    50→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    51→
    52→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    53→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    54→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    55→    """
    56→
    57→    # Output control
    58→    count: int | None = 1
    59→    explain: bool = False
    60→
    61→    # Scope filtering
    62→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    63→    scope: str | None = None
    64→    status: str = "open"
    65→    chronic: bool = False
    66→
    67→    # Subjective gating
    68→    include_subjective: bool = True
    69→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    70→
    71→    # Plan integration
    72→    plan: dict | None = None
    73→    include_skipped: bool = False
    74→
    75→    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan)
    76→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    77→
    78→
    79→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    80→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    81→
    82→    items: list[WorkQueueItem]
    83→    total: int
    84→    grouped: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]]
    85→    new_ids: set[str]
    86→
    87→
    88→def build_work_queue(
    89→    state: StateModel,
    90→    *,
    91→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    92→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    93→    """Build a ranked work queue from state issues.
    94→
    95→    Pipeline:
    96→    1. Gather    — issue items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    97→    2. Score     — estimate impact from dimension headroom
    98→    3. Presort   — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
    99→    4. Lifecycle — filter endgame-only items when objective work remains
   100→    5. Sort      — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
   101→    6. Limit     — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   102→    """
   103→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   104→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   105→
   106→    # 1. Gather
   107→    items = build_issue_items(
   108→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   109→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   110→    )
   111→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
   112→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   113→
   114→    # 2. Score
   115→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   116→
   117→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering (part 1: separate skipped items)
   118→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   119→
   120→    # 4. Lifecycle filter — endgame-only items filtered when objective work remains
   121→    items = _apply_lifecycle_filter(items)
   122→
   123→    # 5. Sort & plan post-processing
   124→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   125→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   126→
   127→    # 6. Finalize
   128→    if not items:
   129→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   130→    total = len(items)
   131→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   132→        items = items[:opts.count]
   133→    if opts.explain:
   134→        for item in items:
   135→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   136→
   137→    return {
   138→        "items": items,
   139→        "total": total,
   140→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   141→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   142→    }
   143→
   144→
   145→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   146→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   147→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   148→
   149→
   150→def _resolve_inputs(
   151→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   152→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   153→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   154→    ctx = opts.context
   155→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   156→
   157→    scan_path: str | None = (
   158→        state.get("scan_path")
   159→        if opts.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   160→        else opts.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   161→    )
   162→
   163→    status = opts.status
   164→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   165→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   166→
   167→    try:
   168→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   169→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   170→        threshold = 100.0
   171→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   172→
   173→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   174→
   175→
   176→def _gather_subjective_items(
   177→    state: StateModel,
   178→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   179→    threshold: float,
   180→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   181→    """Build subjective dimension candidates.
   182→
   183→    Lifecycle filtering (endgame gating) happens in _apply_lifecycle_filter,
   184→    not here. This function only handles configuration and scope.
   185→    """
   186→    if not opts.include_subjective:
   187→        return []
   188→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
   189→        return []
   190→    if opts.chronic:
   191→        return []
   192→
   193→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   194→        state, state.get("issues", {}), threshold=threshold,
   195→    )
   196→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
   197→
   198→
   199→def _gather_workflow_items(
   200→    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
   201→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   202→    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
   203→    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
   204→        return []
   205→
   206→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
   207→    for builder in (
   208→        build_score_checkpoint_item,
   209→        build_import_scores_item,
   210→        build_communicate_score_item,
   211→    ):
   212→        item = builder(plan, state)
   213→        if item is not None:
   214→            items.append(item)
   215→    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
   216→    if plan_item is not None:
   217→        items.append(plan_item)
   218→    return items
   219→
   220→
   221→
   222→def _plan_presort(
   223→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], state: StateModel, plan: dict | None,
   224→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   225→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting.
   226→
   227→    Returns ``(new_ids, skipped)`` — skipped items are removed from
   228→    ``items`` in place and returned separately for post-sort re-append.
   229→    """
   230→    if not plan:
   231→        return set(), []
   232→
   233→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   234→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   235→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
   236→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
   237→    items[:] = remaining
   238→    return new_ids, skipped
   239→
   240→
   241→def _plan_postsort(
   242→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   243→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
   244→    plan: dict | None,
   245→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   246→) -> None:
   247→    """Re-append skipped items and stamp positions.
   248→
   249→    Cluster focus filtering is intentionally NOT applied here — it is a
   250→    view-layer concern that callers apply after building the canonical queue.
   251→    This prevents UI focus state from affecting lifecycle decisions (scan
   252→    gating, score display mode, empty-queue fallback).
   253→    """
   254→    if not plan:
   255→        return
   256→
   257→    if opts.include_skipped:
   258→        items.extend(skipped)
   259→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
   260→
   261→
   262→def _has_objective_items(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   263→    """True if any objective mechanical work items remain in the queue."""
   264→    return any(
   265→        i.get("kind") == "issue"
   266→        and i.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   267→        for i in items
   268→    )
   269→
   270→
   271→def _has_initial_reviews(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   272→    """True if any unassessed subjective dimensions need initial review."""
   273→    return any(
   274→        i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   275→        and i.get("initial_review")
   276→        for i in items
   277→    )
   278→
   279→
   280→def _is_endgame_only(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   281→    """True if this item should only appear when the objective queue is drained."""
   282→    return (
   283→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   284→        and not item.get("initial_review")
   285→    )
   286→
   287→
   288→def _has_triage_stages(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   289→    """True if any pending triage stage items are in the queue."""
   290→    return any(
   291→        i.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
   292→        and str(i.get("id", "")).startswith("triage::")
   293→        for i in items
   294→    )
   295→
   296→
   297→def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   298→    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules.
   299→
   300→    The queue enforces a phase order: scan → initial review → triage → objective work.
   301→
   302→    1. **Initial reviews pending** → only show initial-review subjective items.
   303→       The user must complete first-time reviews before working objective items.
   304→    2. **Triage in progress** → only show triage stages and workflow items.
   305→       Cluster work items aren't ready until triage completes (enrich adds
   306→       the detail that makes steps actionable).
   307→    3. **Objective work remains** → show objective items, hide endgame-only
   308→       subjective reassessments (stale re-reviews).
   309→    4. **Objective drained** → everything visible (endgame).
   310→    """
   311→    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
   312→        # Phase 1: only initial reviews visible — triage and workflow
   313→        # items stay hidden until initial reviews are complete.
   314→        return [
   315→            i for i in items
   316→            if i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and i.get("initial_review")
   317→        ]
   318→    if _has_triage_stages(items):
   319→        # Phase 2: triage in progress — only triage stages and workflow items visible.
   320→        # Cluster work items aren't ready until enrich completes.
   321→        return [
   322→            i for i in items
   323→            if i.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
   324→        ]
   325→    if not _has_objective_items(items):
   326→        return items  # endgame: everything visible
   327→    return [i for i in items if not _is_endgame_only(i)]
   328→
   329→
   330→def _empty_queue_fallback(plan: dict | None) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   331→    """Return a 'run scan' nudge when an active plan cycle has cleared."""
   332→    if not plan:
   333→        return []
   334→    plan_scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   335→    if plan_scores.get("strict") is None:
   336→        return []
   337→    return [{
   338→        "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   339→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   340→        "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   341→        "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   342→        "file": "",
   343→        "detector": "workflow",
   344→        "confidence": "high",
   345→    }]
   346→
   347→
   348→__all__ = [
   349→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   350→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   351→    "QueueContext",
   352→    "WorkQueueResult",
   353→    "build_work_queue",
   354→    "collapse_clusters",
   355→    "group_queue_items",
   356→]
   357→
```
