1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import (
     9→    get_issue_description,
    10→    get_issue_note,
    11→    get_issue_override,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    14→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    18→    """Return issue IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    19→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    20→    if not scan_history:
    21→        return set()
    22→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    23→    if not threshold:
    24→        return set()
    25→    return {
    26→        issue_id
    27→        for issue_id, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    28→        if issue.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    29→    }
    30→
    31→
    32→def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
    33→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    34→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    35→
    36→    for item in items:
    37→        issue_id = item["id"]
    38→        description = get_issue_description(plan, issue_id)
    39→        note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    40→        override = get_issue_override(plan, issue_id)
    41→        if description:
    42→            item["plan_description"] = description
    43→        if note:
    44→            item["plan_note"] = note
    45→        if override.get("cluster"):
    46→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    47→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    48→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    49→                "name": cluster_name,
    50→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    51→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", [])),
    52→                "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
    53→            }
    54→
    55→
    56→def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    plan: dict,
    59→    new_ids: set[str],
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    62→
    63→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    64→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    65→    """
    66→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    67→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    68→
    69→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    70→    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    71→        if issue_id not in skipped_ids:
    72→            position_map[issue_id] = idx
    73→
    74→    for item in items:
    75→        item_id = item["id"]
    76→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    77→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    78→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    79→
    80→
    81→def separate_skipped(
    82→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    83→    plan: dict,
    84→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    85→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    86→
    87→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    88→    """
    89→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    90→    if not skipped_ids:
    91→        return items, []
    92→    non_skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    93→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    94→    for item in items:
    95→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    96→            skipped.append(item)
    97→        else:
    98→            non_skipped.append(item)
    99→    return non_skipped, skipped
   100→
   101→
   102→def filter_cluster_focus(
   103→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   104→    plan: dict,
   105→    cluster: str | None,
   106→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   107→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
   109→    if not effective_cluster:
   110→        return items
   111→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   112→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   113→    cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   115→        return items
   116→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   117→
   118→
   119→def stamp_positions(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
   120→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   121→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   122→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   123→
   124→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   125→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   126→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   127→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   128→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   129→            if skip_entry:
   130→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   131→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   132→                if skip_reason:
   133→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   134→
   135→
   136→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   137→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   138→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   139→    if meta:
   140→        return meta.action_type
   141→    return "manual_fix"
   142→
   143→
   144→def _build_cluster_meta(
   145→    cluster_name: str, members: list[WorkQueueItem], cluster_data: dict[str, Any]
   146→) -> WorkQueueItem:
   147→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   148→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   149→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   150→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   151→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   152→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   153→        action_type = "reorganize"
   154→    else:
   155→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   156→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   157→            action_type = "refactor"
   158→
   159→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   160→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   161→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   162→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   163→    else:
   164→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} issues"
   165→
   166→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   167→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   168→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   169→        autofix_hint = action
   170→    else:
   171→        primary_command = action or f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   172→        autofix_hint = None
   173→
   174→    estimated_impact = max(
   175→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   176→    )
   177→
   178→    return {
   179→        "id": cluster_name,
   180→        "kind": "cluster",
   181→        "action_type": action_type,
   182→        "summary": summary,
   183→        "members": members,
   184→        "member_count": len(members),
   185→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   186→        "autofix_hint": autofix_hint,
   187→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   188→        "cluster_auto": bool(cluster_data.get("auto")),
   189→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   190→        "confidence": "high",
   191→        "detector": detector,
   192→        "file": "",
   193→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   194→        "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
   195→    }
   196→
   197→
   198→def collapse_clusters(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   199→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   200→
   201→    Both auto-clusters and manual (triage) clusters are collapsed.  Manual
   202→    clusters are inserted at the front in plan order so triage-prioritised
   203→    work appears before auto-clustered mechanical items.
   204→    """
   205→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   206→    if not clusters:
   207→        return items
   208→
   209→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   210→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   211→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   212→            # Manual clusters take priority when an issue is in both
   213→            if issue_id not in fid_to_cluster or not cluster.get("auto"):
   214→                fid_to_cluster[issue_id] = name
   215→
   216→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   217→        return items
   218→
   219→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   220→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]] = {}
   221→    for item in items:
   222→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   223→        if cname:
   224→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   225→
   226→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   227→    meta_items: dict[str, WorkQueueItem] = {}
   228→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   229→        if len(members) < 2:
   230→            continue
   231→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   232→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   233→        )
   234→
   235→    # Collect manual cluster names in plan order (for front-insertion)
   236→    manual_names = [
   237→        name for name in clusters
   238→        if not clusters[name].get("auto") and name in meta_items
   239→    ]
   240→
   241→    # Walk in order: replace first auto-cluster member with meta-item,
   242→    # skip subsequent members.  Manual cluster members are always skipped
   243→    # (they'll be inserted at the front).
   244→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   245→    rest: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   246→    for item in items:
   247→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   248→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   249→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   250→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   251→                # Auto-clusters collapse in-place; manual clusters go to front
   252→                if clusters.get(cname, {}).get("auto"):
   253→                    rest.append(meta_items[cname])
   254→            # skip individual member
   255→        else:
   256→            rest.append(item)
   257→
   258→    # Manual clusters at the front in plan order, then everything else
   259→    manual_result = [meta_items[name] for name in manual_names if name in seen_clusters]
   260→    return manual_result + rest
   261→
   262→
   263→__all__ = [
   264→    "collapse_clusters",
   265→    "enrich_plan_metadata",
   266→    "filter_cluster_focus",
   267→    "new_item_ids",
   268→    "separate_skipped",
   269→    "stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   270→    "stamp_positions",
   271→]
   272→

    44→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):

    47→    if recon.resurfaced:
    48→        print(
    49→            colorize(

    52→            )
    53→        )
    54→    return bool(recon.changes)

1→"""Tests for plan reconciliation — supersede, prune, cluster desync fix."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_cluster import add_to_cluster, create_cluster
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.reconcile import reconcile_plan_after_scan
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan, ensure_plan_defaults
     8→
     9→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10→# Helpers
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→
    13→def _plan_with_queue(*ids: str) -> dict:
    14→    plan = empty_plan()
    15→    plan["queue_order"] = list(ids)
    16→    return plan
    17→
    18→
    19→def _state_with_issues(*ids: str, status: str = "open") -> dict:
    20→    issues = {}
    21→    for fid in ids:
    22→        issues[fid] = {
    23→            "id": fid,
    24→            "status": status,
    25→            "detector": "test",
    26→            "file": "test.py",
    27→            "tier": 1,
    28→            "confidence": "high",
    29→            "summary": f"Issue {fid}",
    30→        }
    31→    return {"issues": issues, "scan_count": 5}
    32→
    33→
    34→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35→# _supersede_id clears override cluster ref
    36→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    37→
    38→def test_supersede_clears_override_cluster_ref():
    39→    """When a issue is superseded, its override cluster ref should be cleared."""
    40→    plan = _plan_with_queue("a", "b")
    41→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    42→
    43→    # Create a cluster and add issue "a" to it
    44→    create_cluster(plan, "my-cluster")
    45→    add_to_cluster(plan, "my-cluster", ["a"])
    46→
    47→    # Verify override has cluster ref
    48→    assert plan["overrides"]["a"]["cluster"] == "my-cluster"
    49→
    50→    # State where "a" is gone (not present = not alive)
    51→    state = _state_with_issues("b")
    52→
    53→    result = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)
    54→    assert "a" in result.superseded
    55→
    56→    # Override cluster ref should be cleared
    57→    override = plan["overrides"].get("a")
    58→    assert override is not None
    59→    assert override.get("cluster") is None
    60→
    61→
    62→def test_supersede_preserves_note_in_override():
    63→    """Superseding should preserve the note in the override (for history)."""
    64→    plan = _plan_with_queue("a")
    65→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    66→
    67→    # Add an override with a note
    68→    plan["overrides"]["a"] = {
    69→        "issue_id": "a",
    70→        "note": "important context",
    71→        "cluster": None,
    72→        "created_at": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
    73→    }
    74→
    75→    state = _state_with_issues()  # "a" is gone
    76→    reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)
    77→
    78→    # Superseded entry should have the note
    79→    assert plan["superseded"]["a"]["note"] == "important context"
    80→
    81→
    82→def test_supersede_removes_from_cluster_issue_ids():
    83→    """Superseded issue should be removed from cluster issue_ids."""
    84→    plan = _plan_with_queue("a", "b")
    85→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    86→
    87→    create_cluster(plan, "my-cluster")
    88→    add_to_cluster(plan, "my-cluster", ["a", "b"])
    89→
    90→    # "a" disappears
    91→    state = _state_with_issues("b")
    92→    reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)
    93→
    94→    assert "a" not in plan["clusters"]["my-cluster"]["issue_ids"]
    95→    assert "b" in plan["clusters"]["my-cluster"]["issue_ids"]
    96→

   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    35→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    36→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    37→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    38→    target = cleaned.lower()
    39→
    40→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    41→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    42→            return str(dim_key)
    43→    return slugify(cleaned)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    47→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    48→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    49→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    50→    return {
    51→        cleaned.lower(),
    52→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    53→        slugify(cleaned),
    54→        canonical.lower(),
    55→        slugify(canonical),
    56→    }

    81→            aliases.add(key)
    82→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    83→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    84→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
    85→        for alias in aliases:
    86→            scores[alias] = strict_val
    87→    return scores

   100→def _placeholder_dim(key: str) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
   101→    """Return (dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment) for a placeholder."""
   102→    return (
   103→        {
   104→            "score": 0, "strict": 0, "failing": 0, "checks": 0,
   105→            "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": True, "dimension_key": key}},
   106→        },
   107→        {"score": 0, "placeholder": True},
   108→    )
   109→
   110→
   111→def _scored_dim(key: str, score: float) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
   112→    """Return (dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment) with a real score."""
   113→    return (
   114→        {
   115→            "score": score, "strict": score, "failing": 0, "checks": 1,
   116→            "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": False, "dimension_key": key}},
   117→        },
   118→        {"score": score, "placeholder": False},
   119→    )
   120→
   121→
   122→def _apply_dims(state: dict, dims: dict[str, tuple[dict, dict]]) -> None:
   123→    """Write dimension data into state."""
   124→    for key, (dim_entry, assessment) in dims.items():
   125→        state["dimension_scores"][DIM_DISPLAY[key]] = dim_entry
   126→        state["subjective_assessments"][key] = assessment
   127→
   128→
   129→def _save(state: dict, plan: dict, state_dir: Path) -> None:

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle issue churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import get_issue_note
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_meta import append_log_entry
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_skip import resurface_stale_skips
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    SupersededEntry,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    19→    SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES,
    20→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    21→    sync_triage_needed,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    24→
    25→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass
    29→class ReconcileResult:
    30→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    31→
    32→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    34→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    35→    clusters_completed: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    36→    changes: int = 0
    37→
    38→
    39→def _find_candidates(
    40→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    41→) -> list[str]:
    42→    """Find open issues that could be remaps for a disappeared issue."""
    43→    candidates: list[str] = []
    44→    for fid, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items():
    45→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    46→            continue
    47→        if issue.get("detector") == detector and issue.get("file") == file:
    48→            candidates.append(fid)
    49→    return candidates
    50→
    51→
    52→def _is_issue_alive(state: StateModel, issue_id: str) -> bool:
    53→    """Return True if the issue exists and is open."""
    54→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    55→    if issue is None:
    56→        return False
    57→    return issue.get("status") == "open"
    58→
    59→
    60→def _supersede_id(
    61→    plan: PlanModel,
    62→    state: StateModel,
    63→    issue_id: str,
    64→    now: str,
    65→) -> bool:
    66→    """Move a disappeared issue to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    67→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    68→    detector = ""
    69→    file = ""
    70→    summary = ""
    71→    if issue:
    72→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    73→        file = issue.get("file", "")
    74→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    75→
    76→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    77→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    78→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != issue_id]
    79→
    80→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    81→        "original_id": issue_id,
    82→        "original_detector": detector,
    83→        "original_file": file,
    84→        "original_summary": summary,
    85→        "status": "superseded",
    86→        "superseded_at": now,
    87→        "remapped_to": None,
    88→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    89→    }
    90→
    91→    # Preserve any existing override note
    92→    override_note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    93→    if override_note:
    94→        entry["note"] = override_note
    95→
    96→    plan["superseded"][issue_id] = entry
    97→
    98→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster issue_ids
    99→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   100→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   101→    if issue_id in order:
   102→        order.remove(issue_id)
   103→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   104→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, {issue_id})
   105→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   106→        ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   107→        if issue_id in ids:
   108→            ids.remove(issue_id)
   109→
   110→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
   111→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(issue_id)
   112→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   113→        override["cluster"] = None
   114→        override["updated_at"] = now
   115→
   116→    return True
   117→
   118→
   119→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   120→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   121→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   122→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   123→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   124→
   125→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   126→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   127→        try:
   128→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   129→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   130→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   131→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   132→                to_prune.append(fid)
   133→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   134→            to_prune.append(fid)
   135→
   136→    for fid in to_prune:
   137→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   138→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   139→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   140→
   141→    return to_prune
   142→
   143→
   144→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   145→    plan: PlanModel,
   146→    state: StateModel,
   147→) -> ReconcileResult:
   148→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   149→
   150→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   151→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   152→    """
   153→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   154→    result = ReconcileResult()
   155→    now = utc_now()
   156→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   157→
   158→    # Collect all issue IDs referenced by the plan
   159→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   160→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   161→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   162→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   163→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   164→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   165→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   166→
   167→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   168→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   169→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   170→    referenced_ids = {
   171→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   172→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   173→    }
   174→
   175→    # Snapshot non-epic cluster sizes before superseding so we can detect
   176→    # clusters that become empty (all issues resolved by scan).
   177→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   178→    pre_sizes: dict[str, int] = {
   179→        name: len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   180→        for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   181→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   182→    }
   183→
   184→    # Check each referenced ID
   185→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   186→        if not _is_issue_alive(state, fid):
   187→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   188→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   189→                result.changes += 1
   190→
   191→    # Detect manual clusters that just became empty (all issues auto-resolved).
   192→    # Log cluster_done so the plan tracks completion the same way as user resolves.
   193→    for name, prev_size in pre_sizes.items():
   194→        if prev_size == 0:
   195→            continue  # was already empty
   196→        cluster = clusters.get(name)
   197→        if cluster is None:
   198→            continue
   199→        if len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])) == 0:
   200→            result.clusters_completed.append(name)
   201→            append_log_entry(
   202→                plan,
   203→                "cluster_done",
   204→                issue_ids=[],
   205→                cluster_name=name,
   206→                actor="system",
   207→                detail={"reason": "all issues resolved by scan"},
   208→            )
   209→            result.changes += 1
   210→
   211→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead issues, delete empty epics
   212→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   213→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   214→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   215→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   216→            continue
   217→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   218→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if _is_issue_alive(state, fid)]
   219→        if alive_ids != issue_ids:
   220→            cluster["issue_ids"] = alive_ids
   221→            result.changes += 1
   222→        if not alive_ids:
   223→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   224→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   225→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   226→        result.changes += 1
   227→
   228→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   229→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   230→
   231→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   232→    if resurfaced:
   233→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   234→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   235→
   236→    # Prune old superseded entries
   237→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   238→    result.pruned = pruned
   239→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   240→
   241→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   242→    if result.changes > 0:
   243→        append_log_entry(
   244→            plan,
   245→            "reconcile",
   246→            issue_ids=result.superseded,
   247→            actor="system",
   248→            detail={
   249→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   250→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   251→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   252→                "clusters_completed_count": len(result.clusters_completed),
   253→            },
   254→        )
   255→
   256→    return result
   257→
   258→
   259→@dataclass
   260→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
   261→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
   262→
   263→    new_ids: set[str]
   264→    added_to_queue: list[str]
   265→    triage_injected: bool
   266→
   267→
   268→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   269→    plan: PlanModel,
   270→    state: StateModel,
   271→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   272→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   273→
   274→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order and injects triage stages
   275→    if needed. Returns None when there are no new issues to sync.
   276→    """
   277→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   278→    new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   279→    if not new_ids:
   280→        return None
   281→
   282→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   283→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   284→    existing = set(order)
   285→    added: list[str] = []
   286→    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
   287→        if fid not in existing:
   288→            order.append(fid)
   289→            added.append(fid)
   290→
   291→    # Inject triage stages if needed
   292→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   293→    triage_injected = bool(
   294→        triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "injected", False)
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   298→        new_ids=new_ids,
   299→        added_to_queue=added,
   300→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   301→    )
   302→
   303→
   304→__all__ = [
   305→    "ReconcileResult",
   306→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   307→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   308→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   309→]
   310→

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — sync plan queue metadata after a scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
     7→
     8→if TYPE_CHECKING:
     9→    from desloppify.app.commands.scan.workflow import ScanRuntime
    10→
    11→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    14→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    17→    append_log_entry,
    18→    auto_cluster_issues,
    19→    load_plan,
    20→    reconcile_plan_after_scan,
    21→    save_plan,
    22→    sync_communicate_score_needed,
    23→    sync_create_plan_needed,
    24→    sync_stale_dimensions,
    25→    sync_triage_needed,
    26→    sync_unscored_dimensions,
    27→)
    28→
    29→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    30→
    31→
    32→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    33→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    34→    return bool(
    35→        plan.get("queue_order")
    36→        or plan.get("overrides")
    37→        or plan.get("clusters")
    38→        or plan.get("skipped")
    39→    )
    40→
    41→
    42→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    43→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    44→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    45→        return False
    46→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    47→    if recon.resurfaced:
    48→        print(
    49→            colorize(
    50→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    51→                "cyan",
    52→            )
    53→        )
    54→    return bool(recon.changes)
    55→
    56→
    57→def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    58→    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""
    59→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
    60→    if sync.injected:
    61→        print(
    62→            colorize(
    63→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
    64→                "cyan",
    65→            )
    66→        )
    67→    return bool(sync.changes)
    68→
    69→
    70→def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    71→    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
    72→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
    73→    if sync.pruned:
    74→        print(
    75→            colorize(
    76→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
    77→                "cyan",
    78→            )
    79→        )
    80→    if sync.injected:
    81→        print(
    82→            colorize(
    83→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
    84→                "cyan",
    85→            )
    86→        )
    87→    return bool(sync.changes)
    88→
    89→
    90→def _sync_auto_clusters(
    91→    plan: dict[str, object],
    92→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
    93→    *,
    94→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    95→    policy=None,
    96→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
    97→) -> bool:
    98→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
    99→    return bool(auto_cluster_issues(
   100→        plan, state,
   101→        target_strict=target_strict,
   102→        policy=policy,
   103→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   104→    ))
   105→
   106→
   107→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   108→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   109→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   110→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   111→        return False
   112→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   113→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   114→        return False
   115→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   116→    if scores.strict is None:
   117→        return False
   118→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   119→        "strict": scores.strict,
   120→        "overall": scores.overall,
   121→        "objective": scores.objective,
   122→        "verified": scores.verified,
   123→    }
   124→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   125→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   126→    return True
   127→
   128→
   129→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   130→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   131→) -> bool:
   132→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   133→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   134→        return False
   135→
   136→    try:
   137→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   138→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   139→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   140→            score_display_mode,
   141→        )
   142→
   143→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   144→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   145→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   146→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   147→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   148→        return False
   149→    if not queue_empty:
   150→        return False
   151→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   152→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   153→    return True
   154→
   155→
   156→def _subjective_policy_context(
   157→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   158→    plan: dict[str, object],
   159→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   160→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   161→    from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility
   162→
   163→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   164→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   165→        runtime.state,
   166→        target_strict=target_strict,
   167→        plan=plan,
   168→    )
   169→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   170→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed
   171→
   172→
   173→def _sync_unscored_and_log(
   174→    plan: dict[str, object],
   175→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   176→) -> bool:
   177→    changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   178→    if changed:
   179→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   180→    return changed
   181→
   182→
   183→def _sync_stale_and_log(
   184→    plan: dict[str, object],
   185→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   186→    *,
   187→    policy,
   188→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   189→) -> bool:
   190→    changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
   191→        plan,
   192→        state,
   193→        lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
   194→            p,
   195→            s,
   196→            policy=policy,
   197→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   198→        ),
   199→    )
   200→    if changed:
   201→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   202→    return changed
   203→
   204→
   205→def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   206→    plan: dict[str, object],
   207→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   208→    *,
   209→    target_strict: float,
   210→    policy,
   211→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   212→) -> bool:
   213→    changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   214→        plan,
   215→        state,
   216→        target_strict=target_strict,
   217→        policy=policy,
   218→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   219→    )
   220→    if changed:
   221→        append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   222→    return changed
   223→
   224→
   225→def _sync_triage_and_log(
   226→    plan: dict[str, object],
   227→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   228→) -> bool:
   229→    triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   230→    if not triage_sync.changes:
   231→        return False
   232→    if triage_sync.injected:
   233→        print(
   234→            colorize(
   235→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review issues changed since last triage.",
   236→                "cyan",
   237→            )
   238→        )
   239→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   240→    return True
   241→
   242→
   243→def _sync_communicate_score_and_log(
   244→    plan: dict[str, object],
   245→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   246→    *,
   247→    policy,
   248→) -> bool:
   249→    communicate_sync = sync_communicate_score_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   250→    if not communicate_sync.changes:
   251→        return False
   252→    append_log_entry(
   253→        plan,
   254→        "sync_communicate_score",
   255→        actor="system",
   256→        detail={"injected": True},
   257→    )
   258→    return True
   259→
   260→
   261→def _sync_create_plan_and_log(
   262→    plan: dict[str, object],
   263→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   264→    *,
   265→    policy,
   266→) -> bool:
   267→    create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   268→    if not create_plan_sync.changes:
   269→        return False
   270→    if create_plan_sync.injected:
   271→        print(
   272→            colorize(
   273→                "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   274→                "cyan",
   275→            )
   276→        )
   277→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   278→    return True
   279→
   280→
   281→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   282→    plan: dict[str, object],
   283→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   284→) -> bool:
   285→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   286→    if seeded:
   287→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   288→        return True
   289→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   290→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   291→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   292→    if cleared:
   293→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   294→    return cleared
   295→
   296→
   297→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   298→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   299→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   300→    try:
   301→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   302→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   303→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   304→        return
   305→    dirty = False
   306→
   307→    if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   308→        dirty = True
   309→
   310→    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   311→        dirty = True
   312→
   313→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   314→        runtime,
   315→        plan,
   316→    )
   317→    if _sync_stale_and_log(
   318→        plan,
   319→        runtime.state,
   320→        policy=policy,
   321→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   322→    ):
   323→        dirty = True
   324→
   325→    if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   326→        plan,
   327→        runtime.state,
   328→        target_strict=target_strict,
   329→        policy=policy,
   330→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   331→    ):
   332→        dirty = True
   333→
   334→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   335→        dirty = True
   336→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   337→        dirty = True
   338→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   339→        dirty = True
   340→    if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   341→        dirty = True
   342→
   343→    if dirty:
   344→        try:
   345→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   346→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   347→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   348→

1→"""Tests for stale and unscored subjective dimension sync in the plan."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.reconcile import reconcile_plan_after_scan
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (

     9→    sync_unscored_dimensions,
    10→)
    11→
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→# Helpers
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→
    16→def _plan_with_queue(*ids: str) -> dict:
    17→    plan = empty_plan()
    18→    plan["queue_order"] = list(ids)
    19→    return plan
    20→
    21→
    22→def _state_with_stale_dimensions(*dim_keys: str, score: float = 50.0) -> dict:
    23→    """Build a minimal state with stale subjective dimensions."""
    24→    dim_scores: dict = {}
    25→    assessments: dict = {}
    26→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
    27→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
    28→            "score": score,
    29→            "strict": score,
    30→            "checks": 1,
    31→            "failing": 0,
    32→            "detectors": {
    33→                "subjective_assessment": {
    34→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
    35→                    "placeholder": False,
    36→                }
    37→            },
    38→        }
    39→        assessments[dim_key] = {
    40→            "score": score,
    41→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
    42→            "refresh_reason": "mechanical_issues_changed",
    43→            "stale_since": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
    44→        }
    45→    return {
    46→        "issues": {},
    47→        "scan_count": 5,
    48→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
    49→        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
    50→    }
    51→
    52→
    53→def _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*dim_keys: str) -> dict:
    54→    """Build a minimal state with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions."""
    55→    dim_scores: dict = {}
    56→    assessments: dict = {}
    57→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
    58→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
    59→            "score": 0,
    60→            "strict": 0,
    61→            "checks": 1,
    62→            "failing": 0,
    63→            "detectors": {
    64→                "subjective_assessment": {
    65→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
    66→                    "placeholder": True,
    67→                }
    68→            },
    69→        }
    70→        assessments[dim_key] = {
    71→            "score": 0.0,
    72→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
    73→            "placeholder": True,
    74→        }
    75→    return {
    76→        "issues": {},
    77→        "scan_count": 1,
    78→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
    79→        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
    80→    }
    81→
    82→
    83→def _state_with_mixed_dimensions(
    84→    unscored: list[str],
    85→    stale: list[str],
    86→) -> dict:
    87→    """Build a state with both unscored and stale dimensions."""
    88→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*unscored)
    89→    stale_state = _state_with_stale_dimensions(*stale)
    90→    state["dimension_scores"].update(stale_state["dimension_scores"])
    91→    state["subjective_assessments"].update(stale_state["subjective_assessments"])
    92→    return state
    93→
    94→
    95→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    96→# Unscored dimension sync
    97→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    98→
    99→def test_unscored_injected_at_back():
   100→    """Unscored IDs are appended after existing items."""
   101→    plan = _plan_with_queue("some_issue::file.py::abc123")
   102→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   103→

   107→    assert plan["queue_order"][0] == "some_issue::file.py::abc123"
   108→    assert plan["queue_order"][1].startswith("subjective::")
   109→    assert plan["queue_order"][2].startswith("subjective::")

   117→    )

   120→    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)

   122→    assert plan["queue_order"][-1] == "subjective::design_coherence"

   124→
   125→
   126→def test_unscored_pruned_after_review():

   138→    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in plan["queue_order"]
   139→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" not in plan["queue_order"]
   140→
   141→

   147→    )
   148→    state = _state_with_mixed_dimensions(
   149→        unscored=["design_coherence"],
   150→        stale=["error_consistency"],

   291→    assert result.pruned == []
   292→
   293→
   294→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   295→# Cleanup: prune resolved stale IDs
   296→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   297→
   298→def test_prunes_resolved_dimension_ids():
   299→    plan = _plan_with_queue(
   300→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   301→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   302→    )
   303→    # Only design_coherence is still stale; error_consistency was refreshed
   304→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence")
   305→
   306→    result = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   307→    assert result.pruned == ["subjective::error_consistency"]
   308→    assert plan["queue_order"] == ["subjective::design_coherence"]
   309→
   310→
   311→def test_prune_does_not_touch_real_issue_ids():
   312→    plan = _plan_with_queue(
   313→        "structural::file.py::abc123",
   314→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   315→    )
   316→    # design_coherence is no longer stale
   317→    state = {"issues": {}, "scan_count": 5, "dimension_scores": {}}
   318→
   319→    result = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   320→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in result.pruned
   321→    assert plan["queue_order"] == ["structural::file.py::abc123"]
   322→
   323→
   324→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   325→# Full lifecycle: inject → refresh → prune → re-inject
   326→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   327→
   328→def test_full_lifecycle():
   329→    plan = _plan_with_queue()
   330→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   331→
   332→    # 1. Empty queue, stale dims → inject both
   333→    r1 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   334→    assert len(r1.injected) == 2
   335→    assert plan["queue_order"] == [
   336→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   337→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   338→    ]
   339→
   340→    # 2. User refreshes design_coherence (no longer stale, but still under target)
   341→    state["subjective_assessments"]["design_coherence"]["needs_review_refresh"] = False
   342→
   343→    r2 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   344→    # Not pruned: still under target (score=50)
   345→    assert r2.pruned == []
   346→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in plan["queue_order"]
   347→    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in plan["queue_order"]
   348→
   349→    # 3. User raises both scores above target → queue empties

### **Bottom Line**

This module is a **"gateway" between the plan queue lifecycle and subjective dimension policy**. It bundles:

- **7 independent sync orchestrators** (each handles a distinct queue gate)
- **3 independent result dataclass families** (dimension sync, workflow gates)
- **5 ID/constant groups** (all controlling queue item visibility)

The file grew because it centralizes all "sync" logic. **Safe restructuring candidates**:

1. **Split dimension sync from workflow gates** — move `sync_create_plan_needed()`, `sync_triage_needed()`, `sync_score_checkpoint_needed()`, `sync_import_scores_needed()`, `sync_communicate_score_needed()` + their result types + constants into a separate `workflow_gates.py` or `workflow_sync.py`
2. **Move ID helpers into `stale_policy.py`** — they already live in stale_policy, just re-exported here as thin wrappers
3. **Keep dimension sync functions together** — `sync_stale_dimensions()`, `sync_unscored_dimensions()` are tightly coupled

All consumers use the `engine/plan.py` facade, so import reshuffling is internal — **no public API breakage risk**.

## Analysis Summary: Eviction Logic in `sync_stale_dimensions`

Based on my thorough code review, I can now answer all your questions about the eviction logic and whether it's still needed. Here's what I found:

### 1. **Subjective Item Lifecycle in the Queue**

The lifecycle follows this path:

```
unscored → scored (stale) → refreshed → under-target → (eventually) target-met
```

Queue presence changes at each stage:

- **Unscored phase** (new dimensions): `sync_unscored_dimensions` injects ALL unscored IDs unconditionally to the back of the queue. They stay in the queue regardless of objective backlog. (`stale_dimensions.py:212-242`)

- **Scored→Stale transition**: When a dimension transitions from unscored to scored but needs refresh (stale), `sync_stale_dimensions` handles it:
  - If no objective backlog: inject to back
  - If objective backlog exists: **EVICT from queue** (lines 282-294)
  
- **Refreshed but under-target**: Treated same as stale—conditional on backlog
  
- **Target met**: `sync_stale_dimensions` prunes these from queue (lines 276)

### 2. **Is Eviction Actually Needed?**

**YES, but only if queue-level filtering is the sole gating mechanism.** Here's the key insight:

The work queue builder already has **display-time lifecycle filtering** (`_apply_lifecycle_filter` in `core.py:297-327`) that naturally hides stale/under-target subjective items when objective work exists:

```python
def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    """Phase 3: Objective work remains → show objectives, hide endgame-only subjective."""
    if not _has_objective_items(items):
        return items  # endgame: everything visible
    return [i for i in items if not _is_endgame_only(i)]  # filters stale subjectives

def _is_endgame_only(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    """True if stale/under-target subjective (initial_review=False)."""
    return (
        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
        and not item.get("initial_review")
    )
```

The `initial_review` flag is set in `synthetic.py:226-257` based on whether the item is unassessed (placeholder or unreviewed). **Stale and under-target items will always have `initial_review=False`**, so they are filtered at display time.

### 3. **SubjectiveVisibility Policy Doesn't Gate Display**

The `SubjectiveVisibility` dataclass in `subjective_policy.py` has methods `should_inject_to_plan()` and `should_evict_from_plan()` (lines 34-50), but these are **only called during plan reconciliation** (`_sync_stale_and_log` in `plan_reconcile.py:183-202`), **not at queue-build time**. The queue builder doesn't use `SubjectiveVisibility` to filter—it relies entirely on the lifecycle filter.

### 4. **Queue Display/Next Logic Doesn't Check Plan State**

Tracing `next` command → `build_work_queue()` in `core.py:88-142`:
- Never checks `plan["queue_order"]` to filter items
- Never reads `SubjectiveVisibility` 
- Never applies early queue-level eviction
- Items that survive the pipeline reach `_apply_lifecycle_filter`, which is the ONLY gate that hides endgame items

### 5. **Does Eviction Happen Every Scan?**

YES—critically important: **Every scan calls `reconcile_plan_post_scan()`** (in `plan_reconcile.py:297-348`), which calls `_sync_stale_and_log()` (lines 317-323). This calls `sync_stale_dimensions()` with the current `SubjectiveVisibility` policy. 

**The eviction happens mid-cycle on every scan**, meaning:
- If objective backlog exists: stale items are removed from `queue_order`
- If objective backlog cleared: stale items are re-appended to `queue_order`

This causes repeated bounce-in/bounce-out during mid-cycle work.

### Key Findings

| Question | Answer |
|----------|--------|
| **Do stale items get filtered at display time?** | YES—`_is_endgame_only()` filters them when objective work exists |
| **Is eviction queue-level filtering redundant?** | YES—the lifecycle filter already hides them from `next` output |
| **Would removing eviction break anything?** | **Potentially YES** if callers read `queue_order` directly without applying the lifecycle filter |
| **Eviction happens every scan?** | YES—`reconcile_plan_post_scan()` runs post-scan, triggering re-sync |
| **Would queue positions be more stable without eviction?** | YES—IDs would stay at appended positions instead of bouncing |

### Safe Path Forward

To remove eviction safely, you would need to:

1. **Verify all queue consumers** check the lifecycle filter or the `initial_review` flag
   - `build_work_queue()` ✓ has lifecycle filter
   - `next` command ✓ calls `build_work_queue()`
   - Plan displays ✓ read from work queue output
   - Any direct `queue_order` reads ❓ (check `queue_progress.py`, plan display code)

2. **Change eviction logic** from "remove stale IDs from queue_order when objective backlog exists" to "leave them in queue_order always; they'll be filtered at display time"

3. **Keep the "always append to back" principle** intact—no reordering

The eviction block (lines 282-294 in `stale_dimensions.py`) could be **removed entirely** and replaced with a comment explaining that display-time filtering handles endgame gating. However, first audit any code that reads `queue_order` directly rather than through the work queue builder.

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import enum
     6→import importlib
     7→import logging
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    10→
    11→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    13→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    14→from desloppify.engine import plan as plan_mod
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue import core as work_queue_core_mod
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import is_subjective_queue_item
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    18→
    19→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    20→
    21→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    22→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
    23→
    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    30→class QueueBreakdown:
    31→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    32→
    33→    queue_total: int = 0
    34→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    35→    skipped: int = 0
    36→    subjective: int = 0
    37→    workflow: int = 0
    38→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    39→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    40→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    41→
    42→    @property
    43→    def objective_actionable(self) -> int:
    44→        """Objective items only — excludes subjective and workflow.
    45→
    46→        This is the count used by :func:`score_display_mode` to decide
    47→        frozen vs live display.  Do not use it directly for gating —
    48→        use ``score_display_mode()`` instead.
    49→        """
    50→        return max(0, self.queue_total - self.subjective - self.workflow)
    51→
    52→
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→# ScoreDisplayMode — single decision point for frozen vs live score display
    55→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    56→
    57→class ScoreDisplayMode(enum.Enum):
    58→    """How to display the score given the current plan cycle and queue state.
    59→
    60→    - ``FROZEN``: objective work remains → show frozen plan-start score.
    61→    - ``PHASE_TRANSITION``: objective drained, subjective/workflow remain
    62→      → show live scores + transition banner.
    63→    - ``LIVE``: no active plan cycle or queue fully clear → show live scores.
    64→    """
    65→    FROZEN = "frozen"
    66→    PHASE_TRANSITION = "phase_transition"
    67→    LIVE = "live"
    68→
    69→
    70→def score_display_mode(
    71→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
    72→    plan_start_strict: float | None,
    73→) -> ScoreDisplayMode:
    74→    """Determine the score display mode from breakdown + plan-start score.
    75→
    76→    This is the **single source of truth** for the frozen-vs-live decision.
    77→    Every call site (status, scan reveal, next nudges, plan reconcile, plan
    78→    nudge) must use this instead of ad-hoc count checks.
    79→    """
    80→    if plan_start_strict is None:
    81→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
    82→    if breakdown is None:
    83→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
    84→    if breakdown.objective_actionable > 0:
    85→        return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
    86→    if breakdown.queue_total > 0:
    87→        return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
    88→    return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
    89→
    90→
    91→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    92→    state: dict,
    93→    plan: dict | None = None,
    94→    *,
    95→    context: QueueContext | None = None,
    96→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    97→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call.
    98→
    99→    When *context* is provided, its ``plan`` and ``policy`` are forwarded to
   100→    ``build_work_queue`` so the counts agree with the caller's resolution.
   101→    """
   102→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan
   103→
   104→    result = work_queue_core_mod.build_work_queue(
   105→        state,
   106→        options=work_queue_core_mod.QueueBuildOptions(
   107→            status="open",
   108→            count=None,
   109→            plan=effective_plan if context is None else None,
   110→            include_skipped=False,
   111→            context=context,
   112→        ),
   113→    )
   114→
   115→    # Collapse clusters for display-level counting
   116→    items = result.get("items", [])
   117→    if effective_plan and not effective_plan.get("active_cluster"):
   118→        items = collapse_clusters(items, effective_plan)
   119→
   120→    queue_total = len(items)
   121→
   122→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
   123→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
   124→    subjective = sum(
   125→        1 for item in items
   126→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
   127→    )
   128→    workflow = sum(
   129→        1 for item in items
   130→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    # Plan-derived counts
   134→    plan_ordered = 0
   135→    skipped = 0
   136→    if effective_plan:
   137→        skipped = len(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}))
   138→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
   139→        queue_order = effective_plan.get("queue_order", [])
   140→        skipped_ids = set(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   141→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
   142→
   143→    # Focus cluster info
   144→    focus_cluster = None
   145→    focus_cluster_count = 0
   146→    focus_cluster_total = 0
   147→    if effective_plan:
   148→        active = effective_plan.get("active_cluster")
   149→        if active:
   150→            focus_cluster = active
   151→            cluster_data = effective_plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
   152→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   153→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
   154→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   155→            open_issues = {
   156→                fid
   157→                for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   158→                if f.get("status") == "open"
   159→            }
   160→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_issues)
   161→
   162→    return QueueBreakdown(
   163→        queue_total=queue_total,
   164→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
   165→        skipped=skipped,
   166→        subjective=subjective,
   167→        workflow=workflow,
   168→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
   169→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
   170→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→
   174→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   175→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   176→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   177→
   178→def format_plan_delta(live: float, frozen: float) -> str:
   179→    """Format plan-start vs live delta, or '' if below threshold."""
   180→    if abs(live - frozen) < 0.05:
   181→        return ""
   182→    delta = round(live - frozen, 1)
   183→    return f"{'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f}"
   184→
   185→
   186→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   187→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   188→
   189→    Examples::
   190→
   191→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   192→        Queue: 1934 items
   193→    """
   194→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   195→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   196→
   197→    # Parenthesized segments
   198→    segments: list[str] = []
   199→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   200→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   201→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   202→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   203→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   204→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   205→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   206→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   207→    if segments:
   208→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   209→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   210→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   211→    return label
   212→
   213→
   214→def format_queue_block(
   215→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   216→    *,
   217→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   218→    live_score: float | None = None,
   219→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   220→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   221→
   222→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   223→    """
   224→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   225→
   226→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   227→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   228→        focus_line = (
   229→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   230→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   231→            f" items remaining"
   232→        )
   233→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   234→
   235→    # Score line: show both frozen plan-start and live score when available
   236→    if frozen_score is not None:
   237→        delta_str = format_plan_delta(live_score, frozen_score) if live_score is not None else ""
   238→        if delta_str:
   239→            lines.append((
   240→                f"  Score: strict {live_score:.1f}/100 (plan start: {frozen_score:.1f}, {delta_str})",
   241→                "cyan",
   242→            ))
   243→        else:
   244→            lines.append((
   245→                f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   246→                "cyan",
   247→            ))
   248→
   249→    # Queue headline — always the same
   250→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   251→
   252→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   253→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   254→        lines.append((
   255→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   256→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   257→            "dim",
   258→        ))
   259→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   260→        lines.append((
   261→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   262→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   263→            "dim",
   264→        ))
   265→    else:
   266→        lines.append((
   267→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   268→            "dim",
   269→        ))
   270→
   271→    return lines
   272→
   273→
   274→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   275→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   276→    if not plan:
   277→        return None
   278→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   279→
   280→
   281→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   282→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   283→    *,
   284→    frozen_strict: float,
   285→    live_score: float | None = None,
   286→) -> None:
   287→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   288→
   289→    Only call from a ``ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN`` path — assumes objective
   290→    work remains in the queue.
   291→    """
   292→    block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=frozen_strict, live_score=live_score)
   293→    print()
   294→    for text, style in block:
   295→        print(colorize(text, style))
   296→    print(colorize(
   297→        "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   298→        "dim",
   299→    ))
   300→
   301→
   302→def print_objective_drained_banner(
   303→    frozen_strict: float,
   304→    remaining: int,
   305→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   306→) -> None:
   307→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   308→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   309→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   310→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   311→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   312→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   313→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   314→    print(colorize(
   315→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   316→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   317→        "cyan",
   318→    ))
   319→    print(colorize(
   320→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   321→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   322→        "dim",
   323→    ))
   324→
   325→
   326→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   327→    state: dict,
   328→    prev,
   329→    plan: dict | None,
   330→) -> None:
   331→    """Context-aware score display using :func:`score_display_mode`."""
   332→    frozen_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
   333→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None
   334→
   335→    if frozen_strict is not None:
   336→        try:
   337→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   338→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   339→            _logger.debug("queue breakdown computation skipped", exc_info=True)
   340→
   341→    mode = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict)
   342→
   343→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN:
   344→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   345→            breakdown,
   346→            frozen_strict=frozen_strict,
   347→            live_score=state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict,
   348→        )
   349→        return
   350→
   351→    # LIVE or PHASE_TRANSITION: show current scores
   352→    score_update_mod = importlib.import_module(
   353→        "desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update"
   354→    )
   355→    score_update_mod.print_score_update(state, prev)
   356→
   357→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION:
   358→        print_objective_drained_banner(frozen_strict, breakdown.queue_total, breakdown)
   359→
   360→
   361→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   362→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   363→
   364→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   365→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   366→    """
   367→    try:
   368→        plan = plan_mod.load_plan()
   369→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   370→        plan = None
   371→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   372→
   373→
   374→__all__ = [
   375→    "QueueBreakdown",
   376→    "ScoreDisplayMode",
   377→    "format_plan_delta",

   379→    "format_queue_headline",
