1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — append never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *back* of the queue unconditionally.
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→
    10→Invariant: new items are always appended — sync never reorders existing queue.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan import stale_policy as stale_policy_mod
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    20→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    21→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    24→
    25→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    26→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    27→
    28→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    29→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    30→    "triage::observe",
    31→    "triage::reflect",
    32→    "triage::organize",
    33→    "triage::enrich",
    34→    "triage::sense-check",
    35→    "triage::commit",
    36→)
    37→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    38→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    39→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    40→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    41→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    42→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    43→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    44→
    45→
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# Result dataclasses
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→@dataclass
    51→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    52→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    53→
    54→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    56→
    57→    @property
    58→    def changes(self) -> int:
    59→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    60→
    61→
    62→@dataclass
    63→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    64→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    65→
    66→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    67→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    68→
    69→    @property
    70→    def changes(self) -> int:
    71→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    72→
    73→
    74→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    75→# ID helpers
    76→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    77→
    78→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    79→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    80→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    81→        state,
    82→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    83→    )
    84→
    85→
    86→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    87→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    88→
    89→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    90→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    91→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    92→    """
    93→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    94→        state,
    95→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    96→    )
    97→
    98→
    99→def current_under_target_ids(
   100→    state: StateModel,
   101→    *,
   102→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   103→) -> set[str]:
   104→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   105→
   106→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   107→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   108→    """
   109→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   110→        state,
   111→        target_strict=target_strict,
   112→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   113→    )
   114→
   115→
   116→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→# Helpers for sync_stale_dimensions / sync_unscored_dimensions
   118→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→
   120→def _has_objective_backlog(
   121→    state: StateModel,
   122→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   123→) -> bool:
   124→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   125→    if policy is not None:
   126→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   127→    return any(
   128→        f.get("status") == "open"
   129→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   130→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   131→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   132→    )
   133→
   134→
   135→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   136→    order: list[str],
   137→    *,
   138→    keep_ids: set[str],
   139→    pruned: list[str],
   140→) -> None:
   141→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   142→    to_remove = [
   143→        fid for fid in order
   144→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   145→        and fid not in keep_ids
   146→    ]
   147→    for fid in to_remove:
   148→        order.remove(fid)
   149→        pruned.append(fid)
   150→
   151→
   152→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   153→    order: list[str],
   154→    *,
   155→    inject_ids: set[str],
   156→    injected: list[str],
   157→) -> None:
   158→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   159→
   160→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   161→    """
   162→    existing = set(order)
   163→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   164→        if sid not in existing:
   165→            order.append(sid)
   166→            injected.append(sid)
   167→
   168→
   169→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   170→# Helpers for sync_triage_needed
   171→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   172→
   173→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
   174→    state: StateModel,
   175→    meta: dict,
   176→) -> set[str]:
   177→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
   178→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   179→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids
   180→
   181→
   182→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   183→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   184→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   185→        while sid in order:
   186→            order.remove(sid)
   187→
   188→
   189→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   190→    order: list[str],
   191→    confirmed: set[str],
   192→) -> bool:
   193→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   194→
   195→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   196→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   197→    """
   198→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
   199→    existing = set(order)
   200→    injected_count = 0
   201→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   202→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   203→            order.append(sid)
   204→            injected_count += 1
   205→    return injected_count > 0
   206→
   207→
   208→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   209→# Unscored dimension sync (back of queue, unconditional)
   210→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   211→
   212→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   213→    plan: PlanModel,
   214→    state: StateModel,
   215→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   216→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   217→
   218→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   219→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   220→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   221→    2. **Inject** — append currently-unscored IDs to the *back* of
   222→       ``queue_order``.  Never reorders existing items.
   223→    """
   224→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   225→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   226→
   227→    # Mid-cycle: don't inject unscored dimensions — they'll surface at cycle end.
   228→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   229→        return result
   230→
   231→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   232→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   233→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   234→
   235→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   236→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   237→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   238→
   239→    # --- Inject: append unscored IDs to back of queue ---------------------
   240→    _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=unscored_ids, injected=result.injected)
   241→
   242→    return result
   243→
   244→
   245→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   246→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   247→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   248→
   249→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   250→    plan: PlanModel,
   251→    state: StateModel,
   252→    *,
   253→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   254→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   255→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   256→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   257→
   258→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   259→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   260→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   261→    2. Append stale and under-target dimension IDs to the *back* when either:
   262→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle), OR
   263→       b. A cycle just completed.
   264→       Never reorders existing items.
   265→    """
   266→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   267→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   268→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   269→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   270→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   271→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   272→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   273→
   274→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   275→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   276→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   277→
   278→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   279→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   280→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   281→
   282→    if not should_inject:
   283→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   284→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   285→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   286→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   287→        to_evict = [
   288→            fid for fid in order
   289→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   290→            and fid in injectable_ids
   291→        ]
   292→        for fid in to_evict:
   293→            order.remove(fid)
   294→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   295→
   296→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   297→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   298→
   299→    return result
   300→
   301→
   302→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   303→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   304→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   305→
   306→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   307→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   308→
   309→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   310→    """
   311→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   312→
   313→
   314→@dataclass
   315→class TriageSyncResult:
   316→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   317→
   318→    injected: bool = False
   319→    pruned: bool = False
   320→
   321→    @property
   322→    def changes(self) -> int:
   323→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   324→
   325→
   326→def sync_triage_needed(
   327→    plan: PlanModel,
   328→    state: StateModel,
   329→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   330→    """Append triage stage IDs to back of queue when review issues change.
   331→
   332→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   333→
   334→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   335→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   336→
   337→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   338→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   339→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   340→    """
   341→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   342→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   343→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   344→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   345→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   346→
   347→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   348→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   349→
   350→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   351→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   352→
   353→    if already_present:
   354→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   355→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   356→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   357→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   358→        # user-started triage session.
   359→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   360→            new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   361→
   362→            if not new_since_triage:
   363→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   364→                _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   365→                if current_hash:
   366→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   367→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   368→                result.pruned = True
   369→        return result
   370→
   371→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   372→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   373→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   374→        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   375→
   376→        if new_since_triage:
   377→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   378→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, confirmed)
   379→        else:
   380→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   381→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   382→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   383→
   384→    return result
   385→
   386→
   387→@dataclass
   388→class ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   389→    """What changed during a score-checkpoint sync."""
   390→
   391→    injected: bool = False
   392→
   393→    @property
   394→    def changes(self) -> int:
   395→        return int(self.injected)
   396→
   397→
   398→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   399→    plan: PlanModel,
   400→    state: StateModel,
   401→    *,
   402→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   403→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   404→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   405→
   406→    Injects when:
   407→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   408→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   409→
   410→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   411→    """
   412→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   413→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   414→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   415→
   416→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   417→        return result
   418→
   419→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   420→    if policy is not None:
   421→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   422→            return result
   423→    else:
   424→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   425→        if unscored:
   426→            return result
   427→
   428→    order.append(WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   429→    result.injected = True
   430→    return result
   431→
   432→
   433→@dataclass
   434→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   435→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   436→
   437→    injected: bool = False
   438→
   439→    @property
   440→    def changes(self) -> int:
   441→        return int(self.injected)
   442→
   443→
   444→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   445→    plan: PlanModel,
   446→    state: StateModel,
   447→    *,
   448→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   449→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   450→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   451→
   452→    Only injects when:
   453→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   454→    - At least one objective issue exists
   455→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   456→    - No triage stages are pending
   457→
   458→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   459→    """
   460→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   461→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   462→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   463→
   464→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   465→        return result
   466→
   467→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   468→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   469→        return result
   470→
   471→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   472→    if policy is not None:
   473→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   474→            return result
   475→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   476→    else:
   477→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   478→        if unscored:
   479→            return result
   480→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   481→        has_objective = any(
   482→            f.get("status") == "open"
   483→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   484→            for f in issues.values()
   485→        )
   486→    if not has_objective:
   487→        return result
   488→
   489→    order.append(WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   490→    result.injected = True
   491→    return result
   492→
   493→
   494→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   495→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   496→
   497→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   498→    """
   499→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   500→
   501→
   502→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   503→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   504→
   505→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   506→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   507→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   508→    resolved since then.
   509→
   510→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   511→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   512→    through the plan.
   513→    """
   514→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   515→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state)
   516→

   518→@dataclass

   520→    """What changed during an import-scores sync."""

   528→
   529→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   530→    plan: PlanModel,
   531→    state: StateModel,
   532→    *,
   533→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   534→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   535→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   536→
   537→    Only injects when:
   538→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   539→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   540→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   541→
   542→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   543→    """
   544→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   545→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   546→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   547→
   548→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   549→        return result
   550→
   551→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   552→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   553→        return result
   554→
   555→    order.append(WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   556→    result.injected = True
   557→    return result
   558→
   559→
   560→@dataclass
   561→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   562→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   563→
   564→    injected: bool = False
   565→
   566→    @property
   567→    def changes(self) -> int:
   568→        return int(self.injected)
   569→
   570→
   571→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   572→    plan: PlanModel,
   573→    state: StateModel,
   574→    *,
   575→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   576→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   577→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   578→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   579→
   580→    Injects when either:
   581→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   582→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   583→
   584→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   585→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   586→    """
   587→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   588→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   589→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   590→
   591→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   592→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   593→        return result
   594→
   595→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   596→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   597→
   598→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   599→    if not should_inject:
   600→        if policy is not None:
   601→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   602→        else:
   603→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   604→
   605→    if not should_inject:
   606→        return result
   607→
   608→    order.append(WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   609→    result.injected = True
   610→    return result
   611→

1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import (
     9→    get_issue_description,
    10→    get_issue_note,
    11→    get_issue_override,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    14→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    18→    """Return issue IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    19→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    20→    if not scan_history:
    21→        return set()
    22→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    23→    if not threshold:
    24→        return set()
    25→    return {
    26→        issue_id
    27→        for issue_id, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    28→        if issue.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    29→    }
    30→
    31→
    32→def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
    33→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    34→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    35→
    36→    for item in items:
    37→        issue_id = item["id"]
    38→        description = get_issue_description(plan, issue_id)
    39→        note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    40→        override = get_issue_override(plan, issue_id)
    41→        if description:
    42→            item["plan_description"] = description
    43→        if note:
    44→            item["plan_note"] = note
    45→        if override.get("cluster"):
    46→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    47→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    48→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    49→                "name": cluster_name,
    50→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    51→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", [])),
    52→                "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
    53→            }
    54→
    55→
    56→def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    plan: dict,
    59→    new_ids: set[str],
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    62→
    63→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    64→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    65→    """
    66→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    67→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    68→
    69→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    70→    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    71→        if issue_id not in skipped_ids:
    72→            position_map[issue_id] = idx
    73→
    74→    for item in items:
    75→        item_id = item["id"]
    76→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    77→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    78→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    79→
    80→
    81→def separate_skipped(
    82→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    83→    plan: dict,
    84→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    85→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    86→
    87→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    88→    """
    89→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    90→    if not skipped_ids:
    91→        return items, []
    92→    non_skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    93→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    94→    for item in items:
    95→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    96→            skipped.append(item)
    97→        else:
    98→            non_skipped.append(item)
    99→    return non_skipped, skipped
   100→
   101→
   102→def filter_cluster_focus(
   103→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   104→    plan: dict,
   105→    cluster: str | None,
   106→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   107→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
   109→    if not effective_cluster:
   110→        return items
   111→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   112→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   113→    cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   115→        return items
   116→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   117→
   118→
   119→def stamp_positions(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
   120→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   121→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   122→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   123→
   124→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   125→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   126→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   127→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   128→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   129→            if skip_entry:
   130→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   131→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   132→                if skip_reason:
   133→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   134→
   135→
   136→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   137→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   138→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   139→    if meta:
   140→        return meta.action_type
   141→    return "manual_fix"
   142→
   143→
   144→def _build_cluster_meta(
   145→    cluster_name: str, members: list[WorkQueueItem], cluster_data: dict[str, Any]
   146→) -> WorkQueueItem:
   147→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   148→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   149→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   150→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   151→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   152→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   153→        action_type = "reorganize"
   154→    else:
   155→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   156→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   157→            action_type = "refactor"
   158→
   159→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   160→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   161→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   162→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   163→    else:
   164→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} issues"
   165→
   166→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   167→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   168→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   169→        autofix_hint = action
   170→    else:
   171→        primary_command = action or f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   172→        autofix_hint = None
   173→
   174→    estimated_impact = max(
   175→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   176→    )
   177→
   178→    return {
   179→        "id": cluster_name,
   180→        "kind": "cluster",
   181→        "action_type": action_type,
   182→        "summary": summary,
   183→        "members": members,
   184→        "member_count": len(members),
   185→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   186→        "autofix_hint": autofix_hint,
   187→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   188→        "cluster_auto": bool(cluster_data.get("auto")),
   189→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   190→        "confidence": "high",
   191→        "detector": detector,
   192→        "file": "",
   193→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   194→        "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
   195→    }
   196→
   197→
   198→def collapse_clusters(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   199→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   200→
   201→    Both auto-clusters and manual (triage) clusters are collapsed.  Manual
   202→    clusters are inserted at the front in plan order so triage-prioritised
   203→    work appears before auto-clustered mechanical items.
   204→    """
   205→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   206→    if not clusters:
   207→        return items
   208→
   209→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   210→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   211→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   212→            # Manual clusters take priority when an issue is in both
   213→            if issue_id not in fid_to_cluster or not cluster.get("auto"):
   214→                fid_to_cluster[issue_id] = name
   215→
   216→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   217→        return items
   218→
   219→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   220→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]] = {}
   221→    for item in items:
   222→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   223→        if cname:
   224→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   225→
   226→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   227→    meta_items: dict[str, WorkQueueItem] = {}
   228→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   229→        if len(members) < 2:
   230→            continue
   231→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   232→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   233→        )
   234→
   235→    # Collect manual cluster names in plan order (for front-insertion)
   236→    manual_names = [
   237→        name for name in clusters
   238→        if not clusters[name].get("auto") and name in meta_items
   239→    ]
   240→
   241→    # Walk in order: replace first auto-cluster member with meta-item,
   242→    # skip subsequent members.  Manual cluster members are always skipped
   243→    # (they'll be inserted at the front).
   244→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   245→    rest: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   246→    for item in items:
   247→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   248→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   249→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   250→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   251→                # Auto-clusters collapse in-place; manual clusters go to front
   252→                if clusters.get(cname, {}).get("auto"):
   253→                    rest.append(meta_items[cname])
   254→            # skip individual member
   255→        else:
   256→            rest.append(item)
   257→
   258→    # Manual clusters at the front in plan order, then everything else
   259→    manual_result = [meta_items[name] for name in manual_names if name in seen_clusters]
   260→    return manual_result + rest
   261→
   262→
   263→__all__ = [
   264→    "collapse_clusters",
   265→    "enrich_plan_metadata",
   266→    "filter_cluster_focus",
   267→    "new_item_ids",
   268→    "separate_skipped",
   269→    "stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   270→    "stamp_positions",
   271→]
   272→

1→"""Queue mutation helpers for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import add_promoted_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     7→
     8→
     9→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str) -> None:
    10→    """Remove a issue ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    11→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    12→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    13→    if issue_id in order:
    14→        order.remove(issue_id)
    15→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _resolve_position(
    19→    order: list[str],
    20→    position: str,
    21→    target: str | None = None,
    22→    offset: int | None = None,
    23→    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    24→) -> int:
    25→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    26→
    27→    *issue_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    28→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    29→    """
    30→    moving = set(issue_ids or [])
    31→
    32→    if position == "top":
    33→        return 0
    34→    if position == "bottom":
    35→        return len(order)
    36→
    37→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    38→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    39→
    40→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    41→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    42→            order,
    43→            moving,
    44→            position=position,
    45→            offset=offset,
    46→            issue_ids=issue_ids,
    47→        )
    48→
    49→    return len(order)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _resolve_relative_position(
    53→    order: list[str],
    54→    moving: set[str],
    55→    *,
    56→    position: str,
    57→    target: str,
    58→) -> int:
    59→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    60→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    61→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
    62→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
    63→
    64→
    65→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
    66→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
    67→        if item_id == target:
    68→            return i
    69→    return None
    70→
    71→
    72→def _resolve_offset_position(
    73→    order: list[str],
    74→    moving: set[str],
    75→    *,
    76→    position: str,
    77→    offset: int,
    78→    issue_ids: list[str] | None,
    79→) -> int:
    80→    if not issue_ids:
    81→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
    82→    first_id = issue_ids[0]
    83→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    84→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
    85→    if current_idx is None:
    86→        if position == "up":
    87→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    88→        return len(clean_order)
    89→    if position == "up":
    90→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    91→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    92→
    93→
    94→def move_items(
    95→    plan: PlanModel,
    96→    issue_ids: list[str],
    97→    position: str,
    98→    target: str | None = None,
    99→    offset: int | None = None,
   100→) -> int:

    88→def build_work_queue(
    89→    state: StateModel,
    90→    *,
    91→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    92→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    93→    """Build a ranked work queue from state issues.
    94→
    95→    Pipeline:
    96→    1. Gather    — issue items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    97→    2. Score     — estimate impact from dimension headroom
    98→    3. Presort   — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
    99→    4. Lifecycle — filter endgame-only items when objective work remains
   100→    5. Sort      — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
   101→    6. Limit     — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   102→    """
   103→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   104→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   105→
   106→    # 1. Gather
   107→    items = build_issue_items(
   108→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   109→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   110→    )
   111→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
   112→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   113→
   114→    # 2. Score
   115→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   116→
   117→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering (part 1: separate skipped items)
   118→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   119→
   120→    # 4. Lifecycle filter — endgame-only items filtered when objective work remains
   121→    items = _apply_lifecycle_filter(items)
   122→
   123→    # 5. Sort & plan post-processing
   124→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   125→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   126→
   127→    # 6. Finalize
   128→    if not items:
   129→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   130→    total = len(items)
   131→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   132→        items = items[:opts.count]
   133→    if opts.explain:
   134→        for item in items:
   135→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   136→
   137→    return {
   138→        "items": items,
   139→        "total": total,
   140→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   141→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   142→    }
   143→
   144→
   145→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   146→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   147→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   148→
   149→
   150→def _resolve_inputs(
   151→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   152→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   153→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   154→    ctx = opts.context
   155→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   156→
   157→    scan_path: str | None = (
   158→        state.get("scan_path")
   159→        if opts.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   160→        else opts.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   161→    )
   162→
   163→    status = opts.status
   164→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   165→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   166→
   167→    try:
   168→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   169→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   170→        threshold = 100.0
   171→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   172→
   173→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   174→
   175→
   176→def _gather_subjective_items(
   177→    state: StateModel,
   178→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   179→    threshold: float,
   180→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   181→    """Build subjective dimension candidates.
   182→
   183→    Lifecycle filtering (endgame gating) happens in _apply_lifecycle_filter,
   184→    not here. This function only handles configuration and scope.

   188→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:

   190→    if opts.chronic:

   192→
   193→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   194→        state, state.get("issues", {}), threshold=threshold,
   195→    )
   196→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]

docs/DEVELOPMENT_PHILOSOPHY.md:50:1. **Initial reviews** — Unscored subjective dimensions. The lifecycle filter blocks everything else until all placeholder dimensions are scored.
docs/DEVELOPMENT_PHILOSOPHY.md-51-2. **Communicate score** — `workflow::communicate-score` is injected by `reconcile_plan_post_scan` once all initial reviews are done. Shows the user their first strict score.
docs/DEVELOPMENT_PHILOSOPHY.md-52-3. **Create plan** — `workflow::create-plan` is injected when reviews are complete, objective backlog exists, and no triage is pending.
docs/DEVELOPMENT_PHILOSOPHY.md-53-4. **Triage** — 4 stages (`triage::observe` → `reflect` → `organize` → `commit`) injected when the review-issue snapshot hash changes (new `review`/`concerns` detector issues appear).
--

250→    view-layer concern that callers apply after building the canonical queue.
   251→    This prevents UI focus state from affecting lifecycle decisions (scan
   252→    gating, score display mode, empty-queue fallback).
   253→    """
   254→    if not plan:
   255→        return
   256→
   257→    if opts.include_skipped:
   258→        items.extend(skipped)
   259→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
   260→
   261→
   262→def _has_objective_items(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   263→    """True if any objective mechanical work items remain in the queue."""
   264→    return any(
   265→        i.get("kind") == "issue"
   266→        and i.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   267→        for i in items
   268→    )
   269→
   270→
   271→def _has_initial_reviews(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   272→    """True if any unassessed subjective dimensions need initial review."""
   273→    return any(
   274→        i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   275→        and i.get("initial_review")
   276→        for i in items
   277→    )
   278→
   279→
   280→def _is_endgame_only(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   281→    """True if this item should only appear when the objective queue is drained."""
   282→    return (
   283→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   284→        and not item.get("initial_review")
   285→    )
   286→
   287→
   288→def _has_triage_stages(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   289→    """True if any pending triage stage items are in the queue."""
   290→    return any(
   291→        i.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
   292→        and str(i.get("id", "")).startswith("triage::")
   293→        for i in items
   294→    )
   295→
   296→
   297→def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   298→    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules.
   299→
   300→    The queue enforces a phase order: scan → initial review → triage → objective work.
   301→
   302→    1. **Initial reviews pending** → only show initial-review subjective items.
   303→       The user must complete first-time reviews before working objective items.
   304→    2. **Triage in progress** → only show triage stages and workflow items.
   305→       Cluster work items aren't ready until triage completes (enrich adds
   306→       the detail that makes steps actionable).
   307→    3. **Objective work remains** → show objective items, hide endgame-only
   308→       subjective reassessments (stale re-reviews).
   309→    4. **Objective drained** → everything visible (endgame).
   310→    """
   311→    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
   312→        # Phase 1: only initial reviews visible — triage and workflow
   313→        # items stay hidden until initial reviews are complete.
   314→        return [
   315→            i for i in items
   316→            if i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and i.get("initial_review")
   317→        ]
   318→    if _has_triage_stages(items):
   319→        # Phase 2: triage in progress — only triage stages and workflow items visible.
   320→        # Cluster work items aren't ready until enrich completes.
   321→        return [
   322→            i for i in items
   323→            if i.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
   324→        ]
   325→    if not _has_objective_items(items):
   326→        return items  # endgame: everything visible
   327→    return [i for i in items if not _is_endgame_only(i)]
   328→
   329→

1→"""Lifecycle and reset mutations for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    empty_plan,
    10→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    13→
    14→
    15→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
    16→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
    17→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    18→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
    19→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
    20→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
    21→
    22→
    23→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    24→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
    25→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    26→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
    27→
    28→
    29→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    30→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp.
    31→
    32→    Sets ``plan_start_scores`` to a sentinel so the next scan seeds real
    33→    scores instead of incorrectly treating the reset as a completed cycle.
    34→    """
    35→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
    36→    plan.clear()
    37→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
    38→        plan[k] = v
    39→    plan["created"] = created
    40→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {"reset": True}
    41→
    42→
    43→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> int:
    44→    """Remove issue IDs from the plan entirely.
    45→
    46→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
    47→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
    48→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
    49→    """
    50→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    51→    found = 0
    52→
    53→    purge_set = set(issue_ids)
    54→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, purge_set)
    55→
    56→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    57→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    58→    for fid in issue_ids:
    59→        was_present = False
    60→        if fid in order:
    61→            order.remove(fid)
    62→            was_present = True
    63→        if fid in skipped:
    64→            skipped.pop(fid)
    65→            was_present = True
    66→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    67→            ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
    68→            if fid in ids:
    69→                ids.remove(fid)
    70→                was_present = True
    71→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
    72→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
    73→            override["cluster"] = None
    74→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
    75→        if was_present:
    76→            found += 1
    77→
    78→    return found
    79→
    80→

   272→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   273→
   274→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order and injects triage stages
   275→    if needed. Returns None when there are no new issues to sync.
   276→    """
   277→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   278→    new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   279→    if not new_ids:
   280→        return None
   281→
   282→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   283→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   284→    existing = set(order)
   285→    added: list[str] = []
   286→    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
   287→        if fid not in existing:
   288→            order.append(fid)
   289→            added.append(fid)
   290→
   291→    # Inject triage stages if needed
   292→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   293→    triage_injected = bool(
   294→        triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "injected", False)
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   298→        new_ids=new_ids,
   299→        added_to_queue=added,
   300→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   301→    )

    25→
    26→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    27→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    28→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    29→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    30→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    31→_UNDER_TARGET_KEY = "subjective::under-target"
    32→_UNDER_TARGET_NAME = "auto/under-target-review"
    33→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    34→
    35→
    36→def _manual_member_ids(clusters: dict) -> set[str]:
    37→    """Collect all issue IDs belonging to manual (non-auto) clusters."""
    38→    ids: set[str] = set()
    39→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    40→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    41→            ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    42→    return ids
    43→
    44→
    45→def _group_clusterable_issues(
    46→    issues: dict,
    47→    *,
    48→    manual_member_ids: set[str],
    49→) -> tuple[dict[str, list[str]], dict[str, dict]]:
    50→    """Group open, non-suppressed, non-manual issues by detector/subtype key.
    51→
    52→    Returns (groups_by_key, issue_data) where groups_by_key maps grouping keys
    53→    to lists of issue IDs, filtered to clusters >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE.
    54→    """
    55→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    56→    issue_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
    57→    for fid, issue in issues.items():
    58→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    59→            continue
    60→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
    61→            continue
    62→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
    63→            continue
    64→
    65→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    66→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    67→        key = _grouping_key(issue, meta)
    68→        if key is None:
    69→            continue
    70→
    71→        groups[key].append(fid)
    72→        issue_data[fid] = issue
    73→
    74→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
    75→    return groups, issue_data
    76→
    77→
    78→def _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
    79→    plan: dict,
    80→    *,
    81→    clusters: dict,
    82→    existing_name: str,
    83→    member_ids: list[str],
    84→    now: str,
    85→) -> int:
    86→    """Sync member IDs for a user-modified cluster, returns count of changes."""
    87→    cluster = clusters[existing_name]
    88→    changes = 0
    89→    existing_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    90→    new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
    91→    if new_ids:
    92→        cluster["issue_ids"].extend(new_ids)
    93→        cluster["updated_at"] = now
    94→        changes = 1
    95→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    96→    for fid in member_ids:
    97→        if fid not in overrides:
    98→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
    99→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = existing_name
   100→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now

   299→
   300→    The queue enforces a phase order: scan → initial review → triage → objective work.
   301→

   303→       The user must complete first-time reviews before working objective items.
   304→    2. **Triage in progress** → only show triage stages and workflow items.
   305→       Cluster work items aren't ready until triage completes (enrich adds
   306→       the detail that makes steps actionable).
   307→    3. **Objective work remains** → show objective items, hide endgame-only
   308→       subjective reassessments (stale re-reviews).
   309→    4. **Objective drained** → everything visible (endgame).
   310→    """

   312→        # Phase 1: only initial reviews visible — triage and workflow
   313→        # items stay hidden until initial reviews are complete.
   314→        return [
   315→            i for i in items
   316→            if i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and i.get("initial_review")

   324→        ]

Perfect! Now I have the complete picture. Let me create a comprehensive summary document of my findings.

## Summary: Queue Order Direct Access Analysis

I've conducted a thorough search of the codebase for all code that reads `queue_order` from the plan dict. Here are my findings:

### CRITICAL FINDING: Safe to Remove Eviction Logic

**Removing the eviction logic from `sync_stale_dimensions` is SAFE.** No code path reads `queue_order` directly expecting stale subjective items to already be evicted.

### Key Architecture Facts

1. **Lifecycle Filter is the Single Source of Truth**
   - `_apply_lifecycle_filter()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` (lines 297-327) is executed in `build_work_queue()` step 4 (line 121)
   - This filter removes endgame-only subjective items (stale re-reviews) whenever objective items remain (line 327)
   - The filter operates on the WORK QUEUE ITEMS, not the plan dict

2. **All User-Facing Code Uses `build_work_queue()`**
   All code that displays or counts queue items goes through `build_work_queue()` which applies the lifecycle filter:
   
   - **`plan_aware_queue_breakdown()`** (queue_progress.py:91-171)
     - Called by status/scan output (print_execution_or_reveal)
     - Gets items from `build_work_queue()` (line 104)
     - Line 139: counts `plan_ordered` by iterating plan["queue_order"] directly, BUT this count is only used for display metadata, not queue membership
   
   - **`cmd_plan_queue()`** (plan/queue_render.py:178+)
     - Gets items from `build_work_queue()` (line 198)
     - Line 50-52: reads plan["queue_order"] only to count plan_ordered for the header display
     - Items shown to user come from `build_work_queue()` which has already filtered
   
   - **`cmd_next()`** and all other commands
     - Route through `build_work_queue()` which applies lifecycle filter

### Direct Queue Order Reads (All Safe)

These locations read `queue_order` directly but don't assume eviction has happened:

| File | Usage | Safe? |
|------|-------|-------|
| queue_progress.py:139 | Count plan_ordered for display metadata | YES - counting only, not membership check |
| plan/queue_render.py:50-52 | Count plan_ordered for header display | YES - counting only, not membership check |
| planning/render.py:223 | Check if plan has any queue metadata | YES - simple existence check |
| _plan/persistence.py:134 | Check if plan is non-empty | YES - simple existence check |
| app/commands/helpers/rendering.py:27,97 | Check if plan has any queue metadata | YES - simple existence checks |
| scan/artifacts.py:79 | Check if plan is active | YES - simple existence check |
| scan/plan_reconcile.py:35 | Check if plan is active | YES - simple existence check |
| scan/reporting/agent_context.py:379 | Build plan snapshot for narrative | YES - reads raw queue_order for display (lifecycle filter not applied here, but that's intentional for agent context) |
| scan/reporting/integrity_report.py:88 | Check if plan has items | YES - simple existence check |
| status/render_io.py:14 | Check if plan has items | YES - simple existence check |

### Plan Mutation Code (Direct Access to queue_order)

These functions mutate `queue_order` directly and are the ONLY places where stale items are currently managed:

- **`sync_stale_dimensions()`** (stale_dimensions.py:249-299)
  - Lines 272-294: Mutates `plan["queue_order"]` directly
  - Currently evicts stale subjective IDs when `has_real_items=True` (lines 287-294)
  - **This eviction is redundant** because `_apply_lifecycle_filter()` will filter them anyway during queue building

- **`sync_unscored_dimensions()`** (stale_dimensions.py:212-242)
  - Lines 233-240: Manages unscored IDs in queue_order

- Other sync functions (`sync_triage_needed`, `sync_create_plan_needed`, etc.)
  - Append/prune IDs from queue_order but don't assume eviction

### Key Insight: Two Separate Filtering Mechanisms

1. **Plan-level filtering** (stale_dimensions.py eviction): Currently removes subjective IDs from the plan dict's queue_order when objective backlog exists
2. **Lifecycle filtering** (_apply_lifecycle_filter): Removes endgame-only items from the items list during queue building when objective items exist

The code currently has BOTH mechanisms, making the first one redundant. The lifecycle filter is the authoritative one because:
- It operates on built queue items, not the persistent plan dict
- It considers the actual items present (not just presence of objective backlog)
- All display code goes through it
- It's the documented design (see DEVELOPMENT_PHILOSOPHY.md:50)

### Affected Files When Removing Eviction Logic

No files need to be updated beyond `stale_dimensions.py` because:
- `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` counts `plan_ordered` for display only, doesn't filter by it
- Queue display reads from `build_work_queue()` output, not plan["queue_order"]
- All lifecycle gating happens in `_apply_lifecycle_filter()`
- No code assumes items in queue_order are actually visible to the user

**Conclusion: Safe to remove the eviction block (lines 282-294) from `sync_stale_dimensions()`.**

> DEVELOPER

Design a refactor plan for `desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`. This file has grown into a 645-line dumping ground for all queue sync logic. Here's the current state and what needs fixing:

## Current Problems

1. **File does too much** — named `stale_dimensions.py` but contains 7 sync functions, 7 result dataclasses, triage logic, workflow gates, constants, and ID helpers. Module docstring says "Two independent sync functions" but there are seven.

2. **Seven nearly-identical result dataclasses** — `StaleDimensionSyncResult`, `UnscoredDimensionSyncResult`, `TriageSyncResult`, `ScoreCheckpointSyncResult`, `CreatePlanSyncResult`, `ImportScoresSyncResult`, `CommunicateScoreSyncResult`. Consumers only check `.changes` and `.injected`/`.pruned` existence/length. Some use `list[str]`, others `bool`.

3. **Redundant eviction logic** in `sync_stale_dimensions` (lines 282-294) — removes stale subjective items from queue mid-cycle when objective backlog exists, then re-appends them when backlog clears. This causes position instability. The work queue builder's `_apply_lifecycle_filter()` in `engine/_work_queue/core.py` already handles this at display time. All user-facing code goes through `build_work_queue()`. Direct `queue_order` readers only do existence/count checks. The eviction is confirmed redundant and safe to remove.

4. **Thin wrapper ID helpers** — `_current_stale_ids()`, `current_unscored_ids()`, `current_under_target_ids()` are one-liners that call `stale_policy_mod.*` with `subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX` hardcoded. Three functions that add nothing.

5. **Four workflow sync functions are structurally identical** — `sync_score_checkpoint_needed`, `sync_create_plan_needed`, `sync_import_scores_needed`, `sync_communicate_score_needed`. Each: check if present → check precondition → `order.append(ID)`. Could be one parameterized function.

## Consumer Map

All sync functions are re-exported via `engine/plan.py` facade. Key consumers:

- `app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py` — calls all sync functions, inspects `.injected`/`.pruned`/`.changes` on results
- `engine/_plan/reconcile.py` — uses `sync_triage_needed()`, `compute_new_issue_ids()`, `SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES`
- `engine/_plan/auto_cluster_sync.py` — uses ID helpers + `SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX`
- `engine/_work_queue/synthetic.py` — uses `TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS`, `TRIAGE_IDS`
- `engine/_work_queue/synthetic_workflow.py` — uses workflow ID constants (lazy imports)
- `engine/_plan/operations_queue.py` — uses `TRIAGE_IDS`
- `engine/_plan/schema_migrations.py` — uses `TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS`, `WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID`
- 15+ test files — various constants and sync functions

## Natural Cohesion Clusters

1. **Dimension sync** — `sync_stale_dimensions`, `sync_unscored_dimensions`, their helpers (`_prune_subjective_ids`, `_inject_subjective_ids`, `_has_objective_backlog`), `SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX`
2. **Triage sync** — `sync_triage_needed`, `_new_review_ids_since_triage`, `_prune_all_triage_stages`, `_inject_pending_triage_stages`, `review_issue_snapshot_hash`, `is_triage_stale`, `compute_new_issue_ids`, `TRIAGE_*` constants
3. **Workflow gates** — `sync_score_checkpoint_needed`, `sync_create_plan_needed`, `sync_import_scores_needed`, `sync_communicate_score_needed`, `WORKFLOW_*` constants

## Constraints

- The `engine/plan.py` facade re-exports 23 symbols from this file. Updating the facade is fine (internal tool, no backward compat needed per CLAUDE.md).
- `SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES` is used by `reconcile.py` — needs to stay accessible.
- Tests import directly from `stale_dimensions` — they'll need updating if the module splits.
- Keep changes minimal and testable — don't over-engineer.

## Design Questions

1. How should the file be split? What are the right module names and boundaries?
2. Should the result dataclasses be unified into one or two generic types?
3. Should the four workflow sync functions be collapsed into one parameterized function? If so, how do you handle the varying preconditions (some check unscored, some check triage, some check objective backlog)?
4. Should the ID helpers be inlined or moved to stale_policy?
5. What's the simplest way to handle the SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES constant that depends on all three prefix constants?

   135→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   136→    order: list[str],
   137→    *,
   138→    keep_ids: set[str],
   139→    pruned: list[str],
   140→) -> None:
   141→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   142→    to_remove = [
   143→        fid for fid in order
   144→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   145→        and fid not in keep_ids
   146→    ]
   147→    for fid in to_remove:
   148→        order.remove(fid)
   149→        pruned.append(fid)

   152→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   153→    order: list[str],
   154→    *,
   155→    inject_ids: set[str],
   156→    injected: list[str],
   157→) -> None:
   158→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   159→
   160→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   161→    """
   162→    existing = set(order)
   163→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   164→        if sid not in existing:
   165→            order.append(sid)
   166→            injected.append(sid)

   182→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   183→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   184→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:

   189→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   190→    order: list[str],
   191→    confirmed: set[str],
   192→) -> bool:
   193→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   194→
   195→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   196→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   197→    """
   198→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
   199→    existing = set(order)
   200→    injected_count = 0
   201→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   202→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   203→            order.append(sid)
   204→            injected_count += 1
   205→    return injected_count > 0

   208→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   209→# Unscored dimension sync (back of queue, unconditional)
   210→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   211→
   212→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   213→    plan: PlanModel,
   214→    state: StateModel,
   215→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   216→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   217→
   218→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   219→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   220→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   221→    2. **Inject** — append currently-unscored IDs to the *back* of
   222→       ``queue_order``.  Never reorders existing items.
   223→    """
   224→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   225→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   226→
   227→    # Mid-cycle: don't inject unscored dimensions — they'll surface at cycle end.
   228→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   229→        return result
   230→
   231→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   232→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   233→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   234→
   235→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   236→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   237→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   238→
   239→    # --- Inject: append unscored IDs to back of queue ---------------------
   240→    _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=unscored_ids, injected=result.injected)
   241→
   242→    return result
   243→
   244→
   245→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   246→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   247→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   248→
   249→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   250→    plan: PlanModel,
   251→    state: StateModel,
   252→    *,
   253→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   254→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   255→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   256→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   257→
   258→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   259→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   260→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   261→    2. Append stale and under-target dimension IDs to the *back* when either:
   262→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle), OR
   263→       b. A cycle just completed.
   264→       Never reorders existing items.
   265→    """
   266→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   267→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   268→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   269→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   270→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   271→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   272→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   273→
   274→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   275→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   276→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   277→
   278→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   279→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   280→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   281→
   282→    if not should_inject:
   283→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   284→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   285→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   286→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   287→        to_evict = [
   288→            fid for fid in order
   289→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   290→            and fid in injectable_ids
   291→        ]
   292→        for fid in to_evict:
   293→            order.remove(fid)
   294→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   295→
   296→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   297→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   298→
   299→    return result
   300→

   303→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   304→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   305→
   306→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   307→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   308→
   309→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   310→    """
   311→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)

   313→
   314→@dataclass
   315→class TriageSyncResult:
   316→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   317→
   318→    injected: bool = False
   319→    pruned: bool = False
   320→
   321→    @property
   322→    def changes(self) -> int:
   323→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   324→
   325→
   326→def sync_triage_needed(
   327→    plan: PlanModel,
   328→    state: StateModel,
   329→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   330→    """Append triage stage IDs to back of queue when review issues change.
   331→
   332→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   333→
   334→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   335→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   336→
   337→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   338→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   339→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   340→    """
   341→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   342→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   343→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   344→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   345→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   346→
   347→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   348→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   349→
   350→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   351→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   352→
   353→    if already_present:
   354→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   355→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   356→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   357→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   358→        # user-started triage session.
   359→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   360→            new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   361→
   362→            if not new_since_triage:
   363→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   364→                _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   365→                if current_hash:
   366→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   367→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   368→                result.pruned = True
   369→        return result
   370→
   371→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   372→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   373→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   374→        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   375→
   376→        if new_since_triage:
   377→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   378→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, confirmed)
   379→        else:
   380→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   381→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   382→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   383→
   384→    return result
   385→

   398→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(

   401→    *,
   402→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   403→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   404→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   405→
   406→    Injects when:
   407→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   408→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   409→
   410→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   411→    """
   412→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   413→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   414→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   415→
   416→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   417→        return result

   419→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   420→    if policy is not None:
   421→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   422→            return result

   425→        if unscored:
   426→            return result
   427→
   428→    order.append(WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   429→    result.injected = True
   430→    return result

   444→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   445→    plan: PlanModel,
   446→    state: StateModel,
   447→    *,
   448→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   449→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   450→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   451→
   452→    Only injects when:
   453→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   454→    - At least one objective issue exists
   455→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   456→    - No triage stages are pending
   457→
   458→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   459→    """
   460→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   461→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   462→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   463→
   464→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   465→        return result
   466→
   467→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   468→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   469→        return result
   470→
   471→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   472→    if policy is not None:
   473→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   474→            return result
   475→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   476→    else:
   477→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   478→        if unscored:
   479→            return result
   480→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   481→        has_objective = any(
   482→            f.get("status") == "open"
   483→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   484→            for f in issues.values()
   485→        )
   486→    if not has_objective:
   487→        return result
   488→
   489→    order.append(WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   490→    result.injected = True
   491→    return result

   495→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   496→

   498→    """
   499→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)

   502→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   503→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   504→

   506→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   507→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   508→    resolved since then.
   509→
   510→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   511→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   512→    through the plan.
   513→    """
   514→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   515→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state)
