```
   537→    Only injects when:
   538→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   539→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   540→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   541→
   542→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   543→    """
   544→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   545→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   546→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   547→
   548→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   549→        return result
   550→
   551→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   552→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   553→        return result
   554→
   555→    order.append(WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   556→    result.injected = True
   557→    return result
   558→
   559→
   560→@dataclass
   561→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   562→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   563→
   564→    injected: bool = False
   565→
   566→    @property
   567→    def changes(self) -> int:
   568→        return int(self.injected)
   569→
   570→
   571→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   572→    plan: PlanModel,
   573→    state: StateModel,
   574→    *,
   575→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   576→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   577→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   578→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   579→
   580→    Injects when either:
   581→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   582→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   583→
   584→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   585→    Appended to back of queue.  Never reorders existing items.
   586→    """
   587→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   588→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   589→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   590→
   591→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   592→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   593→        return result
   594→
   595→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   596→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   597→
   598→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   599→    if not should_inject:
   600→        if policy is not None:
   601→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   602→        else:
   603→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   604→
   605→    if not should_inject:
   606→        return result
   607→
   608→    order.append(WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   609→    result.injected = True
   610→    return result
   611→
   612→
   613→__all__ = [
   614→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   615→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   616→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   617→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   618→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   619→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   620→    "WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID",
   621→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   622→    "WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID",
   623→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
```

```
   173→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
   174→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
   175→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   176→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   177→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   178→    if not has_triage:
   179→        return ""
   180→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   181→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   182→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check") if s in stages]
   183→    if completed:
   184→        return (
   185→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/5 stages complete) — "
   186→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   187→        )
   188→    return (
   189→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   190→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   191→    )
   192→
```

```
1→"""Pure policy helpers for stale/unscored subjective planning decisions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import all_subjective_entries
    11→
    12→_REVIEW_DETECTORS = ("review", "concerns")
    13→
    14→
    15→def open_review_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    16→    """Return IDs of open review/concerns issues from state."""
    17→    return {
    18→        fid
    19→        for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    20→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def current_stale_ids(
    25→    state: StateModel,
    26→    *,
    27→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    28→) -> set[str]:
    29→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    30→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    31→    if not dim_scores:
    32→        return set()
    33→
    34→    stale: set[str] = set()
    35→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    36→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    37→            continue
    38→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    39→        if dim_key:
    40→            stale.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    41→    return stale
    42→
    43→
    44→def current_unscored_ids(
    45→    state: StateModel,
    46→    *,
    47→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    48→) -> set[str]:
    49→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored."""
    50→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    51→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
    52→        unscored: set[str] = set()
    53→        for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    54→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    55→                continue
    56→            if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    57→                continue
    58→            if dim_key:
    59→                unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    60→        return unscored
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    unscored = set()
    67→    for data in dim_scores.values():
    68→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
    69→            continue
    70→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    71→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
    72→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
    73→            continue
    74→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
    75→            continue
    76→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
    77→        if dim_key:
    78→            unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    79→    return unscored
    80→
    81→
    82→def current_under_target_ids(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    86→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    87→) -> set[str]:
    88→    """Return under-target subjective IDs that are neither stale nor unscored."""
    89→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    90→    if not dim_scores:
    91→        return set()
    92→
    93→    stale_ids = current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    94→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    95→
    96→    under_target: set[str] = set()
    97→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    98→        if entry.get("placeholder") or entry.get("stale"):
    99→            continue
   100→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   101→        if strict_val >= target_strict:
   102→            continue
   103→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
   104→        if not dim_key:
   105→            continue
   106→        item_id = f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}"
   107→        if item_id not in stale_ids and item_id not in unscored_ids:
   108→            under_target.add(item_id)
   109→    return under_target
   110→
   111→
   112→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   113→    """Hash open review/concerns issue IDs to detect triage-relevant changes."""
   114→    review_ids = sorted(open_review_ids(state))
   115→    if not review_ids:
   116→        return ""
   117→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   118→
   119→
   120→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   121→    """Return open review/concerns IDs that appeared since the last triage."""
   122→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   123→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   124→    return open_review_ids(state) - triaged if triaged else set()
   125→
   126→
   127→def is_triage_stale(
   128→    plan: dict,
   129→    state: StateModel,
   130→) -> bool:
   131→    """Return True when genuinely new review issues appeared since last triage.
   132→
   133→    In-progress triage (confirmed stages + stage IDs in queue) is NOT
   134→    considered stale — the lifecycle filter in the work queue already
   135→    forces triage stages to the front.
   136→    """
   137→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   138→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   139→    return bool(open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids)
   140→
   141→
   142→__all__ = [
   143→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   144→    "current_stale_ids",
   145→    "current_under_target_ids",
   146→    "current_unscored_ids",
   147→    "is_triage_stale",
   148→    "open_review_ids",
   149→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   150→]
   151→
```
```

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle issue churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import get_issue_note
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_meta import append_log_entry
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_skip import resurface_stale_skips
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    SupersededEntry,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    19→    SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES,
    20→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    21→    sync_triage_needed,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    24→
    25→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass
    29→class ReconcileResult:
    30→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    31→
    32→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    34→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    35→    clusters_completed: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    36→    changes: int = 0
    37→
    38→
    39→def _find_candidates(
    40→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    41→) -> list[str]:
    42→    """Find open issues that could be remaps for a disappeared issue."""
    43→    candidates: list[str] = []
    44→    for fid, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items():
    45→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    46→            continue
    47→        if issue.get("detector") == detector and issue.get("file") == file:
    48→            candidates.append(fid)
    49→    return candidates
    50→
    51→
    52→def _is_issue_alive(state: StateModel, issue_id: str) -> bool:
    53→    """Return True if the issue exists and is open."""
    54→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    55→    if issue is None:
    56→        return False
    57→    return issue.get("status") == "open"
    58→
    59→
    60→def _supersede_id(
    61→    plan: PlanModel,
    62→    state: StateModel,
    63→    issue_id: str,
    64→    now: str,
    65→) -> bool:
    66→    """Move a disappeared issue to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    67→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    68→    detector = ""
    69→    file = ""
    70→    summary = ""
    71→    if issue:
    72→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    73→        file = issue.get("file", "")
    74→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    75→
    76→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    77→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    78→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != issue_id]
    79→
    80→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    81→        "original_id": issue_id,
    82→        "original_detector": detector,
    83→        "original_file": file,
    84→        "original_summary": summary,
    85→        "status": "superseded",
    86→        "superseded_at": now,
    87→        "remapped_to": None,
    88→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    89→    }
    90→
    91→    # Preserve any existing override note
    92→    override_note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    93→    if override_note:
    94→        entry["note"] = override_note
    95→
    96→    plan["superseded"][issue_id] = entry
    97→
    98→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster issue_ids
    99→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   100→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   101→    if issue_id in order:
   102→        order.remove(issue_id)
   103→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   104→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, {issue_id})
   105→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   106→        ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   107→        if issue_id in ids:
   108→            ids.remove(issue_id)
   109→
   110→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
   111→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(issue_id)
   112→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   113→        override["cluster"] = None
   114→        override["updated_at"] = now
   115→
   116→    return True
   117→
   118→
   119→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   120→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   121→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   122→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   123→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   124→
   125→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   126→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   127→        try:
   128→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   129→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   130→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   131→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   132→                to_prune.append(fid)
   133→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   134→            to_prune.append(fid)
   135→
   136→    for fid in to_prune:
   137→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   138→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   139→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   140→
   141→    return to_prune
   142→
   143→
   144→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   145→    plan: PlanModel,
   146→    state: StateModel,
   147→) -> ReconcileResult:
   148→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   149→
   150→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   151→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   152→    """
   153→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   154→    result = ReconcileResult()
   155→    now = utc_now()
   156→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   157→
   158→    # Collect all issue IDs referenced by the plan
   159→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   160→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   161→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   162→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   163→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   164→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   165→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   166→
   167→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   168→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   169→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   170→    referenced_ids = {
   171→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   172→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   173→    }
   174→
   175→    # Snapshot non-epic cluster sizes before superseding so we can detect
   176→    # clusters that become empty (all issues resolved by scan).
   177→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   178→    pre_sizes: dict[str, int] = {
   179→        name: len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   180→        for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   181→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   182→    }
   183→
   184→    # Check each referenced ID
   185→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   186→        if not _is_issue_alive(state, fid):
   187→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   188→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   189→                result.changes += 1
   190→
   191→    # Detect manual clusters that just became empty (all issues auto-resolved).
   192→    # Log cluster_done so the plan tracks completion the same way as user resolves.
   193→    for name, prev_size in pre_sizes.items():
   194→        if prev_size == 0:
   195→            continue  # was already empty
   196→        cluster = clusters.get(name)
   197→        if cluster is None:
   198→            continue
   199→        if len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])) == 0:
   200→            result.clusters_completed.append(name)
   201→            append_log_entry(
   202→                plan,
   203→                "cluster_done",
   204→                issue_ids=[],
   205→                cluster_name=name,
   206→                actor="system",
   207→                detail={"reason": "all issues resolved by scan"},
   208→            )
   209→            result.changes += 1
   210→
   211→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead issues, delete empty epics
   212→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   213→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   214→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   215→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   216→            continue
   217→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   218→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if _is_issue_alive(state, fid)]
   219→        if alive_ids != issue_ids:
   220→            cluster["issue_ids"] = alive_ids
   221→            result.changes += 1
   222→        if not alive_ids:
   223→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   224→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   225→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   226→        result.changes += 1
   227→
   228→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   229→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   230→
   231→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   232→    if resurfaced:
   233→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   234→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   235→
   236→    # Prune old superseded entries
   237→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   238→    result.pruned = pruned
   239→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   240→
   241→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   242→    if result.changes > 0:
   243→        append_log_entry(
   244→            plan,
   245→            "reconcile",
   246→            issue_ids=result.superseded,
   247→            actor="system",
   248→            detail={
   249→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   250→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   251→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   252→                "clusters_completed_count": len(result.clusters_completed),
   253→            },
   254→        )
   255→
   256→    return result
   257→
   258→
   259→@dataclass
   260→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
   261→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
   262→
   263→    new_ids: set[str]
   264→    added_to_queue: list[str]
   265→    triage_injected: bool
   266→
   267→
   268→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   269→    plan: PlanModel,
   270→    state: StateModel,
   271→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   272→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   273→
   274→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order and injects triage stages
   275→    if needed. Returns None when there are no new issues to sync.
   276→    """
   277→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   278→    new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   279→    if not new_ids:
   280→        return None
   281→
   282→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   283→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   284→    existing = set(order)
   285→    added: list[str] = []
   286→    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
   287→        if fid not in existing:
   288→            order.append(fid)
   289→            added.append(fid)
   290→
   291→    # Inject triage stages if needed
   292→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   293→    triage_injected = bool(
   294→        triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "injected", False)
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   298→        new_ids=new_ids,
   299→        added_to_queue=added,
   300→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   301→    )
   302→
   303→
   304→__all__ = [
   305→    "ReconcileResult",
   306→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   307→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   308→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   309→]
   310→
```

```
    32→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    33→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    34→    return bool(
    35→        plan.get("queue_order")
    36→        or plan.get("overrides")
    37→        or plan.get("clusters")
    38→        or plan.get("skipped")
    39→    )

    42→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    43→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    44→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    45→        return False
    46→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    47→    if recon.resurfaced:

    49→            colorize(
    50→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    51→                "cyan",

    53→        )
    54→    return bool(recon.changes)

   107→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   108→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   109→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   110→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   111→        return False
   112→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   113→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   114→        return False
   115→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   116→    if scores.strict is None:
   117→        return False
   118→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   119→        "strict": scores.strict,
   120→        "overall": scores.overall,
   121→        "objective": scores.objective,
   122→        "verified": scores.verified,
   123→    }
   124→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   125→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   126→    return True
   127→
   128→
   129→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   130→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   131→) -> bool:
   132→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   133→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   134→        return False
   135→
   136→    try:
   137→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   138→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   139→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   140→            score_display_mode,
   141→        )
   142→
   143→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   144→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   145→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   146→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   147→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   148→        return False
   149→    if not queue_empty:
   150→        return False
   151→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   152→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   153→    return True

   163→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   164→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(

   170→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed

   289→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   290→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   291→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   292→    if cleared:
   293→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   294→    return cleared
   295→
   296→
   297→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   298→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   299→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   300→    try:
   301→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   302→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   303→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   304→        return
   305→    dirty = False
   306→
   307→    if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   308→        dirty = True
   309→
   310→    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   311→        dirty = True
   312→
   313→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   314→        runtime,
   315→        plan,
   316→    )
   317→    if _sync_stale_and_log(
   318→        plan,
   319→        runtime.state,
   320→        policy=policy,
   321→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   322→    ):
   323→        dirty = True
   324→
   325→    if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   326→        plan,
   327→        runtime.state,
   328→        target_strict=target_strict,
   329→        policy=policy,
   330→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   331→    ):
   332→        dirty = True
   333→
   334→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   335→        dirty = True
   336→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   337→        dirty = True
   338→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   339→        dirty = True
   340→    if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   341→        dirty = True
   342→
   343→    if dirty:
   344→        try:
   345→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   346→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   347→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   348→
```

```
    29→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"

    36→def _manual_member_ids(clusters: dict) -> set[str]:
    37→    """Collect all issue IDs belonging to manual (non-auto) clusters."""
    38→    ids: set[str] = set()
    39→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    40→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    41→            ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    42→    return ids
    43→
    44→
    45→def _group_clusterable_issues(
    46→    issues: dict,
    47→    *,
    48→    manual_member_ids: set[str],
    49→) -> tuple[dict[str, list[str]], dict[str, dict]]:
    50→    """Group open, non-suppressed, non-manual issues by detector/subtype key.
    51→
    52→    Returns (groups_by_key, issue_data) where groups_by_key maps grouping keys
    53→    to lists of issue IDs, filtered to clusters >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE.
    54→    """
    55→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    56→    issue_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
    57→    for fid, issue in issues.items():
    58→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    59→            continue
    60→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
    61→            continue
    62→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
    63→            continue
    64→
    65→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    66→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    67→        key = _grouping_key(issue, meta)
    68→        if key is None:
    69→            continue
    70→
    71→        groups[key].append(fid)
    72→        issue_data[fid] = issue
    73→
    74→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
    75→    return groups, issue_data
    76→
    77→
    78→def _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
    79→    plan: dict,
    80→    *,
    81→    clusters: dict,
    82→    existing_name: str,
    83→    member_ids: list[str],
    84→    now: str,
    85→) -> int:
    86→    """Sync member IDs for a user-modified cluster, returns count of changes."""
    87→    cluster = clusters[existing_name]
    88→    changes = 0
    89→    existing_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    90→    new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
    91→    if new_ids:
    92→        cluster["issue_ids"].extend(new_ids)
    93→        cluster["updated_at"] = now
    94→        changes = 1
    95→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    96→    for fid in member_ids:
    97→        if fid not in overrides:
    98→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
    99→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = existing_name
   100→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   101→    return changes
   102→

   104→def _subjective_state_sets(
   105→    state: StateModel,
   106→    *,

   109→) -> tuple[set, set, set]:
   110→    """Return (stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids) for subjective cluster logic."""
   111→    if policy is not None:
   112→        unscored_ids = policy.unscored_ids
   113→        stale_ids = policy.stale_ids
   114→        under_target_ids = policy.under_target_ids
   115→    else:
   116→        unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   117→        stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   118→        under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   119→    return stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids
   120→
   121→
   122→def _has_objective_backlog(
   123→    issues: dict,
   124→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   125→) -> bool:
   126→    """Check if objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   127→    if policy is not None:
   128→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   129→    return any(
   130→        f.get("status") == "open"
   131→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   132→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   133→        for f in issues.values()
   134→    )

   137→def _sync_auto_cluster(
   138→    plan: dict,
   139→    clusters: dict,
   140→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   141→    *,
   142→    cluster_key: str,

   148→    optional: bool = False,

   150→    """Create or update an auto-cluster and sync its override entries."""
   151→    changes = 0
   152→    existing_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key)
   153→    if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   154→        cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   155→        old_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   156→        new_ids_set = set(member_ids)
   157→        if (
   158→            old_ids != new_ids_set
   159→            or cluster.get("description") != description
   160→            or cluster.get("action") != action
   161→        ):
   162→            cluster["issue_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   163→            cluster["description"] = description
   164→            cluster["action"] = action
   165→            cluster["updated_at"] = now
   166→            changes = 1
   167→    else:
   168→        new_cluster = {
   169→            "name": cluster_name,
   170→            "description": description,
   171→            "issue_ids": list(member_ids),
   172→            "created_at": now,
   173→            "updated_at": now,
   174→            "auto": True,
   175→            "cluster_key": cluster_key,
   176→            "action": action,
   177→            "user_modified": False,
   178→        }
   179→        if optional:
   180→            new_cluster["optional"] = True
   181→        clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   182→        existing_by_key[cluster_key] = cluster_name
   183→        changes = 1
   184→
   185→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   186→    current_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key, cluster_name)
   187→    for fid in member_ids:
   188→        if fid not in overrides:
   189→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   190→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   191→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   192→
   193→    return changes
   194→
   195→
   196→def sync_issue_clusters(
   197→    plan: dict,
   198→    issues: dict,
   199→    clusters: dict,
   200→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   201→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   202→    now: str,
   203→) -> int:
   204→    """Group open issues by detector/subtype and sync auto-clusters."""
   205→    changes = 0
   206→
   207→    groups, issue_data = _group_clusterable_issues(
   208→        issues, manual_member_ids=_manual_member_ids(clusters)
   209→    )
   210→
   211→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   212→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   213→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   214→
   215→        rep = issue_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   216→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   217→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   218→        members = [issue_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in issue_data]
   219→
   220→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   221→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   222→
   223→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   224→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   225→
   226→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   227→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   228→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   229→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   230→                changes += _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
   231→                    plan,
   232→                    clusters=clusters,
   233→                    existing_name=existing_name,
   234→                    member_ids=member_ids,
   235→                    now=now,
   236→                )
   237→                continue
   238→
   239→        if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   240→            cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   241→
   242→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   243→            plan,
   244→            clusters,
   245→            existing_by_key,
   246→            cluster_key=key,
   247→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   248→            member_ids=member_ids,
   249→            description=description,
   250→            action=action,
   251→            now=now,
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    return changes
   255→
   256→
   257→def sync_subjective_clusters(
   258→    plan: dict,
   259→    state: StateModel,
   260→    issues: dict,
   261→    clusters: dict,
   262→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   263→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   264→    now: str,
   265→    *,
   266→    target_strict: float,
   267→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   268→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   269→) -> int:
   270→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   271→    changes = 0
   272→
   273→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   274→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   275→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    stale_state_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_state_ids = _subjective_state_sets(
   279→        state, policy=policy, target_strict=target_strict
   280→    )
   281→
   282→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   283→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   284→    )
   285→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   286→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids
   287→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   288→    )
   289→
   290→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   291→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   292→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   293→        description = (
   294→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   295→        )
   296→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   297→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   298→            plan,
   299→            clusters,
   300→            existing_by_key,
   301→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   302→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   303→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   304→            description=description,
   305→            action=action,
   306→            now=now,
   307→        )
   308→
   309→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   310→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   311→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   312→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   313→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   314→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   315→            plan,
   316→            clusters,
   317→            existing_by_key,
   318→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   319→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   320→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   321→            description=description,
   322→            action=action,
   323→            now=now,
   324→        )
   325→
   326→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(under_target_ids)
   327→
   328→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   329→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   330→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   331→    ut_prune = [
   332→        fid for fid in prev_ut_ids
   333→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   334→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   335→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   336→        and fid in order
   337→    ]
   338→    for fid in ut_prune:
   339→        order.remove(fid)
   340→        changes += 1
   341→
   342→    has_objective_items = _has_objective_backlog(issues, policy)
   343→
   344→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   345→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   346→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   347→        description = (
   348→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   349→            f"dimensions under target score"
   350→        )
   351→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   352→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   353→            plan,
   354→            clusters,
   355→            existing_by_key,
   356→            [REDACTED],
   357→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   358→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   359→            description=description,
   360→            action=action,
   361→            now=now,
   362→            optional=True,
   363→        )
   364→
   365→        existing_order = set(order)
   366→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   367→            if fid not in existing_order:
   368→                order.append(fid)
   369→
   370→    if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:
   371→        objective_evict = [
   372→            fid for fid in order
   373→            if fid in under_target_ids
   374→        ]
   375→        for fid in objective_evict:
   376→            order.remove(fid)
   377→            changes += 1
   378→
   379→    return changes
   380→
   381→
   382→def prune_stale_clusters(
   383→    plan: dict,
   384→    issues: dict,
   385→    clusters: dict,
   386→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   387→    now: str,
   388→) -> int:
   389→    """Delete auto-clusters that no longer have matching groups."""
   390→    changes = 0
   391→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   392→        cluster = clusters[name]
   393→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   394→            continue
   395→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   396→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   397→            continue
   398→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   399→            alive = [
   400→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   401→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   402→            ]
   403→            if alive:
   404→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])):
   405→                    cluster["issue_ids"] = alive
   406→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   407→                    changes += 1
   408→                continue
   409→        del clusters[name]
   410→        for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   411→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   412→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   413→                override["cluster"] = None
   414→                override["updated_at"] = now
   415→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   416→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   417→        changes += 1
   418→    return changes
   419→
   420→
   421→__all__ = ["prune_stale_clusters", "sync_issue_clusters", "sync_subjective_clusters"]
   422→
```

```
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    34→
    35→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    36→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    37→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    38→    target = cleaned.lower()
    39→
    40→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    41→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    42→            return str(dim_key)
    43→    return slugify(cleaned)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    47→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    48→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    49→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    50→    return {
    51→        cleaned.lower(),
    52→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    53→        slugify(cleaned),
    54→        canonical.lower(),
    55→        slugify(canonical),
    56→    }
    57→
    58→
    59→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60→# Subjective strict scores
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→
    63→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    64→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    65→    if not dim_scores:
    66→        return {}
    67→
    68→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    69→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    70→    for entry in entries:
    71→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    72→        if not name:
    73→            continue
    74→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    75→        [REDACTED](name)
    76→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    77→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    78→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    79→            if not key:
    80→                continue
    81→            aliases.add(key)
    82→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    83→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    84→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
    85→        for alias in aliases:
    86→            scores[alias] = strict_val
    87→    return scores
    88→
    89→
    90→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    91→# Synthetic item builders
    92→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    93→
    94→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    95→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
    96→
    97→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
    98→    """
    99→    from desloppify.engine._plan.triage_playbook import (
   100→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   101→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
   102→    )
   103→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   104→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   105→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   109→    order_set = set(order)
   110→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   111→    if not present:
   112→        return []
   113→
   114→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   115→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   116→
   117→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   118→    open_review_count = sum(
   119→        1 for f in issues.values()
   120→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   121→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   122→    )
   123→
   124→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   125→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
   126→
   127→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   128→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   129→        if sid not in present:
   130→            continue
   131→        if name in confirmed:
   132→            continue
   133→
   134→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   135→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   136→        blocked_by = sorted(
   137→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   138→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   142→        if name == "commit":
   143→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   144→
   145→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   146→            "id": sid,
   147→            "tier": 1,
   148→            "confidence": "high",
   149→            "detector": "triage",
   150→            "file": ".",
   151→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   152→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   153→            "detail": {
   154→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   155→                "stage": name,
   156→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   157→            },
   158→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   159→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   160→        }
   161→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   162→        items.append(item)
   163→    return items
   164→
   165→
   166→def build_subjective_items(
   167→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   168→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   169→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   170→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   171→    if not dim_scores:
   172→        return []
   173→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   174→
   175→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   176→    if not subjective_entries:
   177→        return []
   178→    unassessed_dims = {
   179→        str(name).strip()
   180→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   181→            dim_scores
   182→        )
   183→    }
   184→
   185→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   186→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   187→    for issue in issues.values():
   188→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   189→            continue
   190→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   191→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   192→            if dim_key:
   193→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   194→
   195→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   196→    def _prepare_command(
   197→        cli_keys: list[str],
   198→        *,
   199→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   200→    ) -> str:
   201→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   202→        if cli_keys:
   203→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   204→        if force_review_rerun:
   205→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   206→        return command
   207→
   208→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   209→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   210→        if not name:
   211→            continue
   212→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   213→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   214→            continue
   215→
   216→        [REDACTED](name)
   217→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   218→        cli_keys = [
   219→            str(key).strip().lower()
   220→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   221→            if str(key).strip()
   222→        ]
   223→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   224→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   225→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   226→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   227→            name in unassessed_dims
   228→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   229→        )
   230→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   231→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   232→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   233→        if open_review > 0:
   234→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   235→        else:
   236→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   237→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   238→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   239→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   240→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   241→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   242→            "file": ".",
   243→            "confidence": "medium",
   244→            "summary": summary,
   245→            "detail": {
   246→                "dimension_name": name,
   247→                "dimension": dim_key,
   248→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   249→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   250→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   251→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   252→            },
   253→            "status": "open",
   254→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   255→        }
   256→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   257→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   258→        items.append(item)
   259→    return items
   260→
   261→
   262→__all__ = [
   263→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   264→    "build_create_plan_item",
   265→    "build_import_scores_item",
   266→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   267→    "build_subjective_items",
   268→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   269→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   270→]
   271→
```

```
297→def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   298→    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules.
   299→
   300→    The queue enforces a phase order: scan → initial review → triage → objective work.
   301→
   302→    1. **Initial reviews pending** → only show initial-review subjective items.
   303→       The user must complete first-time reviews before working objective items.
   304→    2. **Triage in progress** → only show triage stages and workflow items.
   305→       Cluster work items aren't ready until triage completes (enrich adds
   306→       the detail that makes steps actionable).
   307→    3. **Objective work remains** → show objective items, hide endgame-only
   308→       subjective reassessments (stale re-reviews).
   309→    4. **Objective drained** → everything visible (endgame).
   310→    """
   311→    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
   312→        # Phase 1: only initial reviews visible — triage and workflow
   313→        # items stay hidden until initial reviews are complete.
   314→        return [
   315→            i for i in items
   316→            if i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and i.get("initial_review")
   317→        ]
   318→    if _has_triage_stages(items):
   319→        # Phase 2: triage in progress — only triage stages and workflow items visible.
   320→        # Cluster work items aren't ready until enrich completes.
   321→        return [
   322→            i for i in items
   323→            if i.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
   324→        ]
   325→    if not _has_objective_items(items):
   326→        return items  # endgame: everything visible
   327→    return [i for i in items if not _is_endgame_only(i)]
   328→
   329→
   330→def _empty_queue_fallback(plan: dict | None) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   331→    """Return a 'run scan' nudge when an active plan cycle has cleared."""
   332→    if not plan:
   333→        return []
   334→    plan_scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   335→    if plan_scores.get("strict") is None:
   336→        return []
   337→    return [{
   338→        "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   339→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   340→        "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   341→        "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   342→        "file": "",
   343→        "detector": "workflow",
   344→        "confidence": "high",
   345→    }]

   351→    "QueueContext",
   352→    "WorkQueueResult",
```

Good. The lifecycle filter at line 325-327 already hides endgame-only subjective items when objective items exist in the display layer, confirming the eviction logic in `sync_stale_dimensions` (lines 282-294) is redundant.

Here is the complete refactor plan.

---

## Refactor Plan for `stale_dimensions.py`

### Design Answers

**1. How should the file be split?**

Three new modules under `engine/_plan/`, matching the natural cohesion clusters:

| New module | Contents | Lines (approx) |
|---|---|---|
| `sync_dimensions.py` | `sync_stale_dimensions`, `sync_unscored_dimensions`, shared helpers (`_prune_subjective_ids`, `_inject_subjective_ids`, `_has_objective_backlog`), `SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX` | ~120 |
| `sync_triage.py` | `sync_triage_needed`, its helpers (`_new_review_ids_since_triage`, `_prune_all_triage_stages`, `_inject_pending_triage_stages`), `review_issue_snapshot_hash`, `is_triage_stale`, `compute_new_issue_ids`, `TRIAGE_*` constants | ~130 |
| `sync_workflow.py` | All four workflow sync functions (collapsed into one parameterized `sync_workflow_gate` + thin wrappers), `WORKFLOW_*` constants | ~100 |

`SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES` moves to `engine/_plan/constants.py` (which already exists and holds `AUTO_PREFIX`). It is assembled from the three prefix constants which are also re-exported from constants.py.

`stale_dimensions.py` becomes a re-export shim temporarily (for one commit), then gets deleted in a follow-up commit. Since there is no backward compatibility requirement per CLAUDE.md, the shim is purely a migration aid during the PR -- not a long-term artifact.

**2. Should the result dataclasses be unified?**

Yes. Replace all seven with two:

```python
@dataclass
class QueueSyncResult:
    """Result of a queue sync operation."""
    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def changes(self) -> int:
        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
```

This covers `StaleDimensionSyncResult` and `UnscoredDimensionSyncResult` directly. For the five sync functions that currently use `bool` fields (`injected: bool`, `pruned: bool`), the consumer code checks `sync.injected` (truthy), `sync.changes` (int). A `list[str]` is truthy when non-empty and `len([x]) = 1`, so the same consumer code works unchanged. The only adjustment: instead of `result.injected = True`, set `result.injected = [item_id]`.

`TriageSyncResult` also has `injected: bool` and `pruned: bool`. Same treatment: use `QueueSyncResult` with a list containing the stage IDs.

Place `QueueSyncResult` in `engine/_plan/constants.py` alongside the prefix constants, since all three sync modules need it.

**3. Should the four workflow sync functions be collapsed?**

Yes. Define a `WorkflowGateSpec` (a simple NamedTuple or plain tuple) that captures:
- `item_id: str` -- the workflow ID to inject
- `precondition: Callable[[PlanModel, StateModel, SubjectiveVisibility | None, dict], bool]` -- returns True when the gate should fire
- `also_blocks: tuple[str, ...]` -- other IDs whose presence means "already handled" (e.g., `WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID` blocks `WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID`)

Then one function:

```python
def sync_workflow_gate(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    gate: WorkflowGateSpec,
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
    **kwargs,
) -> QueueSyncResult:
```

Keep four thin public wrappers (`sync_score_checkpoint_needed`, etc.) so callers don't need to know about gate specs. Each wrapper is 2-3 lines calling `sync_workflow_gate` with its spec. This preserves the existing call signatures while eliminating the structural duplication.

The varying preconditions are just lambdas or small functions:
- `score_checkpoint`: `not policy.unscored_ids` (or `not current_unscored_ids(state)`)
- `create_plan`: `not unscored_ids and has_objective_backlog and no triage pending`
- `import_scores`: `assessment_mode == "issues_only"` (passed via kwargs)
- `communicate_score`: `scores_just_imported or not unscored_ids` (with also_blocks for score_checkpoint)

**4. Should the ID helpers be inlined?**

Yes, inline them. The three functions (`_current_stale_ids`, `current_unscored_ids`, `current_under_target_ids`) just forward to `stale_policy_mod.*` with `subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX`. Call `stale_policy_mod.*` directly at the two call sites (`sync_dimensions.py` and `auto_cluster_sync.py`), passing `SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX`. The `review_issue_snapshot_hash` one-liner wrapper also gets inlined -- callers call `stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash` directly.

The one complication: `current_unscored_ids` is re-exported via `engine/plan.py` and used by `auto_cluster_sync.py`. Since it is just `stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)`, the facade can re-export directly from `stale_policy` (or the callers can import from `stale_policy`).

**5. How to handle `SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES`?**

Move to `engine/_plan/constants.py`:

```python
SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = (TRIAGE_PREFIX, WORKFLOW_PREFIX, SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
```

Each sync module imports its own prefix from `constants.py`. `reconcile.py` imports `SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES` from `constants.py`. Clean and no circular dependency risk since `constants.py` has no other imports.

### Step-by-Step Implementation

**Step 1: Expand `constants.py` with all prefix constants and `QueueSyncResult`**

Move to `engine/_plan/constants.py`:
- `SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX`, `TRIAGE_PREFIX`, `WORKFLOW_PREFIX`, `SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES`
- `TRIAGE_ID` (deprecated migration constant)
- `TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS`, `TRIAGE_IDS`
- `WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID`, `WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID`, `WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID`, `WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID`
- `QueueSyncResult` dataclass

**Step 2: Create `sync_dimensions.py`**

Extract from `stale_dimensions.py`:
- `sync_stale_dimensions`, `sync_unscored_dimensions`
- `_prune_subjective_ids`, `_inject_subjective_ids`, `_has_objective_backlog`
- Remove the eviction block (lines 282-294) from `sync_stale_dimensions`
- Inline the ID helper calls: replace `_current_stale_ids(state)` with `stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)`
- Return `QueueSyncResult` instead of type-specific dataclasses

**Step 3: Create `sync_triage.py`**

Extract:
- `sync_triage_needed` and its three helpers
- `is_triage_stale`, `compute_new_issue_ids`
- Import triage constants from `constants.py`
- Return `QueueSyncResult` instead of `TriageSyncResult`
- For `sync_triage_needed`, use `result.injected = list(injected_stage_ids)` and `result.pruned = list(pruned_stage_ids)` instead of bools

**Step 4: Create `sync_workflow.py`**

Extract:
- All four `sync_*_needed` functions
- Implement `sync_workflow_gate` as the shared core
- Keep thin public wrappers with current signatures
- Return `QueueSyncResult` instead of per-function dataclasses

**Step 5: Update `auto_cluster_sync.py`**

Change imports from `stale_dimensions` to:
- `SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX` from `constants`
- Call `stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)` directly instead of `_current_stale_ids(state)`
- Same for `current_unscored_ids`, `current_under_target_ids`

**Step 6: Update `engine/plan.py` facade**

Change all `stale_dimensions` imports to point at new modules:
- Dimension sync functions from `sync_dimensions`
- Triage sync functions from `sync_triage`
- Workflow sync functions from `sync_workflow`
- Constants from `constants`
- Remove the seven result type re-exports, add `QueueSyncResult`

**Step 7: Update remaining direct importers**

These files import directly from `stale_dimensions` and need updating:
- `reconcile.py` -- `SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES` from `constants`, `compute_new_issue_ids` + `sync_triage_needed` from `sync_triage`
- `epic_triage_apply.py` -- `review_issue_snapshot_hash` from `stale_policy` directly
- `operations_queue.py` -- `TRIAGE_IDS` from `constants`
- `schema_migrations.py` -- `TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS`, `WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID` from `constants`
- `synthetic.py` -- `TRIAGE_IDS`, `TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS` from `constants`
- `synthetic_workflow.py` -- `WORKFLOW_*_ID` from `constants`

**Step 8: Update test imports (15+ files)**

Bulk update. Most tests only import constants (`TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS`, `TRIAGE_IDS`) or sync functions. Change `from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import X` to the appropriate new module. Tests that construct result types (`StaleDimensionSyncResult`, `UnscoredDimensionSyncResult`) switch to `QueueSyncResult`.

**Step 9: Delete `stale_dimensions.py`**

After all imports are updated, delete the file.

**Step 10: Run tests**

`python -m pytest desloppify/tests/ -q` to verify everything passes.

### Risk Assessment

- **Low risk**: All changes are mechanical import rewiring plus deleting dead code (eviction block, wrapper functions). No behavioral change except removing the redundant eviction.
- **Eviction removal safety**: Confirmed by `_apply_lifecycle_filter` in `core.py` (line 325-327) which already hides endgame-only subjective items when objective work exists. The queue_order eviction was writing to the plan file unnecessarily, causing churn.
- **Result type unification**: Consumer code uses `.changes`, `.injected` (truthy check), `.pruned` (truthy check or `len()`). A `list[str]` satisfies all these patterns. The only callsite that checks `result.injected = True` (a bool) is internal to the sync functions themselves -- those change to `result.injected = [the_id]`.
